### [1] [Fault tolerance estimation in digital circuits with visualised generative networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15212)
*Sascha Biel,Carl Alexander Gaede,Amiel Glaser,Jan Wolter,Alexej Schelle*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于生成对抗网络（GAN）的数值方法，用于评估数字电路结构中故障模式的容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估数字电路设计中不同故障模式（如逻辑器件缺失或互换）对电路鲁棒性的影响，需要一种能够有效分析数字电子信号偏差的方法。

Method: 利用生成网络采样技术生成数字电路中理想数字化模拟电流的按位配置作为随机输入，通过GAN的判别器部分将期望输出电流与数值实验中的真实信号进行比较，计算其与理想数字电子信号的偏差。通过复变量表示GAN来分析不同经典逻辑元件相关故障模式的影响。

Result: 成功实现了对数字电路设计中故障模式容错性的评估，能够区分不同经典逻辑元件相关故障模式对电路鲁棒性的影响。

Conclusion: 该方法为电子设计中的鲁棒性评估提供了一种有效的数值分析手段，可以帮助识别和量化不同故障模式对电路性能的影响。

Abstract: We propose a new numerical method to estimate the fault tolerance of failure modes in digital circuit structures with a generative network sampling technique. From a random input of generated bitwise configurations of ideally digitalised analog currents in the digital circuit design with classical logical gates, expected output currents are compared to the realistic signals of a numerical experiment at the discriminator part of the Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) to calculate the deviation from ideal digital electronic signals, including various error modes, such as missing or interchanged logical devices. From the present analysis of a representation of the GAN in terms of complex variables, it is possible to evaluate the robustness in electronic designs by differentiating the impact of failure modes associated with different classical logical elements in the circuit.

</details>


### [2] [Hardware-Software Co-Design of Scalable, Energy-Efficient Analog Recurrent Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15216)
*Arthur Fyon,Julien Brandoit,Loris Mendolia,Damien Ernst,Jean-Michel Redouté,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过软硬件协同设计，首次实现了适用于循环神经网络的超低功耗模拟电路，解决了模拟循环网络中噪声累积的关键难题，在180nm CMOS工艺下实现了亚微瓦级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常开AI应用（如环境传感器、生物医学植入设备）需要超低功耗。模拟电路可实现亚微瓦级推理，但现有实现仅限于前馈架构，扩展到循环架构被认为不切实际，因为时间反馈会导致噪声累积。

Method: 提出双稳态记忆循环单元（BMRU）的模拟电路实现：1）利用离散值输出和迟滞动力学抑制噪声；2）离散输出在每个单元边界将模拟噪声抑制至少20倍，打破噪声累积；3）重新设计BMRU以实现第一象限运行和固定阈值，在保持表达能力和可训练性的同时，建立学习参数与电路元件的一一对应关系；4）采用电流模式模拟电路设计。

Result: 在180nm CMOS工艺下的晶体管级仿真显示：1）软件预测与电路级行为几乎完美一致，软件模型可作为物理硬件的高保真仿真器；2）添加循环的功耗与状态维度呈线性关系，而占主导的前馈层功耗呈二次方关系，循环以相对于前馈主干线性的边际成本添加；3）端到端关键词检测在RNN核心实现亚微瓦级推理。

Conclusion: 通过BMRU的软硬件协同设计，成功克服了模拟循环神经网络中噪声累积的根本障碍，为常开AI应用提供了一条可行的超低功耗模拟RNN实现路径，证明了循环架构可以以线性边际成本集成到模拟电路中。

Abstract: Always-on AI applications, from environmental sensors to biomedical implants, require ultra-low power consumption. Analog circuits offer a path to sub-microwatt inference, yet existing analog implementations are limited to feedforward architectures: extending them to recurrent dynamics has been considered impractical due to noise accumulation through temporal feedback. We demonstrate that this barrier can be overcome through hardware-software co-design. Specifically, we identify that Bistable Memory Recurrent Units (BMRUs), a class of Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) with discrete-valued outputs and hysteretic dynamics, admit an ultra-low power current-mode analog implementation which we design from first principles. The resulting circuit establishes a one-to-one correspondence between each learned parameter and a circuit element. The discrete outputs suppress analog noise by at least 20-fold at each cell boundary, breaking the noise accumulation that prevents analog recurrence. We reformulate BMRUs for first-quadrant operation with fixed thresholds, enabling the direct correspondence while preserving expressivity and trainability. Transistor-level simulations in 180 nm Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS) show near-perfect agreement between software predictions and circuit-level behavior, with the software model thereby serving as a high-fidelity simulator of the physical hardware at low computational cost. We leverage this fidelity to conduct large-scale noise immunity and power scaling analyses: the power cost of adding recurrence scales linearly with state dimension, while the feedforward layers dominating total power scale quadratically, meaning recurrence is added at linear marginal cost relative to the feedforward backbone. End-to-end keyword spotting achieves sub-microwatt inference at the RNN core.

</details>


### [3] [GenAI-Driven Approach to RISC-V Supply Chain Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15223)
*Nenad Petrovic,Andre Schamschurko,Yingjie Xu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）、视觉语言模型（VLM）与模型驱动工程（MDE）的工作流，用于RISC-V供应链的多模态数据分析、知识图谱构建及韧性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体供应链数据具有异构性强、非结构化程度高的特点，传统分析方法难以从文本、图表及扫描文档等多模态数据中有效提取信息并进行深入的风险与瓶颈分析，亟需一种能够融合多源数据并支持形式化推理的自动化分析方案。

Method: 该方法构建了一个LLM赋能的工作流：首先利用LLM处理文本数据，使用VLM从图表、表格和扫描文档等视觉制品中提取信息；模型协作识别关键实体与关系，构建表示供应链组件及其依赖关系的知识图谱；随后引入模型驱动工程（MDE）和基于约束的建模技术进行形式化验证、瓶颈检测与风险评估，并设计了人在回路机制以支持交互式查询与专家验证。

Result: 在RISC-V生态系统场景中的评估表明，该方法能够有效生成可操作的洞察，提高供应链透明度，并支持复杂的半导体供应链决策。

Conclusion: LLM/VLM的语义理解能力与MDE的形式化分析能力的结合，为复杂供应链的探索性与系统性韧性评估提供了有效支持，证明了多模态数据驱动方法在供应链分析领域的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an LLM-empowered workflow for RISC-V supply chain analysis, integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Model-Driven Engineering (MDE) to enable comprehensive, multimodal data-driven insights. The proposed approach addresses the challenges of heterogeneous and unstructured supply chain data by leveraging LLMs for textual understanding and VLMs for extracting information from visual artifacts such as diagrams, tables, and scanned documents. These models collaboratively identify key entities and relationships, which are then organized into a knowledge graph representing supply chain components and their interdependencies. For analytical reasoning, the workflow incorporates MDE techniques and constraint-based modeling to enable formal validation of dependencies, detection of bottlenecks, and assessment of risks. The synergy between LLM- and VLM-based semantic understanding and MDE-based formal analysis supports both exploratory and systematic evaluation of supply chain resilience. A human-in-the-loop mechanism further enables interactive querying and expert validation. The approach is evaluated in RISC-V ecosystem scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness in generating actionable insights, enhancing transparency, and supporting decision-making in complex semiconductor supply chains.

</details>


### [4] [Is Agentic AI Ready for Real-World Hardware Engineering? A Deep Dive with Phoenix-bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15226)
*Qingyun Zou,Feng Yu,Hongshi Tan,Bingsheng He,WengFai Wong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Phoenix-bench硬件工程基准测试，发现软件智能体在硬件任务上性能下降37-58%，测试用例反馈比文件定位更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件LLM基准测试仅关注孤立子任务，缺乏对代码库导航、层次感知定位、EDA可执行验证和维护式补丁等能力的联合评估，无法验证软件工程智能体是否能迁移到真实的硬件工程场景。

Method: 构建Phoenix-bench基准测试集，包含511个经过验证的Verilator实例，来自114个GitHub仓库，配备开发者补丁、设计流程标签、测试平台和Docker环境。统一评估4个商业智能体和8个开源智能体结构，覆盖4个LLM骨干网络，并设计文件级定位和测试日志反馈两种诊断干预。

Result: 软件与硬件工程存在本质差异：相同智能体从SWE-bench到Phoenix-bench性能下降37-58%，硬件错误通过信号流在并行实例化模块间传播而非调用图；失败集中在控制流/FSM错误、验证测试平台错误以及需要跨层次信号流追踪的困难案例；文件级完美定位仅提升1.4%，而单轮测试用例反馈提升42-45%。

Conclusion: 软件工程智能体难以直接迁移到硬件工程任务，硬件错误定位需要追踪实例化链路；定位粒度比定位本身更重要，测试用例反馈能够显著提升解决率，为硬件智能体设计提供重要指导。

Abstract: We ask whether agentic AI systems built for software engineering transfer to realistic hardware engineering. Existing hardware LLM benchmarks isolate sub-tasks but none jointly requires repository navigation, hierarchy-aware localization, Electronic Design Automation (EDA) executable verification, and maintenance-style patching. We introduce \textbf{Phoenix-bench}, a synchronized corpus of 511 verified Verilator instances from 114 GitHub repositories, each shipped with the developer patch, design-flow labels, fail-to-pass and pass-to-pass testbenches, and a Docker-pinned EDA environment so resolved-rate differences reflect agent behavior rather than toolchain availability. Using Phoenix-bench we run a uniform evaluation of four commercial agents and eight open-source agentic structures across four LLM backbones, plus two diagnostic interventions (file-level oracle localization and one round of testbench-log feedback). Three findings emerge. (i)~Software and hardware are fundamentally different engineering tasks: the same agent loses 37\% to 58\% from SWE-bench Verified to Phoenix-bench because hardware bugs propagate across parallel instantiated modules through signal flow rather than along a software-style call graph, and software-tuned agents stop at the symptom file instead of tracing back through the instantiation chain. (ii)~Failures concentrate on design control-flow / finite state machine (FSM) bugs, verification testbench bugs, and hard cases that demand cross-hierarchy signal-flow tracking and coordinated multi-file edits. (iii)~Localization granularity matters far more than localization itself: a perfect file-level oracle yields only $+1.4$\% because the agent then breaks files that did not need editing, while a single round of test case feedback lifts resolved rate by $42$\% to $45$\% because the test case tells \emph{where} the bug is and \emph{what} the fix has to look like.

</details>


### [5] [A3D: Agentic AI flow for autonomous Accelerator Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15237)
*Abinand Nallathambi,Christopher Knight,Shantanu Ganguly,Wilfried Haensch,Anand Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了A3D，一个基于智能体AI（Agentic AI）的端到端硬件加速器设计自动化流程。该系统无需人工干预，即可从复杂的科学应用代码自动生成加速器设计，成功应用于LAMMPS和QMCPACK等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管高层综合（HLS）等技术有所发展，但为复杂应用设计硬件加速器仍然是一项高度劳动密集型工作，需要应用领域专家具备硬件设计、微架构和EDA工具使用等多方面的专业知识。目前大多数加速器解决方案仅限于具有规则可预测数据流的应用。AI技术的进步为通过智能体AI实现自动化提供了前所未有的潜力。

Method: A3D采用智能体AI流程，通过以下方式实现端到端自动化：（1）合理划分任务给专业智能体；（2）协调专业智能体和验证智能体的流程循环；（3）利用现有和自定义工具；（4）采用智能体RAG技术进行代码库和EDA工具文档探索。系统自动完成工作负载分析、性能瓶颈识别、HLS兼容性代码重构以及微架构生成，并自动探索速度-面积权衡空间以生成多样化的加速器设计。

Result: 使用Claude Sonnet 4.5和Catapult HLS工具实现的A3D系统，成功从LAMMPS（分子动力学模拟）和QMCPACK（量子化学）等复杂科学应用中自动生成了加速器设计，整个过程中无需人工干预。系统能够自动探索设计空间并生成多样化的加速器方案。

Conclusion: A3D展示了智能体AI在硬件加速器设计自动化方面的巨大潜力，成功解决了将现代大语言模型应用于加速器设计时的关键挑战。该方法显著降低了加速器设计的技术门槛，使应用领域专家能够专注于应用本身而非硬件实现细节，为复杂应用的硬件加速提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Accelerating applications through the design of hardware accelerators can significantly enhance system performance and energy efficiency. Despite advances, such as high-level synthesis (HLS), designing accelerators for complex applications still remains highly labor-intensive, demanding considerable expertise in understanding workloads to be accelerated, hardware design, micro-architecture, and EDA tool usage, posing challenges for application domain experts. Therefore, most accelerator solutions are limited to applications with a regular predictable dataflow. Advances in AI have enabled agents that perform autonomous planning, reasoning, execution and reflection, leading to unprecedented potential for automation through agentic AI. We present A3D, an Agentic AI flow for end-to-end Automation of hardware Accelerator Design. A3D automates workload analysis, performance bottleneck identification, code refactoring for HLS compatibility and micro-architecture generation. A3D also generates diverse accelerator designs by automatically exploring the speed-area tradeoff space. Recent efforts have explored the use of AI for specific tasks such as design space exploration in HLS, leaving several tasks to still be performed manually. A3D addresses the challenges in applying modern LLMs to accelerator design by judiciously partitioning tasks among specialist agents, orchestrating process loops with specialist and verifier agents, utilizing pre-existing and custom tools, and employing agentic RAG for codebase and proprietary EDA tool documentation exploration. Our implementation of A3D, using commercial components like Claude Sonnet 4.5 and the Catapult HLS tool, demonstrates its effectiveness by generating accelerator designs with no human intervention from complex scientific applications like LAMMPS (molecular dynamics simulation) and QMCPACK (quantum chemistry).

</details>


### [6] [ITHICA: Intra-Thread Instruction Checking Approach for Defect-Induced Silent Data Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15638)
*Ioanna Vavelidou,Subho S. Banerjee,Eric X. Liu,Mike Fuller,Subhasish Mitra,Caroline Trippel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ITHICA，一种通过指令复制和输出比较自动生成功能测试的方法，用于检测CPU制造缺陷导致的静默数据损坏，在3000多台服务器上检测出的缺陷服务器数量比原生检查多39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模数据中心报告了由硅制造缺陷引起的静默数据损坏（SDCs）问题，这促使了针对缺陷CPU的功能测试开发需求。现有的测试方法存在局限性，需要更有效的检测手段。

Method: ITHICA通过在任意程序中插入线程内、指令级的错误检查来自动生成功能测试。其核心洞察是：最严重的缺陷会导致不一致错误，即在相同输入下，同一指令在同一线程内的两次执行可能因执行上下文不同而产生不同的架构输出。该方法主要利用指令复制和输出比较技术，使任意程序都能作为测试用例，并在检测到错误时识别受影响的指令。

Result: 在超过3000台CPU服务器上的评估表明：ITHICA错误检查比原生检查多检测出39%的缺陷服务器；能够将从工业超大规模测试程序、数据中心工作负载和常用库转换生成的程序变为有效的功能测试；发现了新的缺陷行为特征，挑战了先前超大规模数据中心研究的结论。

Conclusion: ITHICA为检测CPU制造缺陷提供了一种有效的自动化功能测试生成方法，显著提高了缺陷检测能力，并通过大规模实验揭示了新的缺陷行为模式，为未来硬件可靠性研究提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Hyperscaler reports of silent data corruptions (SDCs), presumed to be caused by silicon manufacturing defects, have motivated the development of functional tests for detecting defective CPUs. We present ITHICA, an approach for automatically generating functional tests for defect-induced errors from arbitrary programs by inserting intra-thread, instruction-level error checks, primarily leveraging instruction duplication and output comparison. Our key insight is that the most pernicious defects cause inconsistent errors: two executions of the same instruction within the same thread, given the same inputs, can produce different architectural outputs depending on the execution context in which they run. By exploiting this insight, ITHICA enables arbitrary programs to serve as tests and identifies affected instructions upon error detections. We use ITHICA to transform industrial hyperscaler test programs (our baseline), datacenter workloads, and common libraries into functional tests, and evaluate them on over 3,000 CPU servers. ITHICA error checks detect 39% more defective servers than native checks within the ITHICA tests derived from our baseline programs, and enable novel findings on defect behavior that challenge conclusions drawn by prior hyperscaler fleet studies.

</details>


### [7] [ICP: Exploiting Instruction Correlation for Prefetching Irregular Memory Accesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15645)
*Mengming Li,Chenlu Miao,Buqing Xu,Qijun Zhang,Xiangfeng Sun,Ceyu Xu,Yuan Xie,Wenkai Li,Shang Liu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出指令相关性预取（ICP），利用指令间稳定的依赖关系处理不规则内存访问，以2.1KB的极小存储开销显著超越现有预取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则内存访问难以高效预取。现有的时序预取器依赖于地址重复出现，并需要巨大的元数据存储空间；当面对间接访问等地址弱重复场景时，其收益有限且开销巨大。

Method: 提出指令相关性预取（ICP），一种新型硬件预取机制。其核心思想是：虽然内存地址可能不重复，但生成这些地址的指令通常会以稳定的数据依赖关系重复出现。ICP通过学习这些持久的指令间相关性，利用相关前驱指令的执行结果，推测计算并预取未来的不规则访问。

Result: 在不规则SPEC CPU和GAP基准测试中，ICP的性能比最先进的时序预取器Triangel高出14.0%，比间接预取器DMP高出6.0%。同时，ICP仅需2.1 KB的硬件存储，比时序预取器小三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: ICP证明了利用指令级相关性替代地址相关性来处理不规则内存访问的可行性与优越性，它打破了时序预取器对地址重复的依赖，在大幅降低存储开销的同时显著提升了预取性能。

Abstract: Irregular memory accesses pose challenges for effective and efficient data prefetching. While temporal prefetchers have recently shown promise for irregular memory access patterns, their effectiveness fundamentally depends on temporal address recurrence and large metadata storage. When memory addresses exhibit weak or no recurrence, as in indirect memory accesses, temporal prefetchers achieve limited performance gains while incurring substantial storage overhead.
  This paper proposes Instruction-Correlation Prefetching (ICP), a new hardware prefetching mechanism that exploits instruction-level correlations rather than memory-address correlations to handle irregular memory accesses. ICP observes that although memory addresses may not repeat, the instructions generating them often recur with stable data-dependency relationships. By learning these persistent instruction correlations, ICP speculatively computes and prefetches future irregular accesses using the execution results of their correlated predecessors. Across irregular SPEC CPU and GAP benchmarks, ICP outperforms the state-of-the-art temporal prefetcher Triangel by 14.0% and the indirect prefetcher DMP by 6.0%, while requiring only 2.1 KB of hardware storage, over three orders of magnitude smaller than temporal prefetchers.

</details>


### [8] [SRAM Based Digital Custom Compute Engine for Improved Area Efficiency of AI Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16161)
*Narendra Singh Dhakad,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于10T SRAM单元的XNOR存内计算架构，通过在单元间集成全加器，将布线复杂度降低50%，面积效率相比现有技术提升2.67倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统存内计算系统面临严重的布线挑战和资源开销，特别是在实现乘累加（MAC）操作时，复杂的布线网络限制了硬件效率。针对二值神经网络（BNN）的计算需求，需要优化存内计算架构以降低延迟和面积开销。

Method: 设计基于10T SRAM单元的XNOR存内计算架构，在存内乘法单元之间集成全加器，实现布线复杂度50%的降低；采用14T全加器构建N位行波进位加法器组成加法树，替代传统28T CMOS设计以减少面积；优化XNOR计算以支持二值神经网络的MAC操作。

Result: 布线复杂度降低50%，面积相比传统28T CMOS设计显著减小，MAC操作延迟降低，整体硬件面积效率相比现有先进技术提升2.67倍。

Conclusion: 该架构有效解决了传统存内计算系统的布线瓶颈问题，通过创新的10T SRAM单元设计和14T全加器集成，在保证计算性能的同时大幅提升了硬件效率，为二值神经网络的高效硬件实现提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel architecture utilizing a 10T SRAM cell for XNOR-based in-memory computing, aimed at mitigating the extensive routing challenges typically encountered in conventional in-memory computing systems. By integrating a full adder between in-memory multiplication cells, the proposed design achieves a 50% reduction in routing complexity. The architecture performs multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations using XNOR computation optimized for binary neural networks (BNNs). Additionally, a 14T-based full adder is employed to construct an N-bit ripple carry adder in the adder tree, significantly reducing the area compared to traditional 28T-based CMOS designs. The 10T SRAM XNOR computation further enhances the latency for MAC operations. The proposed approach reduces the latency and area overhead, improving the overall hardware's area efficiency by 2.67x compared to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [9] [ADS-IMC: Accelerating Data Sorting with In-Memory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16213)
*Narendra Singh Dhakad,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 首次提出基于6T SRAM的存内排序架构，消除片外数据传输，相比忆阻器实现延迟降低3.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排序需在内存和处理单元间频繁搬运数据，产生显著的延迟和能耗开销，亟需消除数据搬运的存内计算方案。

Method: 提出首个基于6T SRAM的存内排序架构，采用数字系统通用的标准加权二进制基数格式表示数据，直接在存储阵列内执行排序操作。

Result: 所提架构无需片外数据传输，相比基于忆阻器的存内排序方案实现了3.4倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 基于6T SRAM的存内排序架构可有效解决传统排序的数据搬运瓶颈，为高能效排序加速提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Sorting is a fundamental operation across numerous computational domains. Traditionally, this process involves transferring data from main memory to a processing unit for sorting, followed by writing the sorted data back to memory. This conventional approach incurs substantial latency and energy overheads due to the extensive data movement between memory and processing components. To mitigate these overheads, this paper introduces novel architectures for executing sorting operations directly within the memory fabric, eliminating the need for off-chip data transfer. To our knowledge, this work represents the first exploration of in-memory sorting using 6T SRAM. The proposed architecture is designed to operate on data represented in the standard weighted binary radix format commonly used in digital systems. The proposed architecture achieves a significant 3.4x reduction in latency compared to memristor-based IMC sorting.

</details>


### [10] [TTP: A Hardware-Efficient Design for Precise Prefetching in Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16253)
*Yavuz Selim Tozlu,Anshul Naithani,Huiyang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TTP的硬件预取器，利用光线追踪中已有的树遍历堆栈信息进行高精度预取，平均加速1.48倍，L1预取准确率达98.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光线追踪（RT）技术虽能提供高度逼真的视觉效果，但其实时执行面临巨大挑战。核心瓶颈在于包围体层次结构（BVH）遍历任务，这是一个内存受限问题：GPU线程大部分时间用于从内存读取树节点数据，导致严重的内存延迟瓶颈。现有硬件难以满足实时处理百万级光线通过大规模三角形场景的需求。

Method: 提出了一种新型硬件预取器——树遍历预取器（TTP）。其核心思想是利用RT单元中现有的树遍历堆栈实现高精度预取。具体而言，TTP利用每个线程硬件遍历堆栈上已有的地址信息预取节点。对于基于深度优先搜索（DFS）的遍历，当节点连续从遍历堆栈弹出时（对应树中的向上遍历），生成预取请求。该设计无需额外复杂的地址计算逻辑，直接复用现有堆栈状态信息。

Result: 在周期级模拟器Vulkan-sim 2.0上的评估显示：相比基线实现平均加速1.48倍（最高达1.89倍），硬件开销几乎可忽略。TTP实现了98.92%的平均L1预取准确率（预取块被实际需求加载引用的比率），覆盖率为31.54%（L1缺失减少量占基线L1缺失的比例），与实际加速比吻合良好。

Conclusion: 通过利用光线追踪中固有的树遍历堆栈结构进行智能预取，TTP有效缓解了BVH遍历中的内存延迟瓶颈。该方法以极低的硬件开销实现了极高的预取准确率和显著的性能提升，证明了利用算法固有数据结构信息进行内存优化的有效性，为光线追踪硬件加速提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Ray tracing (RT) is a 3D graphics technique that offers highly realistic visuals. It is becoming prominent and accessible as GPU vendors have integrated dedicated ray tracing acceleration hardware. However, tracing millions of rays through 3D scenes consisting of high numbers of triangles in real time is challenging and requires expensive hardware. The main bottleneck in RT workloads is the expensive Bounding Volume Hierarchy (BVH) traversal task, which is a large tree structure that encodes the 3D scene. BVH traversal is a memory-bound problem, as the GPU threads spend most of their time reading tree node data from memory. In this work, we attack the memory latency bottleneck of ray tracing through prefetching. We propose a novel hardware prefetcher, named Tree Traversal Prefetcher (TTP), for ray tracing. The main idea is to leverage the existing tree traversal stack in the RT units for highly accurate prefetching. In particular, TTP prefetches nodes using the addresses already available on the hardware traversal stacks of each thread. For DFS (Depth-first search) based traversal, prefetches are generated when nodes are being popped consecutively from the traversal stack, potentially corresponding to upward traversal through the tree. We evaluate TTP on a cycle-level simulator, Vulkan-sim 2.0, and show that it achieves 1.48x speedup on average (up to 1.89x) compared to the baseline, with nearly negligible hardware overhead. TTP achieves 98.92% average L1 accuracy, which is the ratio of the prefetched blocks being actually referenced by demand loads. The coverage, computed as the ratio of L1 miss reduction over baseline L1 misses, is 31.54%, correlating well with the achieved speedup.

</details>

### [11] [Designing Dense Satellite Clusters for Distributed Space-based Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15335)
*Jules Pénot,Hamsa Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出平面和3D两种LEO数据中心卫星集群轨道设计，在满足安全约束的同时实现了卫星数量的立方级扩展，并证明了可构建类似地面数据中心的VL2 Clos网络架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对太阳同步低地球轨道数据中心应用，需要设计能够在整个轨道周期内满足多重安全和运营约束的密集卫星集群，包括最小卫星间距、无遮挡太阳能供电以及稳定的星间链路连接。

Method: 提出了两种参数化的集群轨道设计：平面集群和3D集群架构，通过构造和数值分析验证其满足约束条件；针对给定的卫星集群，构建并求解整数优化问题，将VL2类Clos网络数据中心交换结构映射到卫星及其可行的星间链路上。

Result: 证明了两种集群轨道设计均满足星间间距、无遮挡太阳矢量和星间视线约束；平面架构实现了给定约束下最有效的卫星平面封装；3D架构使数据中心卫星数量可按(R_max/R_min)^3的比例扩展，优于以往所有LEO数据中心集群设计；验证了两种架构下集群内都存在足够多的永久无遮挡星间链路。

Conclusion: 所提出的轨道设计方案能够在满足安全运营约束的前提下实现高效的大规模卫星集群组网，并通过适当的交换机卫星配置，成功将地面数据中心的网络架构复制到空间环境中，为LEO数据中心的建设提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Recent proposals for datacenters in sun-synchronous Low Earth Orbit rely on a large number of compute satellites formation-flying in dense clusters. Designing such satellite clusters requires optimizing the satellites' orbital geometry under several safety and operational constraints applied throughout the cluster's entire orbit. These constraints include guaranteeing a minimum inter-satellite spacing, obstruction-less solar power for every satellite, and that each satellite have a stable set of nearest neighbors with which it can maintain inter-satellite links (ISLs). In this work, we propose two main cluster orbital designs, parametrized by the minimum inter-satellite spacing $R_{min}$ and the cluster radius $R_{max}$: a planar cluster, and a 3D cluster. We show by construction and numerical analysis that both cluster orbital designs are consistent with the inter-satellite spacing, unobstructed sun-vector, and inter-satellite line of sight constraints. The proposed planar architecture is the most efficient packing of satellites in a plane for given $R_{min}$ and $R_{max}$ values, and our 3D architecture allows for the number of datacenter satellites to scale proportional to $(R_{max}/R_{min})^3$, an improvement over all previous LEO datacenter cluster designs. Finally, for a given satellite cluster, we formulate and solve an integer optimization problem that maps a VL2-like Clos network datacenter switching fabric onto the satellites and their corresponding set of feasible ISLs. We confirm that for both the planar and 3D architectures, there are sufficiently many permanently unobstructed ISLs within the cluster to replicate the switching fabric of terrestrial datacenters. We also examine the tradeoff between the number of ISLs each satellite can simultaneously sustain, and the corresponding number of cluster satellites that must be dedicated as aggregation and intermediate switches.

</details>


### [12] [Using the Open Science Data Federation for data distribution: Big Bear Solar Observatory use case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15378)
*Sydney Montiel,Alexsandra Guadarrama,Fabio Andrijauskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Open Science Data Federation (OSDF)，这是一个基于StashCache构建的全球数据分发网络，旨在解决科学领域海量数据处理需求，并成功应用于大熊湖太阳天文台的数据访问与处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学领域对大规模数据处理需求的不断增长，如何高效地将数据分发至处理站点并实现无缝共享成为关键挑战。为了满足这一需求以及美国国家科学基金会（NSF）近期对数据共享的要求，迫切需要建立一个扩展性强、访问便捷的全球数据分发基础设施。

Method: 研究者在StashCache项目成功经验的基础上，构建了Open Science Data Federation (OSDF)。该方法通过整合额外的数据源（20个）和缓存节点（30个），引入新的访问方法，并建立监控和计费机制，扩展了原有系统，从而形成一个全球性的数据分发网络。

Result: OSDF不仅成功整合并提升了系统的可访问性和性能，已成为美国国家网络基础设施的重要组成部分。在大熊湖太阳天文台（BBSO）的应用案例中，OSDF提供了标准化且可靠的数据访问服务，利用其全球分布的缓存节点，成功构建了可在全球任意地点对BBSO图像进行处理的流水线。

Conclusion: OSDF通过扩展现有架构并集成新功能，有效解决了科学界的数据分发与共享难题，证明了其在提升数据访问可靠性和支持全球分布式数据处理方面的实用价值。

Abstract: The growing demand for extensive data processing is now a standard in many scientific fields. Efficiently distributing data to processing sites and enabling seamless sharing has become crucial. The Open Science Data Federation (OSDF) builds on the success of the StashCache project to establish a global data distribution network. By expanding StashCache, OSDF integrates additional data origins and caches, enhancing accessibility and performance (20 origins and 30 caches), new access methods, and monitoring and accounting mechanisms. Additionally, the OSDF has become essential to the US national cyber-infrastructure landscape due to the sharing requirements of recent NSF solicitations. One use case for the OSDF is the data access to the Big Bear Solar Observatory (BBSO). Integrating the BBSO data into the OSDF provided standard and reliable data access. Moreover, the OSDF caches provide local data worldwide. Using the OSDF and the BBSO data, creating a pipeline to apply image processing techniques to all images from BBSO anywhere on the planet was possible.

</details>


### [13] [Open Science Data Federation -- operation and monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15437)
*Fabio Andrijauskas,Derek Weitzel,Frank Wuerthwein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了开放科学数据联邦（OSDF），这是一个基于StashCache项目构建的全球数据访问网络，旨在为科学研究提供高效的数据分发和共享服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模数据处理在众多科学领域变得普遍，如何将数据分发到处理站点并与协作者高效共享已成为关键需求。此外，美国国家科学基金会（NSF）近期的项目征集对数据共享提出了要求，迫切需要一个能够支持这些需求的国家网络基础设施。

Method: OSDF建立在成熟的StashCache项目之上，通过扩展该项目来构建全球数据访问网络。具体措施包括增加新的数据源（origins）和缓存节点（caches）、扩展访问方法，以及引入监控和记账机制。

Result: OSDF已成功成为美国国家网络基础设施景观的重要组成部分，能够满足NSF项目征集中的数据共享要求。该系统持续被众多研究合作项目和个体用户使用，能够将数据传输至多种研究基础设施和项目中。

Conclusion: OSDF作为一个全球数据访问网络，有效地解决了科学研究中的数据分发与共享难题，并通过支持国家级科研基础设施需求，确立了其在开放科学数据生态中的重要地位。

Abstract: Extensive data processing is becoming commonplace in many fields of science. Distributing data to processing sites and providing methods to share the data with collaborators efficiently has become essential. The Open Science Data Federation (OSDF) builds upon the successful StashCache project to create a global data access network. The OSDF expands the StashCache project to add new data origins and caches, access methods, monitoring, and accounting mechanisms. Additionally, the OSDF has become an integral part of the U.S. national cyberinfrastructure landscape due to the sharing requirements of recent NSF solicitations, which the OSDF is uniquely positioned to enable. The OSDF continues to be utilized by many research collaborations and individual users, which pull the data to many research infrastructures and projects.

</details>


### [14] [A Few GPUs, A Whole Lotta Scale: Faithful LLM Training Emulation with PrismLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15617)
*Shaoke Xi,ChonLam Lao,Boyi Jia,Jiaqi Gao,Zhipeng Zhang,Jiamin Cao,Brian Sutioso,Erci Xu,Minlan Yu,Kui Ren,Yong Li,Zhengping Qian,Ennan Zhai,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PrismLLM系统，通过切片构建高保真执行图和混合仿真技术，在少量GPU上精确模拟大规模LLM训练行为，解决大规模集群资源稀缺与调试复杂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型训练依赖数千GPU集群，导致训练框架的开发、调试和性能调优变得复杂且成本高昂。工程师需重现生产环境行为以诊断故障或评估优化，但需要频繁独占访问生产级集群，而大部分GPU已被生产任务占用。现有方法中，仿真依赖难维护的复杂性能模型，缩小规模实验无法捕捉规模相关行为。

Method: PrismLLM采用基于切片的方法构建高保真执行图，捕获目标规模的计算、通信和依赖关系。通过混合仿真技术执行：选定rank运行原始程序，其余rank作为虚拟参与者进行回放，从而在少量GPU上观察大规模执行行为。

Result: 在大规模LLM训练负载上，PrismLLM精确重现性能与内存行为：迭代时间平均误差仅0.58%，GPU峰值内存使用误差小于0.01%。系统可用少于原部署所需1%的物理GPU仿真多达8192 GPU的集群。

Conclusion: PrismLLM成功解耦大规模执行与大规模集群访问需求，使工程师能以极少量GPU资源精确观察和调试大规模LLM训练行为，大幅降低开发调试成本，为大规模模型训练框架优化提供实用工具。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training today runs on clusters spanning thousands of GPUs. While this scale enables rapid model advances, developing, debugging, and performance-tuning the training framework inevitably becomes complex and costly. This is because engineers often need to reproduce production behaviors to diagnose failures or evaluate optimizations, thereby demanding frequent and even exclusive access to production-scale clusters -- which becomes increasingly hard given that the majority of GPUs are already committed to production workloads. Simulation relies on complex performance models that are difficult to maintain, and downscaled experiments often fail to capture scale-dependent behaviors.
  We present PrismLLM to decouple large-scale execution from the need to access large clusters, enabling engineers to run and observe ranks of interest under faithful large-scale behavior using only a few GPUs. PrismLLM constructs a high-fidelity execution graph via a slicing-based approach that captures computation, communication, and dependencies of the target scale. Then, PrismLLM performs hybrid emulation where selected ranks execute the original program while the remaining ranks are replayed as virtual participants.
  Experiments on large-scale LLM training workloads show that PrismLLM accurately reproduces performance and memory behavior, achieving only 0.58\% average error in iteration time and less than 0.01\% error in peak GPU memory usage. PrismLLM can emulate clusters of up to 8192 GPUs using fewer than 1\% of the physical GPUs required by the original deployment.

</details>


### [15] [ParamSpMM: Adaptive and Efficient Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication on GPUs for GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15695)
*Lixing Zhang,Guanhua Ye,Hongzheng Li,Shigang Li,Yingxia Shao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ParamSpMM，一种参数化的稀疏矩阵乘法方法，通过新的PCSR数据结构和ML决策器，在GNN训练中实现1.92倍的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络应用快速增长，SpMM是其关键算子，但现有SpMM设计难以适应多样化的输入特性，缺乏对不同输入特征的适应性优化。

Method: 提出ParamSpMM参数化方法，引入参数化压缩稀疏行(PCSR)数据结构，灵活集成现有优化技术；配置优化技术以适应不同输入特性；使用基于机器学习的SpMM决策器，根据输入特征预测最优配置。

Result: ParamSpMM相比Nvidia cuSPARSE实现1.92倍的平均加速，显著提升GNN训练效率；通过统计和实证分析揭示了现有SpMM优化的局限性。

Conclusion: 参数化方法能有效解决SpMM在不同输入特性下的适应性问题，PCSR数据结构和ML决策器的结合为GNN中的SpMM计算提供了高效且自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Fueled by the ability to mine real-world graph data, GNN applications have experienced phenomenal growth. Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (SpMM) is a critical operator in GNNs. However, existing SpMM designs for GNNs struggle to adapt to diverse input characteristics. In this paper, we first conduct a comprehensive analysis of existing SpMM optimizations, revealing their limitations through statistical and empirical evidence. Based on this analysis, we introduce ParamSpMM, a parametric approach for highly adaptive and efficient SpMM computation in GNNs. It incorporates a new data structure, the Parameterized Compressed Sparse Row (PCSR), to flexibly integrate existing optimization techniques. ParamSpMM enables the configuration of these optimization techniques according to various input characteristics. Furthermore, we complement ParamSpMM with an ML-based SpMM-decider that predicts optimal configurations based on carefully crafted input features. Our evaluations demonstrate that ParamSpMM outperforms Nvidia cuSPARSE with an average speedup of 1.92x, significantly enhancing GNN training efficiency.

</details>


### [16] [Scale: Deep Reinforcement Learning for Container Scheduling in Serverless Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15704)
*Chen Chen,Zihan Jia,Andrea Sabbioni,Reza Farahani,Lei Jiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Scale框架，针对无服务器边缘计算场景，利用深度强化学习进行容器调度与资源分配，在满足SLO约束的同时实现了近乎最优的资源分配效率，并将决策时间缩短了99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度动态、异构且分布式的边缘计算系统中，采用无服务器计算的事件驱动模型在请求放置与资源管理方面面临重大挑战，亟需高效的请求到容器分配方案以减少资源过度配置和不必要的数据移动。

Method: 提出了一个名为Scale的感知服务等级目标（SLO）的容器调度与资源分配框架。该框架采用基于策略的深度强化学习算法，在动态工作负载下平衡系统稳定性与性能，并将SLO约束、端到端延迟和数据局部性联合纳入调度决策过程。

Result: 基于华为云大规模真实数据集的仿真实验表明，Scale的求解结果与最先进的整数线性规划求解器相比，差距仅在1.11至1.15倍之间（接近最优），同时决策时间减少了高达99%。

Conclusion: Scale框架成功解决了无服务器边缘计算中的资源管理难题，在保证近似最优解质量的前提下大幅提升了决策效率，证明了深度强化学习在复杂动态边缘环境中进行调度的有效性。

Abstract: Serverless computing has emerged as a promising computing paradigm for edge computing. However, adopting the event driven model in highly dynamic, heterogeneous, and distributed edge systems poses significant challenges in request placement and resource management. Efficiently allocating requests to containers is therefore critical to reduce resource over provisioning and unnecessary data movement. This paper proposes Scale, a Service Level Objective aware container scheduling and resource allocation framework designed for serverless edge computing. Scale employs a policy based deep reinforcement learning algorithm to balance system stability and performance under dynamic workloads. The design jointly incorporates SLO constraints, end to end latency, and data locality into the scheduling decision process. Extensive simulations using large scale real world datasets from Huawei Cloud demonstrate that Scale achieves solutions within a factor of 1.11 to 1.15 of a state of the art Integer Linear Programming solver, while reducing decision making time by up to 99%.

</details>


### [17] [ADAPT: A Self-Calibrating Proactive Autoscaler for Container Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15788)
*Himanshu Singh Baghel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADAPT方法，通过在线EWMA估计器动态追踪冷启动延迟，并结合MPC控制器实现自适应前摄式自动扩缩容，显著降低了SLA违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前摄式容器自动扩缩容依赖于准确的资源配置延迟（冷启动时间）预测，但在实际环境中，该延迟在不同环境和扩容事件间存在显著差异，导致难以确定合适的前瞻规划时间窗口。

Method: 提出ADAPT（Adaptive Duration Approximation for Predictive Timing）在线EWMA估计器，在运行时追踪冷启动持续时间；将ADAPT输出的动态规划窗口FH-OPT输入到模型预测控制器（MPC）中，该控制器在滚动窗口上优化副本数量；形成闭环前摄式自动扩缩容系统，根据实测资源配置延迟自适应调整前瞻时间。

Result: 在六种工作负载原型和五种随机种子上，对比三种策略（MPC+LSTM、MPC+Prophet、HPA）：MPC+LSTM在所有工作负载上SLA违规率均低于5%；响应式HPA为7-19%；MPC+Prophet在双模态流量上高达28.7%。

Conclusion: 通过动态追踪和适应资源配置延迟，ADAPT结合MPC控制器实现了高效的前摄式自动扩缩容，显著优于传统响应式方法和固定前瞻窗口的预测方法，为容器化工作负载提供了更可靠的自适应扩展能力。

Abstract: Proactive autoscaling for containerized workloads depends on knowing the provisioning delay, i.e., the time between a scaling decision and the moment new capacity is ready to serve traffic. In practice, this cold-start duration can vary substantially across environments and even across consecutive scale-out events. We present ADAPT (Adaptive Duration Approximation for Predictive Timing), an online EWMA estimator that tracks coldstart duration at runtime. ADAPT feeds a dynamic planning horizon, FH-OPT, into a Model Predictive Controller (MPC) that optimizes replica counts over a rolling window. Together, these components form a closed-loop proactive autoscaling design that adapts its lookahead based on measured provisioning delay. Evaluated across three policies (MPC+LSTM, MPC+Prophet, HPA) and six workload archetypes with five random seeds, MPC+LSTM achieves below 5% SLA violation on all workloads, compared with 7-19% for reactive HPA and up to 28.7% for MPC+Prophet on bimodal traffic.

</details>


### [18] [Evaluating Container Orchestration for Neuromorphic Workloads in Virtual Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15866)
*Huyen Pham,Bilhanan Silverajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在虚拟边缘环境中使用Kubernetes部署和编排SNN工作负载的可行性。实验发现SNN对资源高度敏感，CPU限制导致延迟增加47.6倍、吞吐量下降49倍；K3d默认的轮询路由策略在副本扩展时引入了显著的尾部延迟，与长时推理工作负载存在不匹配。分类准确度保持稳定。研究为容器化边缘环境中的神经形态工作负载部署提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘计算的广泛采用，能够在严格的资源和能源约束下运行的工作负载需求日益增长。神经形态计算，特别是脉冲神经网络(SNNs)，通过事件驱动计算提供了一种节能的替代方案。然而，SNN工作负载在现代容器编排框架（尤其是边缘环境）中的行为在很大程度上仍未被探索。

Method: 在运行于Windows 11主机（使用WSL2和Docker Desktop）的单节点K3d集群上进行实验。评估指标包括端到端延迟、吞吐量、分类准确度、基础设施开销以及在并发负载下的运行时行为。通过限制CPU和内存资源来测试不同资源约束下的性能表现。

Result: SNN工作负载对资源可用性高度敏感。将CPU限制为0.5核时，中位延迟增加47.6倍，吞吐量下降49倍，最严格的配置因内存不足而失败。分类准确度在所有有效配置中保持稳定。K3d成功部署和扩展了SNN工作负载，但其默认轮询路由策略在副本扩展时引入了显著的尾部延迟，暴露了无状态负载均衡假设与长时推理工作负载之间的不匹配。

Conclusion: 本研究为在容器化边缘环境中部署神经形态工作负载提供了基线，强调了资源供应和编排配置的重要性。未来工作应探索改进的路由策略、内存优化以及在物理边缘硬件上的验证。

Abstract: The growing adoption of edge computing has created an increasing need for workloads capable of operating under strict resource and energy constraints. Neuromorphic computing, and spiking neural networks (SNNs) in particular, offers an energy-efficient alternative to conventional machine learning through event-driven computation. However, how SNN workloads behave when deployed within modern container orchestration frameworks, especially in edge environments, remains largely unexplored. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying and orchestrating SNN workloads in a virtual edge environment using Kubernetes, focusing on end-to-end latency, throughput, classification accuracy, infrastructure overhead, and runtime behavior under concurrent load.
  Experiments were conducted on a single-node K3d cluster running on a Windows 11 host with WSL2 and Docker Desktop. The results show that SNN workloads are highly sensitive to resource availability. Restricting CPU to 0.5 cores increased median latency by 47.6x and reduced throughput by 49x, while the most constrained configuration failed due to insufficient memory. Classification accuracy remained stable across all working configurations. From an orchestration perspective, K3d successfully deployed and scaled SNN workloads, though its default round-robin routing policy introduced significant tail latency under replica scaling, highlighting a mismatch between stateless load-balancing assumptions and long-running inference workloads.
  Overall, this study provides a baseline for deploying neuromorphic workloads in containerized edge environments and highlights the importance of resource provisioning and orchestration configuration. Future work should explore improved routing strategies, memory optimization, and validation on physical edge hardware.

</details>


### [19] [High-Performance Star-M SVD for Big Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16058)
*Md Taufique Hussain,Grey Ballard,Aditya Devarakonda,Srinivas Eswar,Naman Pesricha,Vishwas Rao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种共享内存并行的高性能软件解决方案，用于高效实现star-M张量SVD算法，以实现对大规模科学数据集的最优压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大数据时代，高效压缩大规模数据集并执行复杂数学运算至关重要。基于张量的分解方法相比传统矩阵方法具有更优越的压缩能力和更小的精度损失。然而，现有的star-M张量SVD软件实现主要局限于面向生产力的编程语言，缺乏高性能实现。

Method: 在star-M张量框架下，开发了共享内存并行的高性能解决方案，高效实现star-M张量SVD的底层算法。该方法具有最优性保证，能够以类矩阵方式对张量进行分解。

Result: 成功开发了高性能的并行软件实现，能够对大规模科学数据集实现最优压缩，为增强数据分析和洞察铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 该高性能软件实现使得star-M张量SVD算法能够高效处理大规模科学数据集，显著提升了数据压缩能力，为科学研究中的大数据分析提供了有效的技术支持。

Abstract: In the era of big data, effectively compressing large datasets while performing complex mathematical operations is crucial. Tensor-based decomposition methods have shown superior compression capabilities with minimal loss of accuracy compared to traditional matrix methods. Under the star-M tensor framework, tensors can be decomposed in a matrix-mimetic way, including using the star-M SVD. This tensor SVD has optimality guarantees and has shown exceptional performance on specific types of data, but software implementations have been mostly limited to productivity-oriented languages. In this work, we present our development of a shared-memory parallel, high-performance solution designed to efficiently implement the underlying algorithms. This software will enable optimal compression of extensive scientific datasets, paving the way for enhanced data analysis and insights.

</details>


### [20] [An efficient multi-GPU implementation for the Discontinuous Galerkin ocean model SLIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16082)
*Miguel De Le Court,Vincent Legat,Ange P. Ishimwe,Colin Scherpereel,Emmanuel Hanert,Jonathan Lambrechts*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究开发了支持单/多GPU及NVIDIA/AMD架构的全3D间断伽辽金有限元海洋模型，单张A100 GPU性能等效约1500个CPU核心，4 GPU节点相比128核CPU节点实现约50倍加速，并在大堡礁实现了比现有模型高5倍的空间分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化网格海洋模型在海岸应用中具有重要价值，能够表示复杂几何形状并进行局部网格加密，但其计算成本高昂，尤其是基于间断伽辽金有限元方法的模型自由度显著多于传统方法，限制了其广泛应用。GPU高性能计算架构的出现为解决这一瓶颈提供了新途径。

Method: 开发了优化的全3D间断伽辽金有限元海洋模型实现，支持单GPU和多GPU系统，兼容NVIDIA和AMD架构。采用了内存布局优化、核级并行化以及用于关键垂直过程的无矩阵求解器等高性能计算策略。

Result: 基准测试表明，单张HPC级GPU（如NVIDIA A100）性能约等效于1500个CPU核心；用4xA100 GPU节点替代128核CPU节点可获得约50倍加速；弱扩展效率可维持至1024个GPU。在大堡礁实际应用中实现了比现有最精确模型高5倍的空间分辨率，同时保持物理时间与数值时间比为100。

Conclusion: GPU加速的间断伽辽金有限元方法能够显著提升非结构化网格海洋建模能力，使此前无法实现的超高分辨率海岸模拟成为可能，为海洋模型计算成本问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Unstructured-mesh ocean models are increasingly used for coastal applications due to their ability to represent complex geometries and apply local grid refinement where needed. However, their broader use has been hindered by their high computational cost, particularly for models based on the Discontinuous Galerkin finite element (DG-FE) method, which involves significantly more degrees of freedom than traditional finite volume or continuous finite element approaches. The rapid emergence of GPU-based high-performance computing architectures now offers a pathway to address this limitation, as DG-FE formulations are inherently well suited to massively parallel, element-wise computations. Here, we present a full 3D DG-FE ocean model implementation optimized for both single- and multi-GPU systems, with support for both NVIDIA and AMD architectures. We detail the computational strategies employed to achieve high performance, including memory layout optimization, kernel-level parallelization, and matrix-free solvers for key vertical processes. Benchmark results demonstrate that a single HPC-grade GPU (e.g. NVIDIA A100) delivers performance equivalent to approximately 1500 CPU cores, while replacing a 128-core CPU node with a 4xA100 GPU node yields a speedup of around 50x. Weak-scaling efficiency is maintained up to 1024 GPUs. We further demonstrate the model's capabilities on a real-world application in the Great Barrier Reef, achieving a spatial resolution five times finer than the most accurate existing model while maintaining a physical-to-numerical time ratio of 100. These results highlight how GPU-accelerated DG-FE methods can dramatically advance the capabilities of unstructured-mesh ocean modeling, enabling ultra-high-resolution coastal simulations that were previously infeasible.

</details>


### [21] [A GPU Accelerated Temporal Window-Based Random Walk Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16182)
*Md Ashfaq Salehin,George Parisis,Luc Berthouze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tempest，一个GPU加速的流式时序随机游走引擎，通过双索引组织和分层协作调度器，在十亿级边流上实现了实时处理，显著提升了吞吐量并保证了因果正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序随机游走被广泛用于分析微服务、金融和在线平台等领域的时间戳交互。然而，在真实世界中，图以高容量流的形式演化，使得连续摄入、高效内存使用和严格的时间排序成为实际部署的挑战，现有方法难以在大规模场景下高效生成此类游走。

Method: 提出Tempest系统，采用GPU原生双索引组织共享边存储，结合分层协作调度器。调度器根据每步节点收敛情况，以线程、束或块粒度分派游走，实现高效的起始边选择、逐跳因果强制执行和基于窗口的驱逐（无需同步）。此外，还提供了常见时序偏置函数的闭形式常数时间采样器。

Result: 评估表明，Tempest在滑动窗口下能够持续实时处理十亿级边流，在摄入和游走生成吞吐量方面优于现有系统，同时保持因果正确性。

Conclusion: Tempest成功解决了大规模流式时序随机游走生成的挑战，通过GPU加速和高效调度策略，实现了高吞吐量和低延迟的实时处理，为时序网络分析提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal random walks, which sample causality-preserving paths, are widely used to analyze time-stamped interactions in domains such as microservices, finance, and online platforms. Generating such walks at scale is challenging because real-world graphs evolve as high-volume streams, making continuous ingestion, efficient memory usage, and strict temporal ordering essential for practical deployment. We present Tempest (TEMPoral nEtwork Streaming Traversals), a GPU-accelerated engine for streaming temporal random walks. Tempest combines a GPU-native dual-index organization over a shared edge store with a hierarchical cooperative scheduler that dispatches walks at thread, warp, or block granularity based on per-step node convergence, enabling efficient start-edge selection, hop-by-hop causality enforcement, and window-based eviction without synchronization. It further provides closed-form constant-time samplers for common temporal bias functions. Our evaluation demonstrates sustained real-time processing of billion-edge streams under sliding windows, outperforming prior systems in ingestion and walk generation throughput while preserving causal correctness.

</details>


### [22] [Runtime-Orchestrated Second-Order Optimization for Scalable LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16184)
*Yishun Lu,Junhao Zhang,Zeyu Yang,Wes Armour*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Asteria运行时系统，通过分离二阶优化逻辑与GPU训练路径，利用状态分层存储和异步预计算，解决大模型二阶优化训练中的系统开销瓶颈，实现了在受限和分布式环境下的高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化方法虽然能提高大语言模型训练的样本效率，但在实际应用中，维护和更新大规模矩阵优化器状态会带来巨大的系统开销，这一瓶颈限制了其在GPU上的实用性。

Method: 提出Asteria运行时系统：1) 将优化器状态动态分布到GPU内存、CPU内存和NVMe存储中；2) 利用训练钩子提前准备影子状态，使昂贵的逆根计算在主机端异步进行；3) 在分布式训练中采用有界延迟协议，通过拓扑感知协调限制同步频率。

Result: 在DGX Spark平台（单GPU，128GB统一内存）上支持了1B参数模型的二阶训练；在多节点GH200系统上训练7B参数模型时，降低了优化器开销，减少了延迟峰值，加速了收敛，并保持了SOAP和KL-Shampoo的优化优势。

Conclusion: 二阶大模型训练的实用化不仅需要简化优化器本身，更需要从运行时层面重新设计优化器状态管理、后台计算和分布式同步机制。

Abstract: Second-order methods offer an attractive path toward more sample-efficient LLM training, but their practical use is often blocked by the systems cost of maintaining and updating large matrix-based optimizer states. We introduce \textbf{Asteria}, a runtime system designed to remove this bottleneck by separating second-order optimization logic from the critical GPU training path. Rather than keeping all preconditioner state on the accelerator, Asteria dynamically distributes optimizer state across GPU memory, CPU memory, and optional NVMe storage according to architectural constraints and runtime pressure. It further uses training hooks to prepare shadow states in advance, allowing expensive inverse-root computations to proceed asynchronously on the host while GPU computation continues. For distributed training, Asteria employs a bounded-staleness protocol that limits synchronization frequency while preserving optimizer effectiveness through topology-aware coordination.
  We evaluate Asteria on both memory-constrained and distributed training settings. On a DGX Spark platform with a single GB10 GPU and 128GB unified memory, Asteria supports second-order training for a 1B-parameter language model. On multi-node GH200 systems, it lowers visible optimizer overhead, reduces recurring latency spikes, accelerates convergence in wall-clock time, and maintains the optimization advantages of SOAP and KL-Shampoo in a 7B-parameter language model. Our results suggest that second-order LLM training can be made practical not by simplifying the optimizer alone, but by rethinking how optimizer state, background computation, and distributed synchronization are managed at the runtime level.

</details>


### [23] [Designing Datacenter Power Delivery Hierarchies for the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16255)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 随着AI加速器功率密度激增（预计2027年达1MW），数据中心面临功率搁浅挑战。本文提出评估框架，量化多资源搁浅对可部署容量、成本和性能的影响，指出AI数据中心规划应关注可部署容量而非装机容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器需求导致机架功率密度急剧上升，给数据中心供电设计带来重大挑战。当功率密度增加时，为不同目标密度设计的数据中心可能出现功率搁浅（stranded power），即无法充分利用其供电层级所配置的功率。由于电网容量在AI时代是稀缺资源，设计必须在数据中心长期生命周期和多个硬件世代中保持高效。

Method: 开发了一个评估数据中心供电设计的框架，基于吞吐量、功率和成本指标，在真实的到达序列、超售和解退役序列下进行评估。该框架结合了GPU、计算和存储部署的预测模型，以及基于Microsoft Azure生产数据的运营因素。模型考虑了电气拓扑、部署粒度、放置策略、功率超售和工作负载混合等因素的联合依赖关系及其随时间的演化。

Result: 研究结果表明，多资源搁浅显著改变了可部署容量、有效资本支出和交付性能。研究量化了来自机架级和Pod级AI系统日益增长的密度如何影响这些结果。

Conclusion: 对于AI数据中心设计，相关的规划目标不应是装机兆瓦数（installed megawatts），而是随时间变化的可部署容量（deployable capacity over time）。

Abstract: Demand for AI accelerators is rapidly increasing rack power density, with projections approaching 1MW per deployment by 2027. This poses a major challenge for datacenter power delivery designers. As power densities increase, a datacenter designed for a different target density may strand power, i.e., may be unable to use all the power that its delivery hierarchy has provisioned. Designs must remain efficient over long datacenter lifetimes and multiple hardware generations. Power utilization is particularly important as grid power capacity is a scarce resource in the AI era.
  Designing an efficient power delivery hierarchy for the long run is difficult because rack placement feasibility, workload impact, and cost depend jointly on electrical topology, deployment granularity, placement policy, power oversubscription, and workload mix. Moreover, each of these factors evolve over time, have inter-dependencies across multiple resource dimensions, and generally do not lend themselves to closed-form analysis.
  To address this challenge, we develop a framework for evaluating datacenter power delivery designs using throughput, power, and cost metrics over realistic arrival, oversubscription, and decommissioning sequences. The framework combines projection models for GPU, compute, and storage deployments with operational factors grounded in production data from Microsoft Azure. Our results show that multi-resource stranding materially changes deployable capacity, effective capital expenditure, and delivered performance, and quantify how rising density from rack- and pod-scale AI systems shapes these outcomes. For AI datacenter design, the relevant planning objective is not installed megawatts, but deployable capacity over time.

</details>
