### [24] [Resilience under Uncertainty: Securing 6G through Stochastic Reinstantiation of RAN Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15446)
*Gabriel Almeida,Jacek Kibiłda,Joao F. Santos,Kleber Vieira Cardoso*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对基站解耦移动网络中的级联故障问题，提出了首个利用自适应重新实例化RAN功能的弹性机制。通过两阶段随机优化方法在不确定性下做出重实例化和路由决策，实验表明该方法相比传统机制可提升高达80%的恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络中基站的解耦引入了新的安全威胁，单个RAN功能的故障可能触发级联故障并中断整个功能链，导致网络性能下降和服务中断。现有机制缺乏针对解耦网络特性的有效恢复手段，特别是在基础设施受损情况下存在用户位置和无线信道条件的不确定性。

Method: 提出了一种弹性机制，当级联故障破坏无线电单元(RU)时，通过在备用云端位置重新实例化中央单元(CU)和分布式单元(DU)来恢复功能链。将恢复过程建模为两阶段随机优化问题：第一阶段在不确定性下做出重实例化和路由决策，第二阶段在不确定性解决后进行带宽分配。采用样本平均近似(SAA)方法将问题转化为可处理的确定性等价问题进行求解。

Result: 在真实世界的解耦移动网络拓扑上进行了数值评估，覆盖多种故障场景和流量需求条件。实验结果表明，与传统弹性机制相比，该解决方案可以实现高达80%的恢复性能提升，有效验证了所提方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发并验证了首个针对解耦移动网络的弹性机制，能够在存在基础设施受损和不确定性条件下有效缓解服务中断并维持服务连续性，为未来移动网络的可靠性和弹性设计提供了重要的技术支撑。

Abstract: The disaggregation of base stations into discrete RAN functions introduces new threats to mobile networks, as failures in one RAN function can trigger cascading failures and interrupt entire function chains, with potential to degrade network performance and disrupt service. In this paper, we propose the first resilience mechanism for disaggregated mobile networks that leverages the adaptive reinstantiation of RAN functions under uncertainty to mitigate disruptions and maintain service continuity in the presence of compromised infrastructure. Our mechanism reacts to cascading failures that disrupt Radio Units (RUs) by reinstantiating Central Units (CUs) and Distributed Units (DUs) in alternative cloud locations, restoring their function chains while accounting for uncertainty in users' locations and wireless channel conditions during the in-failure state. We formulate this recovery process as a two-stage stochastic optimization problem, where reinstantiation and routing decisions are made under uncertainty, and bandwidth allocation decisions are performed after uncertainty is resolved. We solve the problem using a Sample Average Approximation (SAA)-based solution as a tractable, deterministic equivalent problem. We numerically evaluate our approach on a real-world disaggregated mobile network topology across multiple failure scenarios and traffic demand conditions, and our results demonstrate that our solution can achieve up to 80% higher recovery performance compared to conventional resilience mechanisms.

</details>


### [25] [TG-DIN: Theory-Guided Demand Inference Network for Generalizable QoS Measurement and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15550)
*Fuliang Yang,Feng Ye*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TG-DIN，一种理论指导的需求推断网络，通过结合可微分理论层从可观测的QoS数据中推断潜在用户需求，在跨分布迁移和真实数据应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的黑盒模型直接预测QoS结果，缺乏可解释性且难以泛化。为了解决从可观测网络QoS数据中推断潜在用户需求的问题，需要一种既能提供可解释性又能在分布变化时保持鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出TG-DIN（理论指导的需求推断网络），将潜在需求显式建模为中间变量，通过基于调度和排队原理的可微分理论层将其与可观测行为关联。引入基于理论的随机化训练机制，在无需标注需求数据的情况下让模型接触多样化且物理意义明确的操作条件。

Result: 大量合成实验表明，TG-DIN在不同容量、需求水平和流量模式下具有强泛化能力，在分布变化下显著优于纯数据驱动基线。仅在合成数据上训练并直接应用于真实数据包追踪时，TG-DIN能准确恢复共享链路场景下的每用户分配结构。

Conclusion: 理论指导的归纳偏置对于在动态网络环境中实现可迁移、可部署的推断具有有效性，为拥塞诊断、资源分配、容量规划和策略评估等下游任务提供了可解释、机制一致的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce TG-DIN, a theory-guided demand inference network that infers latent user demand from observable network quality-of-service (QoS) measurements. Rather than directly predicting QoS outcomes using black-box models, TG-DIN explicitly models latent demand as an intermediate variable and links it to observable behavior through a differentiable theory layer grounded in scheduling and queuing principles. This design yields an interpretable, mechanism-consistent representation of user demand that is directly applicable to downstream tasks such as congestion diagnosis, resource allocation, capacity planning, and policy evaluation. The theory layer further enables a principled randomized training regime that exposes the model to diverse yet physically meaningful operating conditions without requiring labeled demand data. Extensive synthetic experiments show that TG-DIN generalizes robustly across capacities, demand levels, and traffic patterns, substantially outperforming purely data-driven baselines under distribution shift. Moreover, when trained exclusively on synthetic data and applied directly to real packet traces, TG-DIN accurately recovers per-user allocation structure in shared-link scenarios. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of theory-guided inductive biases for achieving transferable, deployment-ready inference in dynamic network environments.

</details>


### [26] [Operator-Controlled 6G: From Connectivity Infrastructure to Guaranteed Digital Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15553)
*David Soldani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文主张6G网络应优先考虑运营商控制权而非技术主导，提出了基于Rakuten Mobile成功部署经验的架构框架，旨在实现运营商可控、可盈利的6G网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五代供应商主导的架构导致运营商采购和运营不属于自己、无法修改平台、无法审计AI层的网络，6G正处于结构性拐点，必须扭转这一趋势，重新排序运营商优先级：控制优先、客户优先、业务优先、运营优先、技术最后。

Method: 提出两个核心贡献：1）6G控制公约，定义三层所有权分类法（拥有、联合、消费），根据战略价值分配架构主权；2）保证经济，定义五层结果定价的商业模型，将运营商控制转化为可执行的服务级目标。框架基于Rakuten Mobile的运营证据（全球首个国家级、全云原生、全Open RAN部署，2025财年实现全年EBITDA盈利），并与ITU-R IMT-2030、3GPP、NGMN、ETSI、O-RAN、AI-RAN等标准对齐。

Result: Rakuten Mobile的部署证明了运营商可控6G的可行性，提出了2025-2032及以后的三阶段路线图和七项利益相关者特定行动呼吁，将架构转化为行业承诺。

Conclusion: 2026-2028年做出的决策将决定6G是成为保证数字服务平台，还是另一个依赖供应商的基础设施周期。6G必须让技术服务于运营商控制、客户结果、可货币化保证和软件驱动的运营。

Abstract: Sixth-generation mobile networks (6G) are approaching a structural inflection point. Five generations of vendor-led architectures have left operators procuring and operating networks they do not own, on platforms they cannot modify, with AI layers they cannot audit. This paper argues that 6G must reverse this trajectory by reordering operator priorities: Control First, Customer First, Business First, Operations First, and Technology Last. Technology should serve operator control, customer outcomes, monetizable guarantees, and software-driven operations, not dictate them.Two contributions operationalize this thesis. The 6G Control Compact defines a three-layer ownership taxonomy--own, federate, and consume--that allocates architectural sovereignty according to strategic value. The Guarantee Economy defines a five-tier, outcome-priced commercial model that converts operator control into enforceable service-level objectives. The framework is grounded in operational evidence from Rakuten Mobile, the world's first national-scale, fully cloud-native, fully Open RAN deployment, which reached full-year EBITDA profitability in FY2025. It is aligned with the ITU-R IMT-2030 framework, 3GPP 6G use cases and service requirements, NGMN recommendations, ETSI standards, O-RAN Alliance and AI-RAN Alliance specifications, IOWN Global Forum sustainability metrics, Linux Foundation initiatives, and leading industry and academic programs. A three-phase roadmap covering 2025-2027, 2027-2029, and 2029-2032 and beyond, together with seven stakeholder-specific calls to action, translates the architecture into industry commitments. The central claim is that Rakuten Mobile's deployment demonstrates the feasibility of operator-controlled 6G. Decisions made during 2026-2028 will determine whether 6G becomes a platform for guaranteed digital services or another vendor-dependent infrastructure cycle.

</details>


### [27] [The Internet Runs on Names](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15646)
*Geoff Huston,Lixia Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 互联网架构原本基于IP地址设计，但实际运行已转向以DNS名称为基础。这种设计与操作的不匹配导致了复杂性、脆弱性和安全风险，本文旨在揭示这一问题以为后续解决方案铺路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网的TCP/IP架构最初是为基于IP地址的主机间弹性数据包传输而设计。然而，随着应用和服务整合到大规模平台中，部署实践发生了根本性变化：网络现在主要基于名称运行。这种在缺乏顶层规划下发生的演变，导致了原始设计与当前操作之间的错配，进而引发严重后果，如运营复杂性增加、系统脆弱性以及易受攻击（近期的高调宕机事件即为例证）。

Method: 本文通过历史回顾与现状分析，系统地阐述了互联网从基于地址的设计向基于名称的操作转变的过程。论文识别了DNS名称如何成为服务身份、可达性、负载均衡和信任的基础，而IP地址如何退化为临时的路由定位符，从而揭示了架构设计意图与实际运行模式之间的核心错配。

Result: 研究揭示了当前互联网架构中存在的关键矛盾：底层协议基于IP地址设计，而上层操作依赖名称。这种错配直接导致了运营复杂性随着间接层增加而增长、系统脆弱性提升以及面临更高的安全风险，这在近期的大规模宕机事故中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 揭示这一设计与操作的不匹配是理解其后果、评估继续沿用现有路径风险的关键第一步，为未来重新审视互联网架构设计或制定新的运营策略提供了必要的基础。

Abstract: The Internet's TCP/IP architecture was designed for resilient packet delivery between hosts identified by IP addresses. Over time, however, the consolidation of applications and services into large-scale platforms built on that universal packet-delivery substrate drove deployment practices that fundamentally changed the Internet's operational model: the network now operates primarily on names. DNS names have become the basis for service identity, reachability, load balancing, and trust, while IP addresses have become ephemeral routing locators. This change was driven by application needs and platform consolidation in the absence of any overarching plan. The resulting mismatch between the original address-based design and the current name-based operation leads to serious consequences: operational complexity that grows with each new layer of indirection, fragility, and vulnerability - as seen in recent high-profile outages. This paper exposes this mismatch as a necessary first step toward understanding its consequences and addressing the risks of continuing on the same path.

</details>


### [28] [The Shared Prosperity Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15791)
*Juan A. Cabrera,Pit Hofmann,Jonas Schulz,Frederic Benken,Hrjehor Mark,Giang T. Nguyen,Holger Boche,Frank H. P. Fitzek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“共享繁荣互联网”(SPI)架构，旨在让全社会广泛受益于自动化和人工智能。通过将Shannon、Landauer、Turing和Einstein的物理约束映射为可信赖性、可持续性和技术主权三大设计原则，并进一步转化为后香农通信、预期决策（负延迟）和超数字计算三大技术支柱，最终在远程教学和老年护理等场景中实现落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化和人工智能技术所带来的红利未能被社会广泛公平地获取，且网络计算架构的设计往往忽视了基本的物理约束。为了让AI和自动化的收益能够普惠社会，亟需一种全新的网络计算架构来填补这一空白。

Method: 该方法首先将Shannon、Landauer、Turing和Einstein的物理约束映射为三大设计原则：可信赖性、可持续性和技术主权。随后将这些原则转化为三大技术支柱：1）后香农、目标导向通信，仅传输任务所需信息；2）具有置信界限预动作和修正能力的预期决策（“负延迟”）；3）超数字计算，在截止期限和可计算性约束下选择能效最优的基底。研究基于远程教学、机器人远程教学和老年护理三个社会用例进行验证。

Result: 本文定义了SPI的可测量结果指标，包括延迟分解、每次事件的比特数、每次任务的能耗和CO2排放、安全与隐私指标以及鲁棒性指标，为评估SPI架构的效能提供了量化依据。

Conclusion: 通过将物理约束系统性地映射到设计原则和技术支柱，SPI架构为实现自动化和AI技术的社会化普惠提供了理论基础和实践路径，为未来网络计算系统的设计指明了方向。

Abstract: The Shared Prosperity Internet (SPI) is a network-computing architecture that makes the benefits of automation and Artificial Intelligence (AI) broadly accessible to the society. To ground its design, this paper maps the physical constraints of Shannon, Landauer, Turing, and Einstein to three design principles: trustworthiness, sustainability, and technological sovereignty, and maps them into three technical pillars: i) post-Shannon, goal-oriented communication that transmits only what the task requires; ii) anticipatory decision-making ("negative latency") with confidence-bounded pre-action and correction; and iii) beyond-digital computing that selects energy-optimal substrates under deadline and computability constraints. The SPI is grounded in three societal use cases: remote teaching for pupils, remote teaching of robots and cyber-physical systems, and elder care. Furthermore, this paper defines measurable outcomes for an SPI, including latency decomposition, bits per event, energy and CO2 per task, safety and privacy indicators, and robustness.

</details>


### [29] [Sustainability in Telecom: Energy-Efficient Networks and Circular Economy Models to Reduce Carbon Footprints and Increase Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16109)
*Praveen Hegde,Robin Joseph Varughese*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了电信行业可持续发展的两大驱动力：环保网络设计和循环经济原则的实施，通过节能技术和资源循环利用来应对环境挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据流量激增和5G技术的普及，电信行业环境影响日益加剧，运营商面临维持数字增长与履行环境责任的双重压力，亟需探索可持续的网络发展路径。

Method: 从两个维度展开研究：（1）网络节能技术：包括动态网络休眠模式、基于AI的流量管理、基站和数据中心的可再生能源利用；（2）循环经济实践：涵盖设备二手销售、电子废物处理和设备寿命延长。同时结合全球最大运营商的案例进行分析。

Result: 全球主要运营商案例显示，上述措施能够有效降低功耗和运营排放，同时带来成本节约和品牌形象提升等附加效益。但研究也揭示了技术限制、政策不足以及跨部门合作需求等局限性。

Conclusion: 提出整合绿色技术采用、循环供应链和监管作用的研究框架，强调技术创新、供应链优化和政策引导的协同对推动电信业长期环境与经济可持续发展的关键作用。

Abstract: The increasing environmental impact of the telecom industry has heightened the need for sustainable telecommunications networks. With skyrocketing data traffic and 5G gaining a foothold, telecom operators are under pressure to sustain digital growth while meeting their environmental responsibilities. In this paper, we discuss two fundamental drivers of sustainability in the telecom sector, namely, the design of environmentally friendly networks and the implementation of circular economy (CE) principles. Energy efficiency is pursued through dynamic network sleep modes, AI-based traffic management, and the utilization of renewable energy sources in base stations and data centers. Concurrently, circular economy practices, including device second-hand sales, e-waste treatment, and equipment lifespan extension, are becoming increasingly popular to address resource demand and mitigate carbon footprint. Case histories from the world's largest operators demonstrate some of the reductions in power consumption and operational emissions, as well as the associated savings and public image benefits. Although these solutions are promising, the paper also highlights several limitations, including technology constraints, policy shortcomings, and the need for cross-sector partnerships. We conclude with research implications in the form of a sustainable perspective that integrates the green adoption of technology, circular supply chains, and the role of regulation in driving long-term environmental and economic sustainability in the telecom industry.

</details>


### [30] [IoT and Massive Connectivity: Massive MIMO Optimization for IoT Connectivity in 5G and Beyond Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16129)
*Praveen Hegde,Robin Joseph Varughese*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大规模MIMO技术在5G/B5G物联网场景中的优化方法，分析了导频污染、能效等关键问题，总结了信道估计和机器学习资源分配等最新进展，并通过仿真揭示了容量、时延与能耗之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备的爆发式增长对无线通信基础设施提出了高速率、广覆盖和低时延的严峻挑战。为了满足5G及后5G/6G网络中海量设备的连接需求，需要研究能够提供可扩展、可靠且低时延通信的关键技术，而大规模MIMO被视为满足这些要求的重要解决方案。

Method: 本文针对大规模MIMO系统在物联网连接和低时延场景下的性能优化进行了全面研究。研究涵盖了密集物联网部署下的关键问题，包括导频污染、能效和用户调度。同时，综述了信道估计、混合波束成形以及基于机器学习的资源分配技术的最新研究进展。

Result: 基于仿真的结果揭示了系统容量、通信时延和能源利用率之间存在权衡关系。研究确定了能够为不同物联网应用提供最优性能的最佳运行点，验证了优化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 大规模MIMO技术是支持5G/B5G物联网需求的关键使能技术。未来的研究方向应关注与无蜂窝设计、智能反射表面（IRS）的集成，以及利用基于人工智能的网络编排来进一步增强物联网能力。

Abstract: The IoT's explosive growth has led to a massive number of connected devices, which demand high-speed and pervasive connectivity, posing significant challenges for current-generation wireless communication infrastructures. Considering our evolution toward 5G and beyond 5G (B5G) and 6G networks, providing scalable, reliable, and low-latency communication for billions of devices is therefore essential. Massive Multi-Input Multi-Output (Massive MIMO) is a promising technology for fulfilling the requirements of 5G, as it can spatially multiplex a large number of users and increase the spectral efficiency per user. In this paper, we focus on optimizing the performance of Massive MIMO systems in IoT connectivity and low-latency use cases for 5G and B5G. It studies key issues, including pilot contamination, energy efficiency, and user scheduling, among dense IoT deployments. In addition, it surveys all recent progress in channel estimation, hybrid beamforming, and machine learning-based resource allocation technologies for enhancing IoT scenarios related to Massive MIMO. Simulation-based results reveal the trade-offs between capacity, latency, and energy utilization, indicating an optimal operating point that ensures optimal performance for diverse IoT applications. The work concludes with a discussion of future research avenues, such as integration with cell-free designs, intelligent reflecting surfaces, or AI-based network orchestration for enhanced IoT capabilities.

</details>


### [31] [Restoring CFAR Validity for Single-Channel IoT Sensor Streams: A Monte Carlo Comparison of Five Detectors under Cortex-M0+ Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16159)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在物联网网状传感器网络场景下，对比了TSNFA检测器与四种经典检测器的性能，证明TSNFA是唯一能同时实现高检测率、高精度和低带宽占用的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对资源受限的物联网网状传感器网络，实时事件检测面临着灵敏度与受限网状无线电上误报负载之间的权衡挑战，需要寻找能平衡检测性能与资源消耗的检测算法。

Method: 将时谱噪底自适应（TSNFA）检测器与四种经典检测器（Lipski FFT能量检测器、CA-CFAR、OS-CFAR和CUSUM）在Cortex-M0+类硬件上实现，处理100 Hz一维时间序列（128样本帧），采用时间参考窗口替代雷达CFAR的空间参考单元。在四种配置（10和50节点；12 dB和18 dB SNR）下进行蒙特卡洛对比实验，每种配置重复5次持续24小时。

Result: TSNFA实现99.97%-100%的事件检测率，100%事件精度，每节点零误报簇。经典检测器各有所短：Lipski FFT、CA-CFAR和OS-CFAR维持近完美检测率但事件精度低于3%，每节点带宽145 kB/h至1.2 MB/h；CA-CFAR和OS-CFAR误警性能相同，均陷入相同的宽带统计失效模式；CUSUM检测率随SNR下降，从18 dB约70%降至12 dB的51%。

Conclusion: TSNFA是唯一能同时实现高检测率、高精度和低节点带宽的检测算法，适合资源受限的物联网网状传感器网络实时事件检测应用。

Abstract: Real-time event detection in IoT mesh sensor networks must balance sensitivity against false-positive load on a constrained mesh radio. We present a Monte Carlo comparison of the Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation (TSNFA) detector against four classical comparators drawn from the radar Constant False Alarm Rate (CFAR) family and from sequential change detection: the Lipski FFT energy detector, Cell-Averaging CFAR (CA-CFAR), Ordered-Statistic CFAR (OS-CFAR), and state-machine Cumulative Sum (CUSUM). All five detectors are implemented to fit a Cortex-M0+ class envelope, process a 1-D 100 Hz time series in 128-sample frames, and use temporal reference windows in place of the spatial reference cells of conventional radar CFAR. Across a factorial set of four configurations (10 and 50 nodes; 12 dB and 18 dB SNR), each replicated five times over 24 hours, TSNFA achieves 99.97 to 100% event detection rate with 100% event precision and zero false-positive clusters per node. The classical comparators each succeed on one quality dimension and fail on another. Lipski FFT (k = 3), CA-CFAR, and OS-CFAR all maintain near-perfect detection rate but with event precision below 3% and per-node bandwidth between 145 kB/h and 1.2 MB/h. CA-CFAR and OS-CFAR are indistinguishable in false-alarm performance, both saturating the same broadband-statistic failure mode. CUSUM shows an SNR-dependent detection-rate drop from about 70% at 18 dB to 51% at 12 dB. TSNFA is the only algorithm tested that simultaneously achieves high detection rate, high precision, and low per-node bandwidth.

</details>


### [32] [Near-optimal Online Traffic Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16187)
*Arvin Ghavidel,Pooria Namyar,Nikolai Matni,Walter Willinger,Ramesh Govindan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了OnlineTE，一种基于优化分解理论的分布式广域网流量工程系统，通过交换机分布式求解和中心协调器协作，实现了秒级响应和近最优解，性能优于现有技术一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集中式广域网流量工程系统在规模较大时存在性能次优、对需求变化或故障响应缓慢（需数分钟）的问题。

Method: 利用优化分解理论设计分布式求解器。每个交换机独立求解部分优化问题，中心协调器协调进度；交换机检测到变化即可触发重优化。支持路径-based MLU和Max-flow问题，并提出了边缘-based TE新方法。

Result: 在750节点WAN拓扑测试床上，性能优于现有技术一个数量级；计算需求低于现代交换机能力，支持大规模部署。

Conclusion: OnlineTE实现了对网络变化的秒级近最优响应，显著提升了TE系统的可扩展性和资源利用效率，为流量工程提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Most deployed WAN Traffic Engineering (TE) systems use a logically centralized controller that periodically gathers traffic demands, runs a TE optimization or heuristic, and then programs the network. At scale, these solutions can be sub-optimal, and can take minutes to react to demand changes or failures. In this paper, we introduce OnlineTE, a system that reacts immediately to demand changes and failures, and delivers near-optimal solutions within seconds of a change. OnlineTE builds on the theory of optimization decomposition to devise scalable, near-optimal, distributed TE solvers for path-based MLU and Max-flow problems. In OnlineTE, each switch solves part of the optimization, and a central coordinator orchestrates the progress of the switches. As such, a switch can trigger a re-optimization as soon as it notices a demand change or failure, enabling high reactivity. OnlineTE scales to large WANs, and its compute requirements are well below the capabilities of modern WAN switches. It also enables a new opportunity, edge-based TE, which can utilize resources more efficiently than today's path-based approaches. On a testbed emulation of a 750-node WAN topology, OnlineTE can outperform the state-of-the-art by up to an order of magnitude.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [33] [AgentStop: Terminating Local AI Agents Early to Save Energy in Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15206)
*Dzung Pham,Kleomenis Katevas,Ali Shahin Shamsabadi,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentStop，一个轻量级效率监督器，通过预测并提前终止不太可能成功的智能体轨迹，在本地部署的LLM智能体上减少15-20%的能源浪费，同时将任务性能损失控制在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然本地部署LLM智能体可以保护隐私并消除API费用，但智能体工作流（如迭代推理、工具使用和失败重试）比典型LLM交互消耗更多资源，导致GPU功耗、温度和电池消耗显著增加，存在计算效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出AgentStop，一个轻量级效率监督器，利用低成本执行信号（如token级别的对数概率）预测不太可能成功的智能体轨迹，并提前终止这些低效的执行路径。

Result: 实验测量表明，智能体执行相比单次推理工作负载会增加GPU功耗、温度和电池消耗；AgentStop在具有挑战性的基于网络的问答和编程基准测试中，能够减少15-20%的能源浪费，且任务性能损失小于5%。

Conclusion: 预测性提前终止是实现在用户设备上可持续、保护隐私的LLM智能体的实用机制，为本地部署智能体的效率优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate complex, multi-step tasks such as coding or web-based question answering. While remote, cloud-based agents offer scalability and ease of deployment, they raise privacy concerns, depend on network connectivity, and incur recurring API costs. Deploying agents locally on user devices mitigates these issues by preserving data privacy and eliminating usage-based fees. However, agentic workflows are far more resource-intensive than typical LLM interactions. Iterative reasoning, tool use, and failure retries substantially increase token consumption, often expending significant compute without successfully completing tasks.
  In this work, we investigate the time, token, and energy overhead of locally deployed LLM-based agents on consumer hardware. Our measurements show that agentic execution increases GPU power draw, temperature, and battery drain compared to single-inference workloads. To address this inefficiency, we introduce AgentStop, a lightweight efficiency supervisor that predicts and preemptively terminates trajectories unlikely to succeed. Leveraging low-cost execution signals, such as token-level log probabilities, AgentStop can reduce wasted energy by 15-20% with minimal impact on task performance (<5% utility drop) for challenging web-based question answering and coding benchmarks. These findings position predictive early termination as a practical mechanism for enabling sustainable, privacy-preserving LLM agents on user devices. Our project code and data are available at https://github.com/brave-experiments/AgentStop.

</details>


### [34] [TeamTR: Trust-Region Fine-Tuning for Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15207)
*Yi Xie,Siao Liu,Falong Fan,Yuanqi Yao,Yue Zhao,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体LLM系统中共享上下文序列微调存在的复合占用偏移问题，提出了TeamTR框架，通过信任区域优化和重采样策略，平均性能提升7.1%，有效缓解了协调退步问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在复杂推理任务中表现不佳，往往不如单模型基线。研究发现，共享上下文团队的序列微调存在结构性失效模式：更新一个智能体会改变团队的上下文分布，当后续更新在缓存的rollouts上评估时，这种失配会产生累积效应，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出TeamTR信任区域框架：(1)在每次组件更新后重新采样轨迹；(2)对每个智能体强制执行散度控制；(3)提供严格的每次更新和每个阶段的改进下界。该方法将陈旧占用评估的二次惩罚降低为线性扩展。

Result: 实验表明TeamTR平均比单智能体和序列基线性能提升7.1%，有效缓解了协调退步问题，并支持即插即用的组件替换。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 形式化了复合占用偏移问题，证明了陈旧占用评估的惩罚随智能体数量二次增长，而中间占用评估将其降为线性增长。TeamTR框架成功解决了多智能体LLM系统的序列微调难题，为多智能体协作优化提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems have shown promise for complex reasoning, yet recent evaluations reveal they often underperform single-model baselines. We identify a structural failure mode in sequential fine-tuning of shared-context teams: updating one agent shifts the team's context distribution, and when subsequent updates are evaluated on cached rollouts, this mismatch compounds. We formalize this as the compounding occupancy shift and prove that stale-occupancy evaluation incurs a penalty that scales quadratically with the number of agents. In contrast, intermediate-occupancy evaluation reduces this to linear scaling. We propose TeamTR, a trust-region framework that resamples trajectories after each component update and enforces per-agent divergence control, yielding rigorous per-update and per-stage improvement lower bounds. Experiments show that TeamTR outperforms single-agent and sequential baselines with 7.1% on average, mitigates coordination regressions, and supports plug-and-play component replacement. Code is available at https://github.com/Yydc/TeamTR.

</details>


### [35] [Quantization Undoes Alignment: Bias Emergence in Compressed LLMs Across Models and Precision Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15208)
*Plawan Kumar Rath,Rahul Maliakkal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模实验发现，低比特量化（尤其是3-bit）会导致大语言模型产生新的偏见行为，而这种安全风险无法被困惑度等常规质量指标检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM量化研究通常只比较全精度与单一量化版本，依赖聚合偏见指标，且仅评估单一模型家族，无法区分渐进式退化与阈值依赖的安全故障。量化压缩对模型质量（特别是公平性）的影响尚不明确。

Method: 对三个指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Phi-3.5-mini）进行受控实证研究，在五个精度级别（BF16至3-bit）下，使用5个随机种子评估12,148个BBQ偏见基准测试项目，共产生911,100条推理记录，并通过逻辑回归验证剂量-反应模式。

Result: 3-bit量化导致6-21%原本无偏见的项目产生新的刻板印象行为，呈现明显的剂量-反应模式；模型选择"未知"答案的意愿下降17.4%。关键发现是：8-bit时困惑度增加不足0.5%，4-bit时增加不足3%，但4-bit时已有2.5-5.6%的项目出现新偏见，说明聚合评估指标系统性地遗漏了公平性关键的退化。

Conclusion: 聚合评估指标无法有效捕捉量化过程中的公平性退化，突显了在部署前需要建立明确测试偏见涌现的质量感知压缩协议的必要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models are routinely compressed via post-training quantization to reduce inference costs and memory footprint for cloud and edge deployment, yet the impact of this compression on model quality remains poorly understood. Existing studies typically compare only two conditions (full-precision vs. a single quantized variant), rely on aggregate bias metrics, and evaluate a single model family, making it impossible to distinguish gradual degradation from threshold-dependent safety failures. We conduct a controlled empirical study of three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Phi-3.5-mini) at five precision levels (BF16 through 3-bit) on 12,148 BBQ bias benchmark items across 5 random seeds, totaling 911,100 inference records. Our results reveal that 3-bit quantization causes 6-21% of previously unbiased items to develop new stereotypical behaviors, following a clear dose-response pattern confirmed via logistic regression, while models' willingness to select "unknown" answers declines by 17.4%. Crucially, these item-level changes are invisible to standard quality metrics: perplexity increases by less than 0.5% at 8-bit and under 3% at 4-bit across all three models, yet 2.5-5.6% of items already develop new biases at 4-bit. These findings demonstrate that aggregate evaluation metrics systematically miss fairness-critical degradation, underscoring the need for quality-aware compression protocols that explicitly test for bias emergence before deployment.

</details>


### [36] [Mask-Morph Graph U-Net: A Generalisable Mesh-Based Surrogate for Crashworthiness Field Prediction under Large Geometric Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15231)
*Haoran Li,Tobias Lehrer,Yingxue Zhao,Haosu Zhou,Philipp Stocker,Tobias Pfaff,Nan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mask-Morph Graph U-Net (MMGUNet)方法，通过图变形和节点掩码策略解决了层级图神经网络在网格仿真中泛化性差和训练效率低的问题，显著提升了非线性碰撞仿真代理模型的准确性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性有限元碰撞仿真精度高但计算成本昂贵，限制了其在迭代设计优化中的应用。基于图神经网络(GNN)的机器学习代理模型提供了更快的替代方案。然而，现有的消息传递GNN和非共享边特定聚合层之间存在权衡：前者泛化性强但精度有限，后者精度高但需要固定图连接性，限制了泛化能力。层级Graph U-Net架构使用边特定下采样和上采样层，同样面临固定粗糙图连接性的限制。

Method: 提出了Mask-Morph Graph U-Net (MMGUNet)方法，包含两个关键创新：(1)图变形策略：使用特征对齐的重心参数化将粗化图层级变形到每个输入网格，在保持固定粗糙图连接性的同时改善空间对应关系，然后构建跨图边缘；(2)两阶段训练策略：首先在监督预训练阶段应用节点掩码，然后在参数高效微调阶段冻结高参数的边特定层。

Result: 在分布内、分布外和跨组件迁移三种设置下进行了评估，使用平均欧氏距离和最大侵入百分比误差作为指标。结果显示：粗图变形相比固定粗图基准提升了测试精度；掩码监督预训练减少了训练-测试差异，并在迁移学习中提高了数据效率；提出的模型相比外部基线取得了更低的预测误差。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一条通向可重用、数据高效的基于网格的代理建模的实用路径，适用于耐撞性设计探索。通过图变形和节点掩码的创新组合，成功解决了层级图神经网络在网格仿真中精度与泛化性的权衡问题。

Abstract: Nonlinear finite element crash simulations are accurate but computationally expensive, limiting their use in iterative design optimisation. Machine-learning surrogate models based on graph neural networks (GNNs) offer a faster alternative. Message-passing GNNs are widely used for mesh simulation, and their shared node and edge update functions are relatively generalisable across varying graph structures. By contrast, non-shareable edge-specific aggregation layers can capture nonlinear relationships more accurately but usually require fixed graph connectivity, which limits generalisability. This paper presents Mask-Morph Graph U-Net (MMGUNet), a practical approach to addressing the limitation of hierarchical Graph U-Net architectures that use edge-specific downsampling and upsampling layers. Fixed coarse graph connectivity is required for edge-specific layers. To retain this while improving spatial correspondence, the proposed method morphs the coarsened graph hierarchy to each input mesh using feature-aligned barycentric parameterisation before constructing cross-graph edges. It further applies node masking during supervised pretraining, followed by parameter-efficient fine-tuning in which high-parameter edge-specific layers are frozen. The proposed approach is evaluated in in-distribution, out-of-distribution, and cross-component transfer settings using mean Euclidean distance and maximum intrusion percentage error. Results show that coarse-graph morphing improves test accuracy relative to a fixed-coarse-graph baseline, while masked supervised pretraining reduces the train-test discrepancy and improves data efficiency during transfer. The proposed model also achieves lower prediction error compared with external baselines. These results demonstrate a practical route toward reusable, data-efficient mesh-based surrogate modelling for crashworthiness design exploration.

</details>


### [37] [MuteBench: Modality Unavailability Tolerance Evaluation for Incomplete Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15235)
*Wugeng Zheng,Ziwen Kan,Tianlong Chen,Chen Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MuteBench基准，系统评估多模态临床AI在模态缺失和模态内缺失两种故障模式下的鲁棒性，发现架构家族是鲁棒性的最强预测因子，并提供了架构选择与设计的实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生理数据驱动的临床AI系统在实际应用中常面临传感器故障，主要表现为整通道缺失的模态缺失和连续时间段丢失的模态内缺失两种模式。然而，现有研究缺乏一个能同时评估多种融合架构在两种故障模式下、不同严重程度及多临床数据集上表现的基准，这阻碍了对系统鲁棒性的深入理解和改进。

Method: 构建了MuteBench基准测试框架，涵盖7个临床领域的9个数据集、6种融合架构、2种缺失数据模式，样本量超过125,000个。通过控制缺失严重程度，系统评估不同融合架构在模态缺失和模态内缺失下的表现，分析架构类型、参数量、通道数、序列长度等因素对鲁棒性的影响，并在PTB-XL数据集上进行了扩散模型插补的案例研究。

Result: 发现架构家族是鲁棒性的最强预测因子，影响力超过参数量。通道独立模型对模态缺失容忍度高，但对模态内缺失（尤其是短序列）敏感。课程模态丢弃仅在训练使用的最大丢弃率范围内提供可靠保护。通道数、序列长度和模态对齐共同决定哪种故障模式威胁更大。扩散模型插补能改善模态内缺失下的下游分类性能，对专家路由对受损输入最敏感的模型增益最大。

Conclusion: MuteBench为临床AI实践者提供了关于现有架构选择和未来鲁棒多模态融合方法设计的具体指导，填补了该领域系统评估的空白，展示了扩散模型插补的潜力，但跨数据集的广泛验证仍是开放研究方向。

Abstract: Multimodal physiological data powers clinical AI systems from intensive care units to wearable devices, but sensors routinely fail in practice. Two failure modes are common: modality missing, where an entire channel is absent, and within-modality missing, where a contiguous time segment is lost. No existing benchmark evaluates multiple fusion architectures under both failure modes at controlled severity levels across diverse clinical datasets. We present MuteBench, a benchmark covering 9 datasets from 7 clinical domains, 6 fusion architectures, and 2 missing-data modes over 125,000 samples. Through this benchmark, we find that architecture family is the strongest predictor of robustness, outweighing parameter count. Channel-independent models tolerate modality missing well but can be sensitive to within-modality missing, especially on short sequences. Curriculum modality dropout protects reliably only up to the maximum dropout rate used in training. We also find that channel count, sequence length, and modality alignment jointly determine which failure mode poses the greater threat. Finally, a PTB-XL case study suggests that diffusion-based imputation can improve downstream classification under within-modality missing, with the largest gains for models whose expert routing is most sensitive to corrupted inputs, though broader validation across datasets remains an open direction. MuteBench provides practitioners with concrete guidance for both selecting existing architectures and informing the design of future robust multimodal fusion methods.

</details>


### [38] [Reducing the Safety Tax in LLM Safety Alignment with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15239)
*Yu Fu,Longxuan Yu,Haz Sameen Shahgir,Zhipeng Wei,Hui Liu,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yue Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OPSA方法（在线策略自蒸馏安全对齐），通过在线策略训练和密集的逐Token KL监督，解决了离线策略训练不匹配问题，在保持推理能力的同时显著提升安全性，尤其在小型模型上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法存在“安全税”问题，即提升安全性的同时牺牲推理能力。除了已知的分布不匹配问题外，作者识别出第二个原因：离线策略训练不匹配。传统方法使用人类、外部模型或固定自生成轨迹的安全演示进行训练，而非从模型自身策略采样的轨迹，导致性能损失。

Method: 提出OPSA（On-Policy Self-distillation for safety Alignment）方法：1）模型生成自己的轨迹（rollouts）；2）从一个冻结的、具有特权安全上下文的教师模型获得密集的逐Token KL监督；3）引入“教师翻转率”指标来衡量特权上下文将不安全响应转换为安全响应的频率；4）使用该信号搜索能激活潜在安全推理而非仅产生安全表象的上下文。

Result: 在两个推理模型家族和五个模型规模上验证：1）OPSA在匹配数据和全参数微调条件下，比离线策略自蒸馏和外部教师蒸馏获得更强的安全-推理权衡；2）小型模型收益最大（R1-Distill-1.5B提升8.85分，Qwen3-0.6B提升5.49分）；3）收益在不同训练集规模和自适应越狱评估中持续存在；4）Token级分析显示OPSA将更新集中在早期合规决策Token附近。

Conclusion: OPSA通过在线策略训练有效减少了安全税，为安全对齐提供了新机制：在早期合规决策Token附近集中更新，从而在提升安全性的同时保留通用推理能力。该方法为构建既安全又具备强推理能力的模型提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Safety alignment often improves robustness to harmful queries at the cost of reasoning ability, a tradeoff known as the safety tax. A common cause is distributional mismatch: supervised fine-tuning trains the target model on safety demonstrations produced by humans, external models, or fixed self-generated traces, rather than on trajectories sampled from its own policy. We identify off-policy training mismatch as a second source of this tax and study on-policy self-distillation for safety alignment, which we call OPSA. The model generates its own rollouts and receives dense per-token KL supervision from a frozen teacher copy of itself conditioned on a privileged safety context. Because this teacher must be safer than the sampled student trajectory, we introduce \emph{teacher flip rate}: a criterion that measures how often a privileged context converts unsafe responses into safe ones. We use this signal to search for contexts that activate latent safety reasoning rather than merely elicit safe-looking demonstrations. Across two reasoning-model families and five model scales, OPSA achieves a stronger safety--reasoning tradeoff than off-policy self-distillation and external-teacher distillation under matched data and full-parameter fine-tuning, with the largest gains on smaller models (+8.85 points on R1-Distill-1.5B and +5.49 points on Qwen3-0.6B). The gains persist across training-set sizes and adaptive jailbreak evaluations. Token-level analyses further show that OPSA concentrates updates near early compliance-decision tokens, providing a mechanism for improving safety while preserving general reasoning.

</details>


### [39] [Logical Grammar Induction via Graph Kolmogorov Complexity: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Self-Healing Clinical Data Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15242)
*Abolfazl Zarghani,Amir Malekesfandiari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Logic-GNN框架，通过将临床记录视为潜在逻辑博弈支配的“私有语言”，利用图最小描述长度识别“语法违规”来实现异常检测，在区分医疗极值与数据错误方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计异常检测方法难以区分医疗信息系统（HIS）中人为数据录入错误与合法的临床极值情况，导致系统可靠性受损。

Method: 提出Logic-GNN神经符号框架，将临床记录建模为结构化的“私有语言”；融合时序图神经网络（TGNN）与图柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂性，诱导出代表医疗交互底层逻辑的符号语法；将异常定义为导致临床图最小描述长度（MDL）显著扩展的“语法违规”。

Result: 在Sina System数据集（超过200万条记录）上，Logic-GNN实现0.94的F1分数，在区分危及生命的医疗离群值与数据损坏方面，性能比最先进基线高12%。

Conclusion: Logic-GNN有效提升了HIS异常检测的准确性，并引入自愈机制实时建议逻辑修正以维护数据完整性，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The reliability of Healthcare Information Systems (HIS) is frequently compromised by human-induced data entry errors, which existing statistical anomaly detection methods fail to distinguish from legitimate clinical extremes. This paper proposes Logic-GNN, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that treats clinical records as a structured ``private language'' governed by latent logical games. By integrating Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNN) with Graph Kolmogorov Complexity, we induce a symbolic grammar that represents the underlying logic of medical interactions. We define anomalies as ``grammatical violations'' that cause a significant expansion in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) of the clinical graph. Evaluated on the Sina System dataset (2M+ records), Logic-GNN achieves an F1-score of 0.94, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by 12\% in distinguishing between life-threatening medical outliers and data corruption. Our approach introduces a self-healing mechanism that suggests logical corrections to maintain data integrity in real-time HIS environments.

</details>

*Ziyu Xu,Zijian Zhang,Liang Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Liang Wang*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: When reliable target structures are unavailable at scale or phenotypes arise from dysregulated pathways, transcriptomic perturbations provide a system-level functional readout for drug action. In this work, we formalize \emph{Transcriptome-based Drug Design (TBDD)} as a generative inverse problem: designing drug molecules conditioned on desired transcriptomic state transitions. We analyze the inherently ill-posed nature of this task, which is further complicated by the profound domain gap between biology and chemistry and by the sparsity of transcriptomic signals. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{\themodel{}} (A \textbf{C}ell\textbf{U}lar \textbf{R}esponse \textbf{E}ngine), a multi-resolution transcriptome-guided diffusion framework. \themodel{} features a specialized \textbf{Transcriptome Perturbation Functional Feature Extractor (TFE)} that (1) distills function-oriented perturbation embeddings from pre/post states, (2) aligns these signatures to dual chemical views to bridge the cross-modal gap, and (3) performs heterogeneity-aware aggregation to extract robust state-specific signals from noisy transcriptomic data. Extensive evaluations on both standard benchmarks and rigorous out-of-distribution protocols demonstrate that \themodel{} consistently outperforms strong baselines in structural quality and functional consistency. Furthermore, we validate its practical utility via a zero-shot gene-inhibitor design task, highlighting the potential of phenotype-driven generative discovery.

</details>
