Result: DiMS采样器在理论上保证能够精确地从最小值水平集采样，并且通过物理启发的超参数可以灵活控制探索能力。在贝叶斯推断的不确定性量化任务中，相比已有方法展现出更优越的性能表现。

Conclusion: DiMS提供了一种物理启发的、理论可靠的框架，用于神经网络重参数化不变解的采样问题，有效平衡了采样精度与探索能力，为贝叶斯深度学习中的不确定性量化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The minima of modern neural network loss functions are typically not isolated, rather they form connected components of reparameterization invariant solutions on the training data. Analytically characterizing these solutions is a hard problem, but sampling approaches are feasible. By construction, existing methods either spread over low-loss regions, and thus do not sample reparameterization invariant solutions exactly, or are inherently local, which limits exploration of other minima valleys. We propose sampling such reparameterization invariant models using a dynamical system based on kinetic energy, subject to a gravitational pull and a friction term that dissipates energy from the system. Our proposed sampler, DiMS, is guaranteed to sample exactly from the minimum level sets and depends on physically motivated hyperparameters which allows control over the exploration capabilities of the sampler. We consider uncertainty quantification in Bayesian inference as the motivating problem and observe improved performance compared to previously proposed approaches.

</details>


### [77] [DrugSAGE:Self-evolving Agent Experience for Efficient State-of-the-Art Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15461)
*Yikun Zhang,Xiwei Cheng,Tianyu Liu,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SAGE框架，通过跨任务记忆机制积累和复用经验，显著提升了药物发现中SOTA预测模型的构建效率，在单任务和跨任务评估中均优于现有SOTA智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在寻找SOTA解决方案时虽然有效，但无法保留搜索过程中积累的经验，导致每次新任务都需要支付完整的搜索成本，效率低下。

Method: 提出SAGE（Self-evolving Agent Experience）框架，维护一个跨任务记忆系统，包含：已验证技能库、有效策略的统计证据、以及常见错误及其修复方案的记录。该框架能够直接迁移有效方案或在零测试时搜索模式下工作。

Result: 在33个分子属性预测任务的单任务设置中，SAGE在九个SOTA智能体中排名第一。在跨任务评估中，利用16个小任务积累的记忆，SAGE在17个保留任务上达到0.935的平均归一化分数，在零测试时搜索机制下比所有基线智能体高出10-30%。

Conclusion: 研究表明跨任务记忆机制对于药物发现中高效的SOTA模型开发具有显著优势，能够有效降低搜索成本并提升模型性能。

Abstract: Building state-of-the-art (SOTA) predictive models for drug discovery requires expensive search over tools, architectures, and training strategies. Current LLM-based agents can find SOTA solutions through extensive trial and error, but they do not retain the experience accumulated along the way and therefore pay the full search cost on every new task. We propose \method (Self-evolving Agent Experience), a framework that accumulates and reuses experience across tasks to build SOTA drug discovery models efficiently. \method maintains a cross-task memory of verified skills, statistical evidence about effective strategies, and a record of recurring errors and their fixes. In some cases, \method transfers a working solution directly without test-time search. In 33 molecular property prediction tasks, \method ranks first among nine SOTA agents in a single-task setting. With memory accumulated from 16 smaller tasks, \method achieves an averaged normalized score of 0.935 on 17 held-out tasks in a cross-task evaluation setting and outperforms all baseline agents by 10-30\% in a zero-test-time search regime. In summary, our work shows the advantage of cross-task memory for efficient SOTA model development in drug discovery.

</details>


### [78] [Layer-wise Derivative Controlled Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15463)
*Rowan Martnishn,Sean Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChainzRule (CR)神经网络架构，通过多项式引擎和差分正则化(DREG)实现高精度、硬件效率和功能稳定性的协同优化，在减少15.5倍参数量的同时降低23.1%的梯度波动，并在Yelp Full数据集上达到70.17%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型在追求高精度时常导致硬件效率低下和功能不稳定，表现为输入微小变化引起输出剧烈波动，这对敏感环境的实际部署构成严重威胁。现有方法通常通过全局、粗粒度的Lipschitz常数约束来限制模型敏感性，但这会损害模型的表达能力，无法平衡准确性、效率和稳定性这三个相互冲突的目标。

Method: 提出ChainzRule架构，核心包含两个创新：1) 用多项式引擎替代标准的分段线性激活函数；2) 引入差分正则化(DREG)对中间导数进行定向正则化。DREG不同于传统全局约束方法，它通过针对性约束中间导数来抑制极端敏感性，同时保留多项式引擎固有的强大表达能力，从而将梯度感知嵌入到架构本身。

Result: 在"公平对决"基准测试中，ChainzRule以15.5倍更少的参数量超越标准模型；在MNIST数据集上将峰值梯度波动平均降低23.1%，实现更平滑、可预测的流形；在Yelp Full序数回归任务中，结合显式DREG正则化达到70.17%的准确率，验证了导数感知正则化与竞争性能的兼容性。

Conclusion: ChainzRule证明了稳定性与准确性不必是相互竞争的目标。通过DREG将梯度感知嵌入架构设计，可以实现高精度、硬件效率和功能稳定性的和谐统一，为实际敏感环境中的模型部署提供了可行的解决方案，挑战了传统观点中模型性能与稳定性必须权衡取舍的假设。

Abstract: As machine learning models grow in complexity, they increasingly struggle with three conflicting demands: the need for high accuracy, the requirement for hardware efficiency, and the necessity of functional stability. Traditional architectures often achieve performance at the expense of spiky or unpredictable behavior, where small changes in input lead to massive swings in output -- a critical flaw for real-world deployment in sensitive environments. This paper introduces ChainzRule (CR), a novel neural architecture designed to harmonize these competing goals. ChainzRule replaces standard piecewise-linear activations with a Polynomial Engine governed by Differential Regularization (DREG). Unlike traditional methods that impose global, coarse-grained constraints on a model's Lipschitz constant, DREG acts as a targeted regularization on intermediate derivatives. This approach suppresses extreme sensitivity without attenuating the representational power inherent in the Polynomial Engine. In head-to-head "Fair Fight" benchmarks, ChainzRule outperformed standard models while using 15.5x fewer parameters. On the MNIST dataset, it reduced peak gradient volatility by an average of 23.1%, ensuring a smoother and more predictable manifold. On Yelp Full ordinal regression under explicit DREG regularization, ChainzRule achieves 70.17% accuracy, validating that derivative-aware regularization is compatible with competitive performance on realistic tasks. By embedding gradient awareness into the architecture via DREG, ChainzRule demonstrates that stability and accuracy need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [79] [GRLO: Towards Generalizable Reinforcement Learning in Open-Ended Environments from Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15464)
*Shangjian Yin,Yu Fu,Yue Dong,Zhouxing Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRLO方法，通过少量开放环境交互学习RLHF，实现对下游任务（数学推理、代码生成）的隐式迁移，以极低的数据和计算成本（仅需5K提示词和22.7 GPU小时）获得显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习后训练分为RLHF和RLVR两种范式，其中RLVR在领域特定任务上效果显著但需要大量GPU计算资源，成为广泛应用的障碍。作者希望研究从少量开放环境交互中学习RLHF的泛化能力，探索其是否会话能力能否隐式迁移到数学推理和代码生成等下游任务，以降低训练成本。

Method: 提出GRLO（Generalized Reinforcement Learning from Open-ended environments）方法，在Qwen3-4B-Base骨干网络上进行实验。该方法从少量开放环境交互中从头学习RLHF，仅使用5K提示词，研究模型显式获得的会话能力能否隐式迁移到数学推理和代码生成等下游任务。

Result: GRLO将所有领域的平均性能从24.1提升至63.1，仅需5K提示词和22.7 GPU小时，相比强域内RLVR基线减少约46倍数据和68倍计算量。所得模型甚至与Qwen官方发布的后训练模型（训练成本高得多）具有竞争力。后续域内RLVR阶段仅在较难的竞赛数学基准上带来选择性增益。

Conclusion: GRLO提供了一种简单高效的方案，用于构建具有广泛能力的后训练模型。研究表明从开放环境中学习的RLHF具有良好的泛化能力，能够以极低的成本获得与传统方法相当甚至更好的性能，为降低大语言模型后训练的计算门槛提供了新思路。

Abstract: Post-training has become a crucial step for unlocking the capabilities of large language models, with reinforcement learning (RL) emerging as a critical paradigm. Recent RL-based post-training has increasingly split into two paradigms: reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), which optimizes models using human preference signals in target domains, and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR), which operates in verifier-backed environments. The latter has dominated recent reasoning-oriented post-training because it delivers stronger gains and higher efficiency on domain-specific tasks (e.g., reasoning). However, although in-domain RL training achieves promising performance, it still requires a substantial amount of GPU compute, which remains a major barrier to broad adoption. In this work, we study the generalization ability of RLHF learned from scratch from a small set of interactions in open-ended environments, and investigate whether the conversational abilities it explicitly acquires can implicitly transfer to downstream tasks such as mathematical reasoning and code generation, namely GRLO. Specifically, on Qwen3-4B-Base backbone, GRLO improves the average performance across all domains from 24.1 to 63.1 with only 5K prompts and 22.7 GPU hours, requiring about $46\times$ less data and $68\times$ less compute than a strong in-domain RLVR baseline. The resulting model is even competitive with Qwen's released post-trained models which required a much larger training cost. Notably, a subsequent in-domain RLVR stage brings only selective gains, mainly on harder competition-math benchmarks. We hope GRLO offers a simple and efficient recipe for building broadly capable post-trained models. Our code and data will be available at: \href{https://github.com/SJY8460/GRLO}{https://github.com/SJY8460/GRLO}.

</details>


### [80] [Toward World Modeling of Physiological Signals with Chaos-Theoretic Balancing and Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15465)
*Yunfei Luo,Xi Chen,Yuliang Chen,Lanshuang Zhang,Md Mofijul Islam,Siwei Zhao,Peter Kotanko,Subhasis Dasgupta,Andrew Campbell,Rakesh Malhotra,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NormWear-2，一种针对生理信号和临床干预变量的世界模型，通过混沌理论平衡的数据集预训练，在跨多个时间尺度和异质干预条件的长期信号预测任务中显著超越现有时间序列基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理信号建模研究主要关注分类、事件预测或短期下一步预测等静态任务，而长期信号级预测以及生理信号的预测性质仍未被充分探索；生理时间序列信号反映了人体复杂的多尺度动态过程，需要能够建模其联合时间演化的方法。

Method: 提出NormWear-2世界模型，将多变量生理信号和临床干预变量编码到共享潜在空间，并将其联合时间演化建模为动力系统；结合先验预训练知识的推理（直觉）与即时非参数潜在状态转换适应（洞察），实现基于异质临床干预条件的跨多时间尺度一致性预测；预训练阶段发现通过混沌理论平衡动态机制多样性可获得更鲁棒的表征。

Result: 在涵盖日常生活、即时护理和临床环境（包括健身计划、血液透析、糖尿病管理和手术监测）的多样化真实世界生理数据集上进行评估，包含8,026名受试者的记录，研究持续时间从高分辨率信号数据的3.2小时到纵向临床生物标志物追踪的2.3年；NormWear-2在时间、频率和潜在表征域上均达到最佳整体预测性能，显著优于最先进的时间序列基础模型，同时保持有竞争力的下游表征质量；较小的平衡语料库性能超过两倍大小的语料库，并能捕捉分岔机制。

Conclusion: NormWear-2为生理信号的通用世界模型迈出了一步，证明了通过混沌理论平衡的数据选择策略可以有效提升模型性能，为长期生理信号预测和多尺度动态建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Physiological time series signals reflect complex, multi-scale dynamical processes of the human body. Existing modeling studies focus on static tasks such as classification, event forecasting, or short-horizon next step prediction, while long-horizon signal-level forecasting and predictive nature of physiological signals remain underexplored. We introduce NormWear-2, a world model that encodes both multivariate physiological signals and clinical intervention variables into a shared latent space and models their joint temporal evolution as a dynamical system. Our approach combines inference from prior pre-trained knowledge (intuition) with instant non-parametric latent state transition adaptation (insight), enabling coherent forecasting across multiple temporal scales, conditioned on heterogeneous clinical interventions. During the pretraining phase, we find that chaos-theoretic balancing of dynamical regime diversity yields more robust representations, with a smaller balanced corpus outperforming one twice its size and capturing bifurcation regimes. We evaluate the world model performance across diverse real-world physiological datasets spanning heterogeneous temporal resolutions and intervention regimes, covering daily life, point-of-care, and clinical settings, including fitness planning, hemodialysis, diabetes management, and surgical monitoring. These evaluation datasets comprise records from 8,026 subjects, spanning study durations from 3.2 hours for high-resolution signal data to 2.3 years for longitudinal clinical biomarker tracking. NormWear-2 achieves the best overall forecasting performance across time, frequency, and latent representation domains, with significant improvements over state-of-the-art time series foundation models, while maintaining competitive downstream representation quality, providing a step toward general-purpose world models for physiological signals.

</details>


### [81] [Njord: A Probabilistic Graph Neural Network for Ensemble Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15470)
*Daniel Holmberg,Joel Oskarsson,Erik Larsson,Fredrik Lindsten,Teemu Roos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Njord，一个概率性的数据驱动海洋预测模型，结合深度潜变量框架和图神经网络架构，能够高效生成集合预报并提供不确定性估计，在全球和区域尺度上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋动力学具有内在的混沌特性，但现有的机器学习海洋模型仅能产生确定性预报，无法捕捉预测的不确定性。为了解决这一局限性，需要开发能够进行概率预测的模型。

Method: 提出了Njord模型，结合深度潜变量框架与图神经网络（GNN）架构，实现了在单次前向传播中对每个预报步骤进行采样。为了适应大规模海洋网格，引入了K-means聚类网格以适应不规则的海面几何形状。

Result: 在全球0.25°分辨率和波罗的海2 km分辨率的区域应用中，Njord相比确定性机器学习基线表现优异。在全球OceanBench基准测试中，在上层海洋变量上平均误差最低，尤其在表层温度预测上改进最大，同时能从采样的集合预报中提供不确定性估计。

Conclusion: Njord成功实现了概率性海洋预报，解决了现有模型确定性输出的局限，通过生成集合预报提供了不确定性量化能力，并在全球和区域预测任务中均取得了领先的预测精度。

Abstract: Ocean dynamics are inherently chaotic, yet existing machine learning ocean models produce only deterministic forecasts. We introduce Njord, a probabilistic data-driven model for ocean forecasting, applicable to both global and regional domains. Njord combines a deep latent variable framework with a graph neural network architecture, enabling sampling each forecast step in a single forward pass. We apply Njord globally at 0.25° resolution and regionally to the Baltic Sea at 2 km resolution. To scale to these large ocean grids we introduce K-means cluster meshes that adapt to irregular sea surface geometry. Experiments demonstrate strong performance on both domains compared to deterministic machine learning baselines, while also providing uncertainty estimates from the sampled ensemble forecasts. On the global OceanBench benchmark, Njord achieves the lowest errors on average across upper-ocean variables when evaluated against real-world observations, with the largest improvements in surface temperature prediction.

</details>


### [82] [Learning Normalized Energy Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15487)
*Nicolas Zilberstein,Santiago Segarra,Eero Simoncelli,Florentin Guth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于能量的去噪模型，通过协方差正则化实现了显式的归一化后验密度计算，解决了扩散模型在逆问题中隐式表示和采样偏差的局限性，并引入了自适应采样、无偏校正和盲退化估计等新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成扩散模型在解决成像逆问题时存在两个关键局限：先验密度是隐式表示的，以及依赖的似然近似会引入采样偏差。这些限制阻碍了模型在逆问题中的更广泛应用和性能提升。

Method: 提出一种新的基于能量的模型，通过带有协方差正则化项的去噪任务进行训练，以强制不同测量条件之间的一致性。该训练好的模型可直接计算归一化的后验密度，无需针对不同线性逆问题进行额外重训练或微调。

Result: 该方法在ImageNet、CelebA和AFHQ等多个数据集以及修复（inpainting）、去模糊（deblurring）等任务上进行了验证，显示出与现有基准方法相比具有竞争力或更优的性能，并实现了能量引导的自适应采样、无偏Metropolis-Hastings校正和盲退化算子估计等新功能。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功解决了扩散模型在逆问题中的核心局限，在保留扩散模型强大生成能力的同时，通过显式能量表示解锁了自适应采样、无偏推断和盲估计等关键能力，为成像逆问题提供了更灵活且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models can provide powerful prior probability models for inverse problems in imaging, but existing implementations suffer from two key limitations: $(i)$ the prior density is represented implicitly, and $(ii)$ they rely on likelihood approximations that introduce sampling biases. We address these challenges by introducing a new energy-based model trained for denoising with a covariance-based regularization term that enforces consistency across different measurement conditions. The trained model can compute normalized posterior densities for diverse linear inverse problems, without additional retraining or fine tuning. In addition to preserving the sampling capabilities of diffusion models, this enables previously unavailable capabilities: energy-guided adaptive sampling that adjusts schedules on-the-fly, unbiased Metropolis-Hastings correction steps, and blind estimation of the degradation operator via Bayes rule. We validate the method on multiple datasets (ImageNet, CelebA, AFHQ) and tasks (inpainting, deblurring), demonstrating competitive or superior performance to established baselines.

</details>


### [83] [SurvivalPFN: Amortizing Survival Prediction via In-Context Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15488)
*Shi-ang Qi,Vahid Balazadeh,Michael Cooper,Russell Greiner,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SurvivalPFN，一种基于先验数据拟合网络的生存分析基础模型，通过上下文学习实现单次前向传播的贝叶斯推断，无需任务特定训练，在61个数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析为处理截尾事件时间数据提供了强大框架，但从众多专业方法中选择合适估计器需要大量方法论和领域专业知识，模型选择困难且门槛高。

Method: 提出SurvivalPFN——先验数据拟合网络，在多样化、可识别、右截尾的合成数据生成过程上进行预训练；利用上下文学习对截尾观测进行贝叶斯推断摊销；推理时仅需单次前向传播，无需任务特定训练或超参数调优，避免限制性参数假设。

Result: 在大规模基准测试（61个数据集、21种方法、5个评估指标）中，SurvivalPFN取得强劲预测性能，经常优于既有生存模型；能够生成校准良好的生存分布，自适应数据集的有效复杂度。

Conclusion: SurvivalPFN为生存分析提供了有原则且实用的基础模型，具有在医疗、金融、工程等高影响力领域应用的潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Survival analysis provides a powerful statistical framework for modeling time-to-event outcomes in the presence of censoring. However, selecting an appropriate estimator from the many specialized survival approaches often requires substantial methodological and domain expertise. We introduce SurvivalPFN, a prior-data fitted network that amortizes Bayesian inference for censored observations through in-context learning. SurvivalPFN is pretrained on a diverse family of synthetic, identifiable, and right-censored data-generating processes, enabling it to amortize survival analysis in a single forward pass during inference. As a result, the model adapts to the effective complexity of each dataset without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, avoids restrictive parametric assumptions, and produces calibrated survival distributions. In a large-scale benchmark spanning 61 datasets, 21 methods, and 5 evaluation metrics, SurvivalPFN achieves strong predictive performance and often improves upon established survival models. These results suggest that SurvivalPFN offers a principled and practical foundation model for survival analysis, with potential applications in high-impact domains such as healthcare, finance, and engineering (https://github.com/rgklab/SurvivalPFN).

</details>


### [84] [Ghosted Layers: Unconstrained Activation Alignment for Recovering Layer-Pruned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15491)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Junhyuk Jo,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sunwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ghosted Layers方法，通过求解边界激活对齐问题，以闭式线性算子重建剪枝层引入的激活差异，在无需训练的情况下显著缓解大模型层剪枝导致的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层剪枝会移除Transformer解码器块，导致下一保留层接收的隐藏状态与其训练时处理的分布不匹配，从而引发严重的性能退化。现有方法在受限的算子子空间内寻求解决方案，无法最优地对齐激活。

Method: 提出Ghosted Layers，一种无需训练的恢复模块。该方法将问题建模为边界激活对齐问题，利用小规模校准集推导出闭式最优线性算子，以重建被剪枝层引入的激活差异。该解对应于对齐目标的无约束最优解，突破了现有方法在受限算子子空间上的局限。

Result: 在多种大语言模型骨干和剪枝策略上的实验表明，该方法在准确率和困惑度上均持续优于先前的无需训练基线方法，同时保留了层剪枝带来的效率提升。

Conclusion: Ghosted Layers通过理论上的最优线性算子有效解决了层剪枝后的分布不匹配问题，为训练-free的大模型压缩提供了一种高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Layer pruning removes entire Transformer decoder blocks from large language models, but introduces a mismatch between the hidden state received by the next surviving layer and the distribution it was trained to process, leading to significant performance degradation. We propose Ghosted Layers, a training-free recovery module that addresses this issue by solving a boundary activation alignment problem. Our method derives a closed-form optimal linear operator from a small calibration set to reconstruct the activation discrepancy introduced by the pruned layers. We show that this solution corresponds to the unconstrained optimum of the alignment objective, whereas existing methods are restricted to constrained solutions over limited operator subspaces. Experiments across multiple LLM backbones and pruning strategies demonstrate that our method consistently improves accuracy and perplexity over prior training-free baselines, while preserving the efficiency gains of layer pruning.

</details>


### [85] [Learning with Conflicts of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15504)
*Nischal Aryal,Arash Termehchy,Ali Vakilian,Marianne Winslett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对机器学习系统与用户间的利益冲突，提出了一个博弈论框架及可扩展算法，在保护用户免受偏见信息影响的同时最大化有益信息的获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融、社会和政治因素导致机器学习系统所有者与用户利益不一致，系统常产生有偏见的信息影响用户决策，而现有解决方案缺乏激励机制促使系统所有者实施偏见缓解协议。

Method: 提出了一个博弈论框架来建模机器学习系统与存在利益冲突的用户之间的交互，并开发了具有理论保证的可扩展算法。

Result: 所提出的算法能够最大化期望信息和行动的数量，同时最小化与机器学习系统交互中的偏见和操纵性行动。

Conclusion: 成功的解决方案必须承认机器学习系统与用户之间的利益冲突，并利用此信息保护用户免受不利影响，同时允许用户安全地从这些系统中受益。

Abstract: Financial, social, and political factors often prevent the interests of the owners of ML systems and services and their users from being perfectly aligned. ML systems often produce biased information that can influence users to make decisions that are not in their best interest. Current solution approaches require ML systems to implement protocols to mitigate their biases. However, ML system owners usually do not have any incentive to implement these protocols and often argue that it limits their freedom of expression or business. We believe that a successful solution to this problem must recognize the conflict of interest between the ML systems and their users, and use this information to protect users against information that adversely influences their decisions while allowing users to safely benefit from these systems. To this end, we propose a game-theoretic framework that models the interaction between ML systems and users with conflicts of interest. We present scalable algorithms with theoretical guarantees that maximize the amount of desired information and actions and minimize the amount of biased and manipulative actions in interaction with ML systems.

</details>


### [86] [STS: Efficient Sparse Attention with Speculative Token Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15508)
*Ceyu Xu,Jiangnan Yu,Yongji Wu,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了STS，一种无需模型重训练的稀疏注意力机制。利用小模型的注意力分数动态构建稀疏掩码，在NarrativeQA基准上实现了90%的稀疏度和2.67倍加速，精度损失可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）中注意力机制的二次复杂度导致了严重的内存和计算瓶颈，这一问题在需要处理数百万token序列的智能体应用中尤为突出。现有的解决方案往往需要昂贵的模型重训练，难以在实际应用中高效部署。

Method: 提出STS（Sparse Attention via Speculative Decoding），一种免重训练的稀疏注意力机制。核心思想是利用较小的草稿模型识别出的重要token来预测较大目标模型中的重要token。该方法集成在推测解码框架中，复用草稿模型的注意力分数动态构建token级和头级的稀疏掩码，从而有效剪枝目标LLM中昂贵的注意力计算。

Result: 在代表性基准NarrativeQA上的评估显示，STS在约90%的稀疏度下实现了2.67倍的速度提升，且与稠密注意力相比精度下降可忽略不计。STS在稀疏度-精度权衡上确立了新的最先进水平，在给定精度预算下能够实现比以往技术更高的稀疏度。

Conclusion: STS成功解决了LLM推理中的计算瓶颈问题，通过无需重训练的方式实现了高效的稀疏注意力。该方法为处理超长序列的智能体应用提供了实用解决方案，并在稀疏度与精度的平衡上超越了现有技术。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention imposes severe memory and computational bottlenecks on Large Language Model (LLM) inference. This challenge is particularly acute for emerging agentic applications that require processing multi-million token sequences. We propose STS, a sparse attention mechanism that requires no model retraining. STS leverages the key insight that tokens identified as important by a smaller draft model are highly predictive of important tokens for a larger target model. By integrating into speculative decoding frameworks, STS repurposes the draft model's attention scores to dynamically construct a token-and-head-wise sparsity mask. This mask effectively prunes the expensive attention computation in the target LLM. Our evaluation shows that STS achieves a 2.67x speedup operating at approximately 90% sparsity on representative benchmark NarrativeQA, maintaining negligible accuracy degradation compared to dense attention. STS establishes a new state-of-the-art on the sparsity-accuracy trade-off, outperforming prior techniques by enabling higher sparsity levels for a given accuracy budget.

</details>


### [87] [parallelcbf: A composable safety-filter and auditability framework for tensor-parallel reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15509)
*Yijun Lu,Zilei Yang,Yuyin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParallelCBF是首个统一大规模并行UAV仿真、控制障碍函数(CBF)安全过滤器、分层BC-to-RL流水线及可审计性的端到端安全约束训练框架，以Apache 2.0协议开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架存在能力割裂问题：Isaac Lab提供大规模并行UAV仿真，OmniSafe和safe-control-gym提供约束RL基准，CBFKit提供控制障碍函数综合工具，但缺乏统一框架实现端到端安全约束训练。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出ParallelCBF框架，整合四大核心能力：(i)张量并行UAV环境；(ii)硬门控CBF安全过滤器；(iii)分片BC-to-RL流水线；(iv)一等操作可审计性（预注册、监控注册表、故障取证、数据集审计），以可组合API形式实现而非用户脚本。框架采用四层可组合API架构，提供双屏障（平方/线性预测）CBF的CPU PyTorch参考实现。

Result: 发布ParallelCBF v0.1.0：属性驱动的安全不变性测试在全量39测试套件上1.67秒完成；完成31,415回合的行为克隆数据采集，其课程混合、每桶产量及数据集SHA-256均可通过框架ops原语审计；端到端流水线演示中，可审计层成功拦截未满足预注册收敛标准的下游训练阶段，防止劣质检查点静默传播。

Conclusion: 框架所提供的可审计性架构特性对可复现的实证机器人研究是必要而非仅仅有用的；该框架填补了安全约束训练领域统一框架的空白，通过组合式API设计提升了研究可复现性和安全性保障。

Abstract: While Isaac Lab provides massive parallel UAV simulation, OmniSafe and safe-control-gym provide constrained-RL benchmarks, and CBFKit provides control-barrier-function synthesis tooling, no existing framework unifies these capabilities for end-to-end safety-constrained training. ParallelCBF is the first framework to unify (i)~tensor-parallel UAV environments, (ii)~hard-gate CBF safety filters, (iii)~sharded BC-to-RL pipelines, and (iv)~first-class operational auditability -- pre-registration, watchdog registries, failure forensics, and dataset audits as composable APIs rather than user-implemented scripts. We release ParallelCBF v0.1.0 under Apache~2.0 with a four-layer composable API, a CPU PyTorch reference implementation of a dual-barrier (squared / linear-predictive) CBF, property-based safety invariance tests across vectorized batch sizes that complete in 1.67~s for the full 39-test suite, and a 31{,}415-episode behavior-cloning collection campaign whose curriculum mix, per-bucket yields, and dataset SHA-256 are auditable through the framework's own \texttt{ops} primitives. We report a representative end-to-end pipeline execution in which the framework's auditability layer halted a downstream training stage that did not meet pre-registered convergence criteria, preventing silent propagation of a degraded checkpoint -- an architectural property we argue is necessary, not merely useful, for reproducible empirical robotics research. The framework is installable via \texttt{pip install parallelcbf}; source and release artifacts are available at https://github.com/xiaoyang-123-cell/ParallelCBF.

</details>


### [88] [OgBench: A Framework for Evaluating Graph Neural Networks on Omics Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15511)
*Louisa Cornelis,Johan Mathe,Louis Van Langendonck,Guillermo Bernárdez,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推出了OgBench，这是首个针对组学数据中“样本数远少于节点数”（n≪p）场景的图神经网络基准测试平台。研究发现，当前主流GNN在该场景下往往不如简单的MLP和传统基线方法，挑战了图结构在此类数据中必然有益的普遍假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络基准测试主要关注样本数远大于节点数（n≫p）的场景，忽视了组学数据（如基因、转录组、蛋白质组）中典型的样本数远少于节点数（n≪p）的反常情况。这引发了对GNN在此类低样本、高维节点组学场景下的性能质疑。

Method: 提出了OgBench基准测试平台，提供从原始组学数据到具有不同结构特性的特征图家族的标准化、端到端模块化基础设施。该平台对比评估了经典GNN、针对大图和组学设计的GNN、MLP以及传统机器学习基线方法，建立了参考性能基准。

Result: 实验结果表明，广泛使用的图神经网络在组学数据的n≪p场景下往往无法超越简单的多层感知机和传统基线方法。这一发现挑战了图结构在该领域必然带来价值的主流假设。

Conclusion: 通过暴露当前GNN架构在组学数据场景下的局限性，OgBench为学术界提供了一个开源生态系统，促进社区开发并验证专门针对生物图的新型架构，推动对现有学习范式的批判性重新评估。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become the dominant framework for inductive graph-level learning. Yet most benchmarks focus on the regime $n \gg p$, where the number of graphs $n$ greatly exceeds the number of nodes per graph $p$. This overlooks biological domains such as omics, which operate in the opposite $n \ll p$ regime, characterized by large graphs of genes, transcripts, or proteins across few patient samples. This raises the question: \textit{how do GNNs perform in this low-sample, high-node omics setting?} We introduce \texttt{OgBench} (Omics-Graph Bench), the first benchmarking platform for graph-level prediction in the $n \ll p$ regime characteristic of omics data. We provide a standardized, end-to-end modular infrastructure from raw omics data to families of featured graphs with varied structural properties. We benchmark classical GNNs, as well as GNNs designed for large graphs and omics applications, alongside MLPs and machine learning baselines to establish reference performances. Our results show that widely used GNNs often do not outperform simple MLPs and classical baselines. These findings challenge the prevailing assumption that graph structure inherently adds value in this domain, fostering a critical reassessment of current learning paradigms. Ultimately, by exposing these limitations, OgBench provides the open-source ecosystem necessary for the community to develop and validate novel architectures explicitly tailored for biological graphs. The code is available at https://github.com/geometric-intelligence/ogbench.

</details>


### [89] [Neural Point-Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15524)
*Bruno Trentini,Jacob Hume,Vincenzo Antonio Isoldi,Philipp Misof,Ekaterina S. Ivshina,Kelly Maggs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经点形式（NPFs），一种基于微分形式和扩散几何的有原则的可学习几何特征，用于点云学习。该方法通过比较矩阵编码高阶切线信息，在采样、带宽、密度和流形假设下证明了长期一致性，在依赖采样密度、流形结构或响应相关群体几何的任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的点云学习方法假设观测样本是高维特征空间中嵌入流形等几何对象的噪声轨迹，但坐标、成对距离或学习到的图邻域无法直接捕获大部分几何信息。在光滑设置中，微分形式可以编码高阶切线信息，但点云缺乏自然的切线结构，因此需要一种新的方法来捕获这些几何特征。

Method: 作者引入神经点形式（NPFs）这一新的可学习几何特征族。利用扩散几何中基于拉普拉斯的技术，在没有自然切线结构的情况下，通过内积构建比较点云上微分形式的离散模型。在连续统中，共享环境特征空间的子流形被表示为比较矩阵，其条目描述特征形式对如何与外在切线信息相互作用。该方法产生了一个紧凑、高效且置换不变的神经网络层，输出为学习到的形式比较矩阵。

Result: 在标准采样、带宽、密度和流形假设下证明了比较矩阵的长期一致性。在合成实验和生物相关实验中，NPFs提供了具有竞争力和可解释性的表示，当标签依赖采样密度、流形结构或响应相关群体几何时，优势最为明显。

Conclusion: NPFs成功地将微分几何中的微分形式概念应用于点云学习，提供了一种有原则的、可学习的几何特征表示方法。该方法在理论和实践上都显示出有效性，特别是在几何结构对任务至关重要的场景中，为点云学习领域提供了一种新的几何建模工具。

Abstract: Point cloud learning often rests on the premise that observed samples are noisy traces of an underlying geometric object, such as a manifold embedded in a high-dimensional feature space. Yet much of this geometry is not captured directly by coordinates, pairwise distances, or learned graph neighborhoods alone. In the smooth setting, differential forms are devices to encode higher order tangency information. In this work, we introduce a new family of principled learnable geometric features for point clouds called neural point-forms (NPFs). In the absence of a natural tangency structure, we instead use Laplacian-based techniques from Diffusion Geometry to build a discrete model for comparing differential forms on point clouds via inner products. In the continuum, submanifolds of a shared ambient feature space are represented as comparison matrices, whose entries describe how pairs of feature forms interact with extrinsic tangency information. We make this intuition precise by proving the long-run consistency of comparison matrices under standard sampling, bandwidth, density, and manifold-hypothesis assumptions. This yields a compact, efficient and permutation-invariant neural layer whose output is a learned form-comparison matrix. Across synthetic and biologically relevant experiments, we show that NPFs provide a competitive, and interpretable representation, with the strongest benefits appearing when labels depend on sampling density, manifold-like structure, or response-relevant population geometry.

</details>


### [90] [Rethinking Neural Network Learning Rates: A Stackelberg Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15530)
*Sihan Zeng,Sujay Bhatt,Sumitra Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从Stackelberg博弈优化视角揭示了“网络主体层小学习率、输出层大学习率”策略的有效性，提供了收敛性保证，并发现该策略能利用更优的优化结构和更尖锐的局部曲率加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有经验证据表明为神经网络不同层分配差异化学习率可加速训练，但目前缺乏对其有效条件和内在机制的原理性理解，旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将非均匀学习率训练（主体层小、输出层大）解释为对Stackelberg博弈重构目标的“双时间尺度交替梯度下降”算法，并在包含约束集和非平滑激活函数的广泛条件下进行了理论分析。

Result: 建立了算法的有限时间收敛保证；揭示了两大加速机制：(i) Stackelberg目标函数比原目标具有更强的优化结构，能更快收敛至全局最优；(ii) 数值分析表明Stackelberg目标在训练初期具有更尖锐的局部曲率，产生信息量更大的梯度。监督学习和强化学习实验验证了上述发现。

Conclusion: 非均匀学习率的有效性可归因于Stackelberg优化视角下的特定结构与曲率特性，这为设计差异化学习率策略提供了坚实的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Neural networks are typically trained with a single learning rate across all layers. While recent empirical evidence suggests that assigning layer-specific learning rates can accelerate training, a principled understanding of the conditions and mechanisms under which non-uniform learning rates are beneficial remains limited. In this work, we investigate non-uniform learning rates through the lens of Stackelberg optimization. Specifically, we demonstrate that training neural networks with a smaller learning rate for the body layers and a larger learning rate for the final layer can be interpreted as a two-time-scale alternating gradient descent algorithm applied to a Stackelberg reformulation of the original objective. We establish finite-time convergence guarantees for the algorithm under broad conditions that accommodate constraint sets and non-smooth activation functions. Beyond convergence, we identify two mechanisms by which non-uniform learning rates can outperform uniform learning rates: (i) we show that certain problem instances induce a Stackelberg objective with stronger optimization structure than the original objective, yielding faster convergence to globally optimal solutions, (ii) our numerical analysis reveals that the Stackelberg objective can exhibit substantially sharper local curvature, especially in early training, which leads to more informative gradients and learning acceleration. Experiments in supervised learning and reinforcement learning support our findings.

</details>


### [91] [DeltaPrompts: Escaping the Zero-Delta Trap in Multimodal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15532)
*Jaehun Jung,Hyunwoo Kim,Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,David Acuna,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发现现有蒸馏数据集中69%的提示词为零增量（师生答案分布一致），导致训练低效。提出基于答案分歧度（Δ）的合成流程，构建20万高分歧推理题数据集DeltaPrompts，在多场景下实现最高15%相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型蒸馏过程中，提示词选择依赖简单启发式方法或现成数据集，作者揭示其中的关键低效问题：标准数据集中多达69%的提示词为零增量，即教师与学生模型已产生相同答案分布，训练信号微弱，学生改进迅速饱和。因此需要识别并利用能暴露师生能力差距的有效提示词。

Method: 基于蒸馏本质是最小化分布差异的原理，提出答案分歧度（Δ）量化师生能力差距，证实非零分歧对有效扩展的重要性。基于此设计分阶段合成流程：以现有数据集为种子，主动针对学生失败模式生成高分歧提示词，构建包含20万合成推理题的DeltaPrompts数据集。

Result: 在三种设置下评估：目标师生对的策略内蒸馏、无需重新生成数据的跨模型迁移、非推理模型的离策略微调。在图表、文档、感知中心推理的10个基准测试中，DeltaPrompts驱动显著提升，即使在高度优化的推理模型（如Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking）之上仍获得最高15%相对改进。

Conclusion: 通过识别零增量陷阱并量化答案分歧度，DeltaPrompts数据集证明高分歧提示词对蒸馏效率至关重要。该方法在不同场景下均取得显著成效，为视觉语言模型蒸馏提供了一条有效的数据合成路径。

Abstract: Distillation enables compact Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to obtain strong reasoning capabilities, yet the prompts driving this process are typically chosen via simple heuristics or aggregated from off-the-shelf datasets. We reveal a critical inefficiency in this approach: up to 69% of the prompts in standard chart / document reasoning datasets are effectively zero-delta, meaning the teacher and student already induce the exact same answer distribution. Training on these prompts provides minimal learning signal, causing student improvement to rapidly saturate regardless of data scale. To escape the zero-delta trap, we return to first principles: distillation fundamentally minimizes distributional divergence, and thus a prompt is valuable only if it exposes a functional capability gap between the teacher and student. We quantify this gap through answer divergence ($Δ$), demonstrating that non-zero divergence is critical for effective scaling. Building on this insight, we propose a staged synthesis pipeline that repurposes existing datasets as seeds, actively targeting student failure modes to produce better prompts. The result is DeltaPrompts, a diverse dataset of 200k synthetic, high-divergence reasoning problems. We evaluate DeltaPrompts across three distinct settings: on-policy distillation with the target teacher-student pair, transfer to a novel model family without regenerating the data, and off-policy fine-tuning of a non-reasoning model. Across all scenarios, DeltaPrompts drives substantial gains, yielding up to 15% relative improvement even on top of a highly-optimized reasoning model (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) -- averaged over 10 benchmarks spanning chart, document and perception-centric reasoning.

</details>


### [92] [CTF4Nuclear: Common Task Framework for Nuclear Fission and Fusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15549)
*Stefano Riva,Carolina Introini,Antonio Cammi,Dean Price,Alexey Yermakov,Yue Zhao,Philippe M. Wyder,Judah Goldfeder,Jan Williams,Amy Sara Rude,Matteo Tomasetto,Joe Germany,Joseph Bakarji,Georg Maierhofer,Miles Cranmer,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个核工程机器学习通用任务框架（CTF），包含精选数据集和12项评估指标，用于标准化比较不同ML方法在核系统行为预测中的性能，旨在提高核工业科学ML研究的严谨性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核能系统设计和运行极其复杂，高保真仿真计算成本高昂且不适合实时应用，而传统模型方法对简化假设敏感。机器学习有潜力构建快速可靠的代理模型，但在安全关键的核工程领域，缺乏对不同ML方法公平比较和对其优缺点清晰理解的标准框架。

Method: 借鉴动力系统和地震学领域的经验，构建了核工程机器学习通用任务框架（CTF）。该框架包含从不同核及核相关系统中精选的数据集，使用12个既定指标评估方法性能，并引入了仅从稀疏测量进行系统监测的新范式。通过对标准ML基线进行基准测试来展示框架。

Result: 使用提出的CTF框架对标准ML基线方法进行了基准测试，揭示了当前方法在核系统行为预测任务中的局限性，验证了框架的有效性，为未来方法比较提供了标准化评估基础。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在用隐藏测试集上的标准化评估替代临时比较，提高核工业科学机器学习研究的严谨性和可复现性，推动ML方法在安全关键核工程应用中的发展。

Abstract: The demand for clean energy is ever increasing, with new nuclear technologies presenting a complementary solution to renewable energies. However, designing and operating these systems is exceptionally difficult, given the complexity of the physical phenomena that interact to form the system dynamics. While high-fidelity simulations help to understand the non-linear, multi-physics interactions within a reactor, they are computationally expensive and rarely suitable for real-time applications. Furthermore, model-based approaches are inherently sensitive to simplifying assumptions required to derive their governing equations and parameters, leading to inevitable discrepancies with real-world measurements. In contrast, Machine Learning (ML) methods have the potential to generate reliable surrogate models which may be able to quickly predict the system's behaviour. However, the number of data-driven methods that can potentially be used for this task is large and diverse. In a safety-critical setting such as nuclear engineering, a fair comparison of different ML methods, and a clear understanding of their advantages and limitations, is of paramount importance. To address this, we introduce a Common Task Framework (CTF) for ML in nuclear engineering, building upon previous efforts in dynamical systems and seismology. This CTF considers a curated set of datasets from different nuclear and nuclear-adjacent systems. The CTF evaluates the performance of a method on 12 established metrics, alongside a new paradigm focused on system monitoring from sparse measurements only. We illustrate the framework by benchmarking standard ML baselines against these datasets, revealing current method limitations. Our vision is to replace ad hoc comparisons with standardized evaluations on hidden test sets, raising the bar for rigour and reproducibility in scientific ML for the nuclear industry.

</details>


### [93] [Characterizing Learning in Deep Neural Networks using Tractable Algorithmic Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15551)
*Pedram Bakhtiarifard,Sophia N. Wilson,Mahmoud Afifi,Jonathan Wenshøj,Raghavendra Selvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuBD方法，首次实现了对大规模深度神经网络权重的KCS算法复杂度的可扩展估计，揭示了网络权重复杂度在训练过程中的动态变化规律及其与泛化性能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码定理方法（CTM）和块分解方法（BDM）的KCS复杂度估计器仅适用于小型二进制对象，无法扩展到现代大规模深度神经网络，而"学习即压缩"假设认为训练会在网络权重中诱导出可压缩的结构，因此需要一种能够衡量大规模神经网络权重结构的方法。

Method: 提出量化块分解方法（QuBD），将算法复杂度估计扩展到任意k进制对象。该方法首先将网络权重量化到有限字母表，然后通过聚合每个位平面的CTM估计来估计KCS复杂度，理论上证明了QuBD比基于二值化的方法具有更严格的估计间隙。

Result: 实验发现神经网络权重的算法复杂度随着模型学习而降低，随数据预算扩展，在过拟合时增加，遵循grokking过程中观察到的延迟泛化现象，并与泛化性能相关；同时发现算法信息主要存在于最高有效位平面中，这可作为确定训练后量化级别的实用诊断工具。

Conclusion: 本研究通过提供首个针对大型非二进制对象（如DNN权重）的可扩展、可处理的KCS复杂度估计，为深度神经网络的学习机制提供了新见解，为理解"学习即压缩"假设提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Training large-scale deep neural networks (DNNs) is resource-intensive, making model compression a practical necessity. The widely accepted ''learning as compression'' hypothesis posits that training induces structure in network weights, which enables compression. Measuring this structure through Kolmogorov-Chaitin-Solomonoff (KCS) complexity is appealing, but existing estimators based on the Coding Theorem Method (CTM) and the Block Decomposition Method (BDM) are limited to small binary objects and do not scale to modern DNNs. We introduce the Quantized Block Decomposition method (QuBD), which extends algorithmic complexity estimation to any $k$-ary object. QuBD first quantizes the network weights to a finite alphabet, then estimates the KCS complexity by aggregating per bit-plane CTM estimates. We show theoretically that QuBD yields a strictly tighter estimation gap with respect to true KCS complexity than binarization-based methods. Using QuBD, we study how the algorithmic complexity of neural network weights evolves during training, showing that it decreases as models learn, scales with data budget, increases during overfitting, follows the delayed generalization observed during grokking, and correlates with generalization performance. We further show that algorithmic information resides predominantly in the most significant bit-planes, which can serve as a practical diagnostic for determining appropriate post-training quantization levels. This work offers novel insights into learning mechanisms in DNNs by providing the first scalable, tractable estimates of KCS complexity for large, non-binary objects such as DNN weights.

</details>


### [94] [CrystalBoltz: End-to-End Protein Structure Determination via Experiment-Guided Diffusion for X-Ray Crystallography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15564)
*Minseo Kim,Huanghao Mai,Jay Shenoy,Alec Follmer,Gordon Wetzstein,Frederic Poitevin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CrystalBoltz，一个将晶体学精修转化为贝叶斯推断的生成式框架，通过直接利用结构因子幅值进行实验引导的后验采样，在降低坐标RMSD和R因子的同时，将运行时间缩短了33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于公共蛋白质结构数据库训练的生成模型虽然为结构预测提供了强大的先验，但难以直接以新的晶体学实验测量数据为条件进行优化，限制了其在X射线结构确定中的应用。由于相位信息缺失，晶体学结构确定仍是一个反问题，需要大量人工精修。

Method: 提出CrystalBoltz框架，将晶体学精修建模为原子结构的贝叶斯推断问题。该方法直接操作结构因子幅值，从预训练的蛋白质结构先验出发，过渡到实验引导的后验采样，最后进行原子坐标和B因子精修。

Result: 在多个蛋白质晶体学数据集上，CrystalBoltz相比最强基线实现了更低的坐标RMSD和更低的R因子，同时相比现有的实验引导精修方法，运行时间减少了33倍。

Conclusion: CrystalBoltz成功地将生成模型与实验数据相结合，为晶体学结构确定提供了一种高效、自动化的解决方案，显著提升了精修精度并大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Generative models trained on public databases of protein structures, most of which have been determined by X-ray crystallography, now provide powerful priors for structure prediction. However, they are not readily conditioned on the measurements from a new crystallographic experiment, limiting their use for X-ray structure determination. In crystallography, the measured structure-factor amplitudes do not by themselves determine an electron density map or atomic structure because the associated phases are unobserved and must be inferred. Structure determination therefore remains an inverse problem in which candidate models must be both structurally plausible and consistent with measured diffraction data, often requiring substantial manual refinement by human experts. Emerging methods aim to incorporate experimental information more directly into predictive and refinement workflows. We present CrystalBoltz, a generative framework that casts crystallographic refinement as Bayesian inference over atomic structures and operates directly on structure-factor amplitudes. CrystalBoltz moves from unguided generation with a pre-trained prior over protein structures to experiment-guided posterior sampling, followed by atomic coordinate and B-factor refinement. Across multiple protein crystallography datasets, CrystalBoltz attains lower coordinate RMSD and lower R-factors than the strongest baselines considered, while reducing runtime by a factor of 33 relative to existing experimentally guided refinement.

</details>


### [95] [AstraFlow: Dataflow-Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15565)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Jiahui Wang,Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Yongji Wu,Z. Morley Mao,Ion Stoica,Jiawei Zhao,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AstraFlow，一种面向数据流的强化学习系统，通过解耦组件架构解决智能体LLM训练的系统工程负担问题，在多策略协作训练中实现2.7倍加速且保持相当或更优的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM强化学习系统采用以训练器为中心的控制架构，缺乏针对系统组件的原则性抽象，导致每次扩展都需要专门的系统工程工作。扩展到智能体LLM需要支持多策略协作训练等复杂工作负载，并高效利用弹性、异构和跨区域计算资源。

Method: 提出面向数据流的RL系统AstraFlow，将rollout服务、数据流管理和训练解耦为自主组件，用原则性组件抽象替代传统以训练器为中心的控制架构。该系统原生支持复杂多策略智能体RL工作负载，并能高效利用多样化计算资源。

Result: 在数学、代码、搜索和AgentBench工作负载上进行评估，同一系统无需系统级代码修改即可支持多策略训练、弹性扩展、异构跨区域执行和可组合数据算法。在多策略协作训练中，相比现有RL系统训练速度提升2.7倍，准确性相当或更优。

Conclusion: AstraFlow成功通过数据流导向架构实现了组件解耦，消除了传统系统中的系统工程负担，证明了该架构在支持复杂智能体RL工作负载方面的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to improve the reasoning, coding, and tool-use capabilities of large language models, but agentic RL remains prohibitively expensive. Scaling RL to agentic LLMs requires supporting complex workloads, including multi-policy collaborative training, while efficiently using elastic, heterogeneous, and cross-region compute resources. Existing LLM RL systems support some of these capabilities, but each new extension often requires dedicated system engineering. This burden arises from trainer-centered control architectures and the lack of principled abstractions for RL system components. To address these limitations, we propose AstraFlow, a dataflow-oriented RL system that replaces conventional trainer-centered control with principled component abstractions. In AstraFlow, rollout services, dataflow management, and training are decoupled into autonomous components, enabling the system to natively support complex multi-policy agentic RL workloads and efficiently exploit diverse compute resources. We evaluate AstraFlow across math, code, search, and AgentBench workloads, showing that the same system supports multi-policy training, elastic scaling, heterogeneous cross-region execution, and composable data algorithms without system-level code changes. In multi-policy collaborative training, AstraFlow achieves comparable or better accuracy than existing RL systems while speeding up training time by 2.7x.

</details>


### [96] [Gaussian Relational Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15575)
*Zezhong Ding,Jin Li,Xugang Wang,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GelGT（高斯关系图Transformer），通过结构-语义协同采样和高斯图注意力机制，解决了现有关系图学习中长距离依赖捕获不足和多维信息联合建模受限的问题，在真实数据集上预测性能最高提升13.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关系图学习模型在消息传递机制中存在信息衰减问题，难以捕获长距离依赖；同时，近期的关系图Transformer在联合建模结构、语义和时间信息方面仍存在局限性。

Method: 提出GelGT模型，包含两个核心组件：1）结构-语义协同采样策略，在保留结构连通性的同时过滤无关语义信息；2）高斯图注意力机制，在采样子图上引入可学习的高斯偏置，动态编码时间依赖关系。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，GelGT达到了最先进的下游任务性能，预测性能最高提升13.8%。

Conclusion: GelGT有效解决了关系图学习中的长距离依赖捕获和多维信息联合建模问题，显著提升了预测性能，为关系数据库的图学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Relational graph learning models relational databases as graphs and has demonstrated superior performance on a wide range of relational predictive tasks. However, existing methods struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to information decay in their message-passing mechanisms, and recent relational graph transformers remain limited in jointly modeling structural, semantic, and temporal information. In this paper, we propose GelGT, a Gaussian relational graph transformer that explicitly addresses these challenges. GelGT introduces a structure-semantic collaborative sampling strategy to preserve structural connectivity while filtering irrelevant semantic information, and incorporates a Gaussian graph attention mechanism with a learnable Gaussian bias on the sampled subgraphs to dynamically encode temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on various real-world datasets demonstrate that GelGT achieves state-of-the-art downstream task performance, with up to a 13.8% improvement in predictive performance.

</details>


### [97] [Embracing Biased Transition Matrices for Complementary-Label Learning with Many Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15586)
*Tan-Ha Mai,Chao-Kai Chiang,Han-Hwa Shih,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BICL框架，通过设计非均匀的偏置标签生成过程，解决了互补标签学习在大标签空间下性能下降的问题，在多类分类任务上实现了超过七倍的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统互补标签学习方法假设标签生成过程是均匀的，在大标签空间（多类别）场景下，该假设会导致学习信号被过度稀释，使得CLL方法难以扩展。为了突破这一长期瓶颈，本文旨在研究如何通过改变标签生成假设来提升CLL在大规模类别下的性能。

Method: 提出Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling (BICL) 框架。该方法跨越数据收集到模型训练全流程，核心在于故意设计一个有偏置（非均匀）的生成过程，将互补标签限制在类别的一个子集中，从而增强学习信号。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet-200数据集上的实验表明，BICL相比于传统方法实现了超过七倍的准确率提升，成功验证了该框架在多类别场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过设计非均匀的偏置标签生成过程可以克服CLL在大标签空间下的局限性。BICL框架的提出为使互补标签学习在实际应用中处理大量类别开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Complementary-label learning (CLL) is a weakly supervised paradigm where instances are labeled with classes they do not belong to. Despite a decade of research, CLL methods remain competitive mainly on 10-class classification, with scaling to large label spaces continuing to be an enduring bottleneck. This limitation stems from the common assumption of uniform label generation in traditional methods, which fatally dilutes the learning signal in many-class settings. In this paper, we demonstrate that this long-standing barrier can be overcome by deliberately designing a biased (non-uniform) generation process that restricts complementary labels to a subset of classes. This finding motivates us to propose Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling (BICL), a principled framework spanning data collection to training that leverages this bias. BICL enables effective learning on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet-200, achieving more than sevenfold accuracy improvements over traditional methods. Our findings establish a new trajectory for making CLL feasible for many classes in real-world applications.

</details>


### [98] [Offline Reinforcement Learning with Universal Horizon Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15603)
*Hojun Chung,Junseo Lee,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了通用视界模型（UHM），通过预测任意视界下的未来状态并采用缩尾视界分布来稳定训练，有效解决了离线强化学习中的复合误差问题，在100个OGBench任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的离线强化学习虽然可以通过在想象的策略轨迹上进行价值学习来提升性能，但存在复合误差问题。现有的几何视界模型（GHM）虽然通过直接预测无限视界未来缓解了该问题，但在准确建模远期未来状态方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出了通用视界模型（UHM），作为GHM的推广，能够直接预测任意视界下的未来状态。基于此灵活性，设计了一种可扩展的价值学习方法，通过采用缩尾视界分布来限制过大的视界，从而稳定训练过程。

Result: 在100个具有挑战性的OGBench任务上的实验表明，该方法优于竞争基线方法，特别是在具有高度次优数据集和需要长期推理的任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: UHM通过灵活的任意视界预测和缩尾视界分布，有效解决了复合误差和远期建模不准确的问题，为离线强化学习提供了一种更稳定且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) offers a compelling approach to offline RL by enabling value learning on imagined on-policy trajectories. However, it often suffers from compounding errors due to repeated model inference on self-generated states. While geometric horizon models (GHM) alleviate this issue through direct prediction over a discounted infinite-horizon future, they remain challenged in accurately modeling distant future states. To this end, we introduce universal horizon models (UHM), a generalization of GHM that directly predicts future states under arbitrary horizons. Leveraging this flexibility, we propose a scalable value learning method that employs a winsorized horizon distribution to stabilize training by capping excessively large horizons. Experimental results on 100 challenging OGBench tasks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms competitive baselines, particularly on tasks with highly suboptimal datasets and those requiring long-horizon reasoning. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/UHM/

</details>


### [99] [VSPO: Vector-Steered Policy Optimization for Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15604)
*Xuechen Zhang,Zijian Huang,Kai Yang,Weijia Zhang,Jiasi Chen,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了向量引导策略优化（VSPO），通过引入与目标行为关联的引导向量，有效解决了大语言模型在优化主要目标时难以获得稀疏次级行为偏好的问题，在多个推理基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型需要在优化主要准确性目标的同时满足次级行为偏好（如冗长性、亲和力或技术专业水平）。在实际应用中，基础模型可能很少或根本不表现出期望的行为，这造成了稀疏行为奖励瓶颈的问题，使得模型难以学习到目标行为。

Method: 提出向量引导策略优化（VSPO），通过修改GRPO方法来实现。VSPO使用与目标行为关联的引导向量来控制生成样本的行为强度，通过采样不同引导强度的样本进行训练。该过程可解释为一种在线策略潜在自蒸馏过程，模型在此过程中内化其引导向量。通过改变引导强度，VSPO对稀有行为进行上采样并丰富样本多样性，从而缓解稀疏奖励问题。

Result: 在多臂老虎机抽象下，当引导产生的分布与目标行为充分对齐时，VSPO在迭代复杂度上优于奖励整形的GRPO。在MATH和MMLU-Pro等多个推理基准测试中，针对四个目标行为（解释专业性、置信度表达、对误导性上下文的鲁棒性、响应冗长度）进行评估，VSPO在保持或提高任务准确性的同时，能够持续改善对目标行为的控制能力。

Conclusion: VSPO相比奖励整形、教师轨迹蒸馏和基于指导的基线方法具有优势，能够有效解决多目标优化问题，为语言模型的行为控制提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern language models often need to optimize a primary accuracy objective while also accommodating secondary behavioral preferences, such as verbosity, agreeableness, or the level of technical expertise in its response. In practice, a base model may exhibit a desired behavior very rarely or not at all. Thus, endowing the model with a target behavior creates a sparse behavioral reward bottleneck. To address such multi-objective problems, we introduce Vector-Steered Policy Optimization (VSPO) which employs a steering vector associated with the target behavior to control the behavior intensity of the generated rollouts. VSPO is obtained by modifying GRPO to sample rollouts with varying steering intensities. This process can be interpreted as an on-policy latent self-distillation procedure where the model internalizes its steering vector. By varying steering intensities, VSPO upsamples rare behaviors and enriches rollout diversity, which alleviates the sparse reward issue and provably accelerates the policy optimization. Through comprehensive theory and experiments, we establish that VSPO has favorable properties compared to vanilla reward shaping and other alternative approaches. Specifically, under a bandit abstraction, VSPO provably achieves better iteration complexity than reward-shaped GRPO when the steering-induced distributions are sufficiently aligned with the target behavior. We evaluate VSPO across multiple reasoning benchmarks, including MATH and MMLU-Pro, for four target behaviors: explanation expertise, confidence expression, robustness to misleading context, and response verbosity. Our results show that VSPO consistently improves the control along target behavior while maintaining or improving task accuracy compared with reward shaping, teacher-trace distillation, and guidance-based baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Transformer-like Inference from Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15608)
*Aditya Kudre,Heng-Sheng Chang,Prashant G. Mehta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最优控制理论推导出了与解码器仅Transformer相似的推理架构，揭示了Transformer的层级结构可视为最优控制问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decoder-only Transformer通过计算条件概率预测下一个token，但其架构设计的理论基础尚不明确。本文旨在从最优控制理论的基本原理出发，推导能够解决相同预测问题的推理架构，以理解Transformer层级结构的理论根源。

Method: 针对两类模型进行理论推导：(1)直接受Transformer启发的离散值过程非线性模型；(2)作为可处理基线的线性高斯模型。将预测目标重构为最优控制问题，其解产生显式推理算法——双重滤波器，其层级结构镜像了Decoder-only Transformer的层级结构。通过数值实验比较最优控制得到的权重与训练好的Transformer的注意力权重。

Result: 成功从最优控制理论推导出类似Transformer的推理架构，证明Transformer类层级操作是最优控制理论的自然结果。实验发现当嵌入维度不足时，Transformer会隐式利用非马尔可夫结构，这揭示了Transformer在有限表达能力下的自适应行为。

Conclusion: Transformer的层级结构可以通过最优控制理论从基本原理推导得出，这为理解Transformer架构提供了新的理论视角，并揭示了其在嵌入维度受限时的隐式策略行为。

Abstract: Decoder-only transformers compute the conditional probability of the next token from a sequence of past observations. This paper derives, from first principles, inference architectures that solve the same prediction problem - and in doing so, recovers transformer-like layer operations as a consequence of optimal control theory. The framework is developed for two model classes: a nonlinear model of discrete-valued processes, directly motivated by the transformer, and a linear Gaussian model as a tractable baseline. For both model classes, the prediction objective is reformulated as an optimal control problem whose solution yields an explicit inference algorithm, the dual filter, with a layer structure that mirrors the layer structure of a decoder-only transformer. Numerical experiments provide a comparison of the optimal control to attention weights from a trained transformer. These experiments reveal that when the embedding dimension is insufficient, the transformer implicitly exploits non-Markovian structure.

</details>


### [101] [Position: Zeroth-Order Optimization in Deep Learning Is Underexplored, Not Underpowered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15622)
*Sijia Liu,Yicheng Lang,Soumyadeep Pal,Changsheng Wang,Yancheng Huang,Chongyu Fan,James Diffenderfer,Bhavya Kailkhura,Yihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为零阶优化正名，指出其并非不可扩展，而是因“全空间、逐元素、估计器中心”的局限设计被误判。作者提出利用子空间/谱视角降方差、以前向传播优势优化系统效率，并呼吁重构算法与评估体系以释放其大规模潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化因无需反向传播、内存效率高且适用于灰盒/黑盒流程而备受关注，但普遍被认为受估计器方差与查询复杂度限制，难以扩展。作者认为这种结论源于开发实践的短视，阻碍了对ZO潜力的挖掘。

Method: 1) 从方差控制、方差-查询权衡与方向导数视角重新审视估计器中心的ZO可行性边界；2) 提出三个被忽视的机会：子空间与谱视角实现可解释的方差缩减与优雅的查询扩展；利用ZO仅前向传播的特性提升通信效率、流水线友好性与资源受限训练的适用性；解耦评估中的任务复杂度与ZO本身的性能。作者从算法、系统与评估三层提出六大主张。

Result: 揭示了传统ZO方法的局限主要来自全空间逐元素设计；展示了子空间/谱方法可有效降低方差并缓解查询开销；证明了ZO的前向传播特性在分布式与资源受限场景具备系统级优势；提出评估应分离任务复杂度干扰。

Conclusion: ZO优化并非能力不足，而是探索不足。应基于其独特优势重新设计算法与系统，推动面向大规模、系统感知、资源高效学习的ZO优化发展。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization, learning from finite differences of function evaluations without backpropagation, has recently regained attention in deep learning due to its memory efficiency and applicability to gray- or black-box pipelines. Yet, ZO methods are often dismissed as fundamentally unscalable because of estimator variance and unfavorable query complexity. We argue that this conclusion might be misguided: ZO optimization is underexplored, not underpowered. We show that many perceived limitations stem from myopic development practices, most notably full-space, element-wise, estimator-centric designs. We articulate six positions spanning the algorithmic, systems, and evaluation stack. First, we revisit the feasibility boundaries of estimator-centric ZO methods through variance control, variance-query tradeoffs, and directional-derivative lenses. Then, we identify three underexplored opportunities: (i) subspace and spectral views of ZO that enable interpretable variance reduction with graceful query scaling, (ii) the forward-only nature of ZO as a systems advantage for communication-efficient, pipeline-friendly, and resource-constrained training, and (iii) the need to de-obfuscate ZO evaluations from task complexity. We strongly advocate rethinking ZO optimization around its unique strengths and acting accordingly, opening a viable path toward large-scale, system-aware, and resource-efficient learning with ZO optimization.

</details>


### [102] [IO-SVD: Input-Output Whitened SVD for Adaptive-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15626)
*Ali Abbasi,Chayne Thrash,Haoran Qin,Hamed Pirsiavash,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IO-SVD方法，通过构建KL感知的双边白化空间和异构秩分配策略，实现了大语言模型的高效压缩，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低了存储和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SVD的后训练压缩方法存在局限性：仅依赖输入端白化、采用均匀秩分配或损失无关的分配启发式方法，这些限制导致在激进压缩下难以保持模型质量。需要开发一种能够更好保持模型性能的压缩方法。

Method: 提出Input-Output Whitened SVD (IO-SVD)方法：1）基于KL散度的二阶展开构建输出端度量，结合输入白化形成KL感知的双边白化空间；2）设计高效的异构秩分配策略，使用一阶校准损失估计对白化奇异分量进行评分，在全局预算下剪枝最不敏感的分量；3）改进混合SVD-量化压缩，通过损失感知重映射选择适合8-bit量化的低秩因子行。

Result: 在多种LLM和VLM模型家族上进行了广泛实验，结果表明IO-SVD能够以最小的性能退化压缩LLM，同时实现实际的推理加速。方法在硬件无关的前提下有效降低了模型大小并提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: IO-SVD通过双边白化和损失感知的异构秩分配，成功解决了现有SVD压缩方法的关键局限性，为大语言模型在资源受限和延迟敏感场景的部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models deliver strong performance across language and reasoning tasks, but their storage and compute costs remain major barriers to deployment in resource-constrained and latency-sensitive settings. SVD-based post-training compression offers a hardware-agnostic way to reduce model size and improve inference efficiency through low-rank factorization. However, existing methods often rely on input-only whitening spaces, homogeneous rank allocation, or loss-agnostic allocation heuristics, limiting their ability to preserve model quality under aggressive compression. We propose Input-Output Whitened SVD (IO-SVD), a post-training compression method that forms a KL-aware double-sided whitening space for model weights. Using a second-order expansion of the KL loss over the top-K token probabilities, IO-SVD constructs an output-side metric that captures predictive sensitivity, while input whitening captures activation statistics. We further introduce an efficient heterogeneous rank-allocation strategy that scores whitened singular components using first-order calibration loss estimates and prunes the least sensitive components under a global budget. Inspired by prior work that combines SVD truncation with quantization, we improve hybrid SVD-quantization compression through loss-aware remapping, which selects low-rank factor rows for 8-bit quantization based on the predicted loss change incurred by quantizing them. Extensive experiments across diverse LLM and VLM families, and inference-time analysis shows that IO-SVD compresses LLMs with minimal performance degradation while delivering practical inference speedups. Code is available at https://github.com/mint-vu/IO-SVD.git

</details>


### [103] [Perforated Neural Networks for Keyword Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15647)
*Vishy Gopal,Aris Ilias Goutis,Ralph Crewe,Erin Yanacek,Rorry Brenner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Perforated Backpropagation应用于边缘关键词识别任务，在减少模型参数的同时提升了准确率，证明了该方法能有效解决边缘AI部署中的资源约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘机器学习面临严格的内存预算、有限的计算资源和不可妥协的准确率阈值等多重约束。现有的压缩和优化技术通常只能在不同资源之间进行权衡，难以同时提升准确率并减小模型尺寸。因此，本文旨在探索一种能够同时改善模型质量和部署效率的方法。

Method: 本文应用Perforated Backpropagation技术，通过在标准卷积神经网络中添加人工树突节点（Dendrite Nodes），在Edge Impulse平台上构建了树突模型。研究在Edge Impulse关键词识别教程流程中进行了800次超参数试验，系统性地比较了树突模型与传统架构在不同参数数量和准确率阈值下的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，树突模型在所有参数数量级别和所有测试的准确率阈值下均优于传统架构。最佳树突模型仅用1,500个参数就达到了0.933的测试准确率，而基线模型需要约4,000个参数才能达到0.921的准确率。这意味着在更少参数的情况下实现了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明Perforated Backpropagation是边缘AI工程师工具箱中的有力补充，能够同时实现模型质量的提升和部署效率的优化。该方法为解决边缘设备上的模型部署约束提供了新的技术路径，具有重要的实践价值。

Abstract: Edge machine learning presents a unique set of constraints not encountered in cloud-scale model deployment: strict memory budgets, limited compute, and non-negotiable accuracy thresholds must all be satisfied simultaneously. Existing compression and optimization techniques can trade one resource for another, but rarely improve both accuracy and model size at the same time. This paper presents the application of Perforated Backpropagation to keyword spotting on the Edge Impulse platform, an experiment that won the Best Model award at the Edge Impulse 2025 Hackathon in December 2025. By adding artificial Dendrite Nodes to a standard convolutional neural network trained on the Edge Impulse keyword spotting tutorial pipeline, we demonstrate that dendritic models outperform traditional architectures at every level of parameter count and at every accuracy threshold tested across 800 hyperparameter trials. The best dendritic model achieved a test accuracy of 0.933 with only 1,500 parameters, versus the baseline accuracy of 0.921 requiring approximately 4,000 parameters. These results suggest that Perforated Backpropagation is a powerful addition to the edge AI engineer's toolkit, offering simultaneous gains in both model quality and deployment efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [Towards Code-Oriented LM Embeddings for Surrogate-Assisted Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15649)
*Pranav Somu,Advay Balakrishnan,Stepan Kravtsov,Aaron McDaniel,Jason Zutty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种低成本嵌入策略COLE，利用预训练语言模型的归纳偏置作为NAS代理模型，无需昂贵微调即可实现高效性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS性能代理模型的开发通常需要昂贵的微调过程或复杂的表示工程，成本较高。本文旨在消除这些开销，探索利用语言模型的归纳偏置来构建低成本、高效的架构表示方法。

Method: 将神经架构表示为PyTorch类定义文本，利用现成的冻结语言模型作为特征提取器，无需NAS专用微调。通过提取代码导向的语言模型嵌入（COLE），并通过轻量级回归头构建最终预测器。同时研究了提高嵌入质量和利用率的策略。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201和einspace搜索空间上的实验表明：原始代码输入比其他文本编码（如ONNX-to-text）具有更高的预测性能；COLE在BANANAS算法中表现优异；在CIFAR-100优化中，用COLE替换结构路径编码可使达到搜索空间最优架构1%以内性能所需的评估预算减少34%。

Conclusion: 由于任何神经架构都可以表示为代码，COLE确立了作为推进NAS发展的通用且高效基础的地位，为NAS代理模型的构建提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Developing effective surrogates (performance predictors) for Neural Architecture Search (NAS) typically requires expensive fine-tuning or the engineering of complex representations. We propose a low-cost embedding strategy that leverages the inductive bias of Language Models (LMs) to eliminate these overheads. By representing architectures as PyTorch class definition text, we demonstrate that off-the-shelf LMs act as competitive feature extractors without NAS-specialized fine-tuning. The final predictor is constructed by passing the extracted Code-Oriented LM Embeddings (COLE) through a lightweight regression head. We also investigate strategies to improve embedding quality and utilization. Our experiments on the NAS-Bench-201 and einspace search spaces reveal that raw code inputs yield higher predictive performance than other text-based encodings (e.g., ONNX-to-text encodings) when using frozen LMs. We also observe COLE drives superior surrogate-assisted search using the BANANAS algorithm in NAS-Bench-201. When optimizing for CIFAR-100 performance, replacing structural path encodings with COLE for architecture representation allows for a 34% decrease in the evaluation budget required to reach within 1% of the fittest architecture in the search space (by test accuracy). As any neural architecture can be represented as code, these findings establish COLE as a versatile and efficient foundation for advancing NAS.

</details>


### [105] [Sharp Spectral Thresholds for Logit Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15651)
*Tongxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Softmax反馈系统的一个被经典理论遗漏的稳定区域，建立了一个精确的、与维度无关的稳定性阈值（$β\|ΠWΠ\|_{\mathcal T\to\mathcal T}<2$），从而将稳定性保证扩展到了奖励敏感但全局可预测的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax反馈系统是熵正则化强化学习、逻辑博弈动力学等领域的核心。经典理论将其视为单位尺度响应，仅在强随机化（过正则化）区域认证稳定性，给出了过于保守的条件，且遗漏了整个稳定区域。本文旨在寻找Softmax系统产生唯一且全局可预测结果的精确边界。

Method: 针对有限维仿射逻辑（affine logit）系统，研究者推导出了一个精确的欧几里得阈值条件。该方法不再局限于经典理论中仅适用于过正则化区域的保守分析，而是从理论上确定了系统定性行为发生相变的临界点。

Result: 证明了系统全局稳定性的精确充要条件是 $β\|ΠWΠ\|_{\mathcal T\to\mathcal T}<2$。这一阈值是维度无关的，比经典条件更宽松。它填补了分岔前被遗漏的稳定区域，使稳定性保证延伸至既能响应奖励又保持全局可预测的系统。

Conclusion: 本文大幅扩展了仿射Softmax反馈系统的认证稳定边界，指出了模型发生相变的真实位置，修正了经典理论的保守性，为理解和设计此类系统提供了更精确的理论依据。

Abstract: Softmax feedback systems are a common mathematical core of entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, logit game dynamics, population choice, and mean-field variational updates. Their central stability question is simple: when does a self-reinforcing softmax system produce a unique and globally predictable outcome? Classical theory gives a conservative answer. By treating softmax as a unit-scale response, it certifies stability only in a strongly randomized regime.
  We prove that the classical approach misses an entire stable regime and does not identify the point at which the qualitative change truly occurs. For finite-dimensional affine logit systems, the sharp dimension-free Euclidean threshold is $$β\|ΠWΠ\|_{\mathcal T\to\mathcal T}<2,$$ rather than the previously used condition, which certifies stability only while the softmax system remains safely over-regularized. Our theorem fills the previously missing pre-bifurcation regime, extending stability guarantees for affine softmax feedback systems to reward-responsive yet globally predictable systems. It enlarges the certified stability boundary for these systems and identifies where the model genuinely undergoes a phase transition.

</details>


### [106] [On the Power of Adaptivity for $\varepsilon$-Best Arm Identification in Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15663)
*Arnab Maiti,Yunbei Xu,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性老虎机中$\varepsilon$-最优臂识别的极小极大样本复杂度，证明了非自适应固定设计方法的上下界匹配，发现对多种自然动作集（超立方体、$\ell_2$球等）自适应采样仅能带来对数级改进，并首次构造出能实现多项式级改进的动作集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解线性老虎机中$\varepsilon$-最优臂识别问题的样本复杂度，特别是探究自适应采样相比非自适应方法能否带来显著改进，这是一个重要的结构性问题。

Method: (1)提出非自适应固定设计方法并证明匹配下界；(2)针对多种自然动作集分析自适应采样的改进程度；(3)构造特殊动作集实现多项式级改进，关键在于设计了$\ell_2$范数估计的自适应算法。

Result: 非自适应方法达到最优复杂度$\Theta(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2})$；对超立方体、$\ell_2$球、$m$-集、多任务多臂老虎机等，自适应仅带来对数级改进；首次构造出实现多项式级改进的动作集。

Conclusion: 首次建立了线性老虎机最优臂识别问题中自适应与非自适应方法的系统分离结果，既证明了在多种自然场景下自适应增益有限，又展示了在特定结构下可以实现多项式级改进，为理解自适应采样的价值提供了完整图景。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of $\varepsilon$-best arm identification in linear bandits. Given a compact action set $\mathcal{X}$ that spans $\mathbb{R}^d$ and an unknown reward vector $θ\in\mathbb{R}^d$, the goal is to output an arm $\widehat{x}\in\mathcal{X}$ such that $\langle \widehat{x},θ\rangle \ge \max_{x\in\mathcal{X}} \langle x,θ\rangle - \varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, using as few samples as possible.
  First, we present a non-adaptive fixed-design method with sample complexity $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, where $w(\mathcal{X})$ is a Gaussian width term dependent on $\mathcal{X}$, and we prove a matching lower bound $Ω\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ for all non-adaptive fixed-design methods.
  We then turn to adaptive sampling. We raise an important structural question: beyond the canonical basis, are there structured action sets for which adaptivity yields only logarithmic-factor improvements over the optimal non-adaptive rate? We answer in the affirmative for several natural action sets, namely the hypercube, the $\ell_2$ ball, $m$-sets, and multi-task multi-armed bandits.
  Finally, we provide the first construction of an action set $\mathcal{X}$ for which adaptivity yields a polynomial-factor improvement over every non-adaptive algorithm. A key ingredient behind this separation is an $\ell_2$-norm estimation subroutine: we design an adaptive algorithm that uses $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ samples from the unit $\ell_2$ ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and outputs an estimate $\widehat r$ satisfying $|\widehat r-\|θ\|_2|\le \varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, where $θ$ is the unknown reward vector.

</details>


### [107] [Rule2DRC: Benchmarking LLM Agents for DRC Script Synthesis with Execution-Guided Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15669)
*Jinuk Kim,Junsoo Byun,Donghwi Hwang,Seong-Jin Park,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rule2DRC大规模基准测试，用于DRC脚本生成任务，包含1000个任务和13921个评估版图；并提出了SplitTester代理，利用执行反馈生成区分性测试用例，显著提升Best-of-N选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言设计规则翻译为正确的DRC脚本需要大量人工和专业知识的投入，而现有基准测试规模小且多采用代码相似度评估而非执行正确性，现有机器学习方法要么忽略执行反馈，要么需要标注测试版图作为输入，因此需要大规模执行驱动的基准测试和更有效的脚本选择方法。

Method: 构建Rule2DRC基准测试，包含1000个规则到脚本任务和13921个评估芯片版图，提供基于DRC执行结果的评估流程；提出SplitTester测试代理，通过执行反馈生成区分性测试用例来分离此前难以区分的候选脚本，从而改进Best-of-N选择。

Result: Rule2DRC提供了大规模DRC脚本合成与调试基准，可通过执行结果直接评估功能正确性，无需将评估版图作为代理输入；SplitTester显著提升了该领域中Best-of-N脚本选择的性能。

Conclusion: Rule2DRC填补了DRC脚本合成领域大规模执行驱动评估的空白，SplitTester验证了利用执行反馈进行程序选择的有效性，为LLM在EDA领域的应用提供了新工具和方法。

Abstract: Manufacturable chip layouts must satisfy thousands of geometry-based design rules, and design rule checking (DRC) enforces them by running executable DRC scripts on layouts. Translating natural language rules into correct DRC scripts is labor-intensive and requires specialized expertise, motivating LLM agents for DRC script synthesis and debugging. However, existing benchmarks have small evaluation sets and often evaluate scripts by code similarity rather than execution correctness, and prior machine learning-based methods either ignore execution feedback or require labeled test layouts as agent's input. To this end, we introduce Rule2DRC, a large-scale benchmark for DRC script coding agents with 1,000 rule-to-script tasks and 13,921 evaluation chip layouts for execution-based scoring. Rule2DRC provides an evaluation pipeline that measures functional correctness via DRC execution outcomes without requiring evaluation layouts as input to the agent. We also propose SplitTester, a tester agent for program selection that uses execution feedback to generate discriminative test cases and separate previously indistinguishable candidate scripts, substantially improving Best-of-N selection performance in this domain. We release the code at https://github.com/snu-mllab/Rule2DRC.

</details>


### [108] [Interaction-Aware Influence Functions for Group Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15675)
*Jaeseung Heo,Kyeongheung Yun,Youngbin Choi,Sehyun Hwang,Jungseul Ok,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互感知的影响函数，通过引入成对交互项来捕捉样本间的协同或冗余效应，相比传统一阶方法能更准确地估算成组样本的影响，在模型性能追踪和指令微调数据选择任务中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影响函数在估算成组训练样本的影响时，仅简单加总单个样本的影响值，这种方式无法捕捉样本间的联合效应（如冗余性或互补性），导致估算不准确。

Method: 通过将目标函数围绕训练参数进行二阶泰勒展开，提出了一种交互感知的影响函数估计器。该估计器在标准加总项基础上，增加了一个成对交互项，用于量化两个样本对目标函数影响的对齐程度，从而刻画样本间的交互作用。

Result: 在包含逻辑回归、MLP和ResNet-9的六组数据集-模型实验中，所提方法对留组重训练结果的追踪精度在所有设置下均显著优于一阶影响函数；在针对Llama-3.1-8B的指令微调数据选择任务中，该方法在七个下游任务的五个上击败了先前基于影响函数和表示相似性的基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模样本间的交互作用，二阶影响函数相比传统一阶方法能更准确地量化成组样本的影响，证明了其在模型调试和高质量数据选择中的实用价值。

Abstract: Influence functions approximate how removing a training example changes a quantity of interest, called the target function, such as a held-out loss. To estimate the influence of a group of examples, the standard practice is to sum the individual influences of its members. However, this sum does not capture how examples jointly affect the target: a pair of examples may be redundant or complementary, but the sum cannot distinguish these cases. We propose an interaction-aware influence function that characterizes how interactions between examples influence the target. By expanding the target to second order around the trained parameters, we obtain an estimator that augments the standard sum with a pairwise interaction term that captures the alignment between two examples' effects on the target. We empirically evaluate our estimator in two settings. First, on six dataset-model pairs spanning logistic regression, MLPs, and ResNet-9, our estimator tracks leave-group-out retraining substantially better than first-order influence across all settings. Second, when used as a greedy selection rule for instruction-tuning data on Llama-3.1-8B, it beats prior influence-based and representation-similarity baselines on five of seven downstream tasks, in a regime where standard influence-based selection underperforms random selection.

</details>


### [109] [FRWKV+: Adaptive Periodic-Position Branch Interaction for Frequency-Space Linear Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15690)
*Qingyuan Yang,Dongyue Chen,Da Teng,Junhua Xiao,Jiaji Pan,Shizhuo Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FRWKV+模型，通过引入跨分支门控和自适应相位门机制，解决了频域中长期时间序列预测中复数谱实虚部信息交互弱和周期位置线索可靠性不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级预测模型在频域空间预测中面临两个挑战：复数谱的实部和虚部流包含互补信息但交互较弱；周期位置线索仅在特定数据集和预测范围内可靠，否则可能引入噪声。

Method: FRWKV+在FRWKV骨干网络基础上引入两个关键组件：(1)跨分支门控机制，在实部和虚部频率流之间交换紧凑上下文并相互调制；(2)自适应相位门机制提取周期位置上下文并生成有符号校正。同时设计自适应信任机制，在样本、变量和通道层面控制校正强度。

Result: 在匹配种子协议下的严格消融实验中，FRWKV+在FRWKV系列变体中实现了最大的MSE获胜覆盖率，在选定的周期性场景中提供显著提升。组件分析验证了周期位置上下文、有符号校正和自适应信任机制的有效性，同时也发现了简单校正规则更优的边界情况。

Conclusion: FRWKV+通过选择性周期位置分支交互机制，在保持FRWKV骨干网络效率的同时，有效利用可靠的周期位置信息改进预测性能，为中长期时间序列预测提供了一种平衡效率与准确性的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting is essential for decision making in energy, finance, transportation, and healthcare systems. Recent lightweight forecasting models improve efficiency by operating in transformed or linearized spaces, but two challenges remain in frequency-space forecasting. The real and imaginary streams of complex spectra contain complementary information that is often weakly exchanged, and periodic-position cues can help recurring patterns only when they are reliable for the current dataset and prediction horizon. To address these challenges, we propose FRWKV+, an enhanced FRWKV forecasting model for selective periodic-position branch interaction. FRWKV+ first introduces cross-branch gates that exchange compact contexts between the real and imaginary frequency streams, allowing each stream to modulate the other. It then uses the Adaptive PhaseGate mechanism to extract periodic-position context and generate signed corrections to these gates. An adaptive trust mechanism controls the correction strength at the sample, variable, and channel levels, so periodic-position information is admitted as a reliable correction signal while preserving the efficiency of the FRWKV backbone. External benchmark tables report a separately labeled FRWKV-family selected system for manuscript-level comparison, while mechanism-level claims are based on strict matched-seed FRWKV-family ablations and representative component-level ablations. Under this matched protocol, FRWKV+ achieves the largest MSE winner coverage among the family variants and provides clear gains in selected periodic regimes. Component analysis further supports the usefulness of periodic-position context, signed correction, and adaptive trust in these regimes, while revealing boundary cases where simpler correction rules remain preferable.

</details>


### [110] [SEED: Targeted Data Selection by Weighted Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15691)
*Yuan Zhang,Lifeng Guo,Junwen Pan,Chang Liu,Wenzhao Zheng,Kuan Cheng,Kurt Keutzer,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEED方法，将数据选择问题建模为相似性图上的加权独立集问题，通过节点价值校准和局部尺度标准化两个改进，实现了高质量且多样化的数据子集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据选择旨在从大规模训练语料库中识别出紧凑而信息丰富的子集。现有方法面临两个挑战：朴素的节点权重无法区分信息信号与梯度噪声；在异构域分布下，边构造会产生结构不平衡的图，导致选择偏向稀疏区域。

Method: 提出SEED方法：将数据选择建模为加权独立集问题，在相似性图上进行优化。引入两个关键改进：(1)节点价值校准，将影响估计限制在双边显著子空间中，使节点重要性基于任务相关信号而非表面统计；(2)局部尺度标准化，使边阈值适应局部邻域密度，缓解跨域分布偏移引起的图不平衡。

Result: 构建了Honeybee-Remake-SEED-200K紧凑多模态数据集。实验表明SEED在指令微调、视觉指令微调和语义分割任务上，跨多种模型家族均一致优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: SEED提供了一个鲁棒且可扩展的数据选择流程，通过统一的图视角解决了数据质量和多样性的平衡问题，在多个任务和模型上取得了优异性能。

Abstract: Data selection seeks to identify a compact yet informative subset from large-scale training corpora, balancing sample quality against collection diversity. We formulate this problem as a Weighted Independent Set (WIS) on a similarity graph, where nodes represent data samples weighted by influence, and edges connect semantically redundant pairs. This formulation naturally yields subsets that are simultaneously high-quality and diverse. However, two challenges arise in practice: naive node weights fail to distinguish informative signals from gradient noise, and edge construction under heterogeneous domain distributions produces structurally imbalanced graphs that bias selection toward sparse regions. To address these issues, we introduce two principled refinements from a unified graph perspective: (1) \textit{node value calibration} that restricts influence estimation to the bilateral salient subspace to ground node importance in task-relevant signals rather than surface-level statistics; (2) \textit{local scale normalization} that adapts edge thresholds to local neighborhood density, mitigating graph imbalance induced by cross-domain distribution shifts. Together, these components yield a robust and scalable data selection pipeline dubbed SEED. We further construct \texttt{Honeybee-Remake-SEED-200K}, a compact multimodal dataset curated by SEED. Extensive experiments show that SEED consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on instruction tuning, visual instruction tuning, and semantic segmentation across diverse model families.

</details>


### [111] [Tighter Regret Bounds for Contextual Action-Set Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15692)
*Zijun Chen,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将MVP算法扩展到动作集随回合变化的情境强化学习，证明了对抗性情境下最小极大遗憾界$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$，随机情境下遗憾界$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$，以及依赖于间隔的更优遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究回合强化学习问题中动作集随回合变化的情况，即在每回合开始时观察到该回合的可行动作集。目标是扩展MVP算法以适应这种更一般的框架，并提供强有力的理论保证。

Method: 将MVP算法自然扩展到动作集变化的强化学习框架中。分析算法在对抗性情境（adversarial contexts）和随机情境（stochastic contexts）下的性能，并推导依赖于间隔的遗憾界。

Result: 对抗性情境下获得最小极大遗憾界$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$（$L$为可能情境数）；随机情境下获得遗憾界$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$；固定情境分布下获得样本复杂度界$\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$；推导出依赖于间隔的遗憾界，当相关次优性间隔较大时可显著优于最小极大速率。

Conclusion: MVP算法能够有效处理动作集随回合变化的情况，在对抗性和随机情境下均提供强有力的理论保证，且间隔相关的遗憾界在次优性间隔较大时能显著改进最小极大速率。

Abstract: We study episodic reinforcement learning with fixed reward and transition functions, but with episode-dependent admissible action sets that are observed at the start of each episode.
