Performance is measured by cumulative regret against the episode-wise optimal value, $\sum_{k=1}^K [V^{*,M^k} - V^{π^k,M^k}]$, where $M^k$ represents the action context in the $k$-th episode. We show that the MVP algorithm naturally extends to this framework and enjoys strong theoretical guarantees. In particular, we establish a minimax regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$ for adversarial contexts, where $L$ denotes the number of possible contexts. This result implies a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$ for stochastic contexts. We further translate the stochastic regret guarantee into a sample complexity bound of $\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$ for a fixed context distribution.
  In addition, we derive a gap-dependent regret bound of \[ \widetilde O\left( \inf_{p\in [0,1)} \left( \frac{1}{Δ_{\min}^{p}} + pKΔ_{\min}^{p} \right)\log K \cdot \mathrm{poly}(S,A,H) \right), \] where $Δ_{\min}^{p}$ is the global $p$-trimmed positive-gap floor over suboptimal $(h,s,a)$ triples. This bound can substantially improve upon the minimax rate when the relevant suboptimality gaps are large.

</details>


### [112] [Going Beyond the Edge: Distributed Inference of Transformer Models on Ultra-Low-Power Wireless Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15694)
*Alexander Gräfe,Ding Huo,Johannes Berger,Marco Zimmerling,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CATS框架，首次在超低功耗无线设备上实现分布式Transformer推理，支持多达16个设备协作运行比单设备大14倍的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型是现代物联网应用的核心，但其计算和内存需求远超单个超低功耗IoT设备的能力，因此需要分布式推理方案。

Method: 提出通信感知的分布式推理框架CATS，包括：1）SomeGather剪枝通信原语，选择性广播激活列以减少通信带宽和RAM使用；2）基于SomeGather的模型并行划分方法；3）消息丢弃训练策略，模拟数据包丢失以提高模型对不可靠无线通信的鲁棒性。

Result: 在实际部署中，CATS成功在最多16个超低功耗无线设备上实现分布式Transformer推理，协作执行的模型规模可达单设备容量的14倍。

Conclusion: CATS首次将分布式Transformer推理带入超低功耗无线设备领域，通过通信感知的协同设计实现了大规模模型在资源受限设备上的高效运行。

Abstract: Transformer models are rapidly becoming a cornerstone of modern Internet of Things (IoT) applications, yet their computational and memory demands far exceed the capabilities of a single typical ultra-low-power IoT device. We present CATS, a framework for distributed transformer inference on ultra-low-power wireless devices, enabling multiple devices to collaboratively execute models far larger than what a single device can sustain. At its core, CATS is a communication-aware distributed transformer inference scheme co-designed across transformer partitioning, wireless communication and training. It employs SomeGather, a new pruned communication primitive that selectively broadcasts activation columns to reduce communication bandwidth and RAM usage without sacrificing model accuracy. Building on SomeGather, we design a partitioning method that exploits this primitive for efficient model parallelism. To cope with unreliable wireless communication, CATS employs message-dropout during training, which mimics packet losses and yields models that are robust to message loss during inference. In real-world experiments, we show that CATS brings distributed transformer inference to ultra-low-power wireless devices for the first time, with deployments on up to 16 devices that collaboratively execute transformer models up to 14 times larger than what a single device can run.

</details>


### [113] [AGOP-IxG: A Gradient Covariance Filter for Local Feature Attribution on Tabular Data, with a Controlled Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15700)
*Raj Kiran Gupta Katakam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AGOP-IxG，一种针对表格分类器的快速特征归因方法。在合成数据集上优于SHAP等主流方法，且速度提升350-1650倍，但在全局忠实度评估中与其他方法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化机器学习流程产生的模型需要对预测结果进行解释，但最广泛使用的特征归因方法（SHAP、Integrated Gradients、LIME）通常基于惯例选择而非保真度测量，因为在真实数据上缺乏ground-truth归因进行严格评估。

Method: 提出AGOP-IxG方法，通过将逐样本梯度与top-K秩截断的平均梯度外积矩阵预相乘，实现快速的逐样本归因。构建了三个合成多类表格任务（线性、稀疏非线性、基于交互），其中每个样本的ground-truth归因可通过解析或数值方法推导，并比较了AGOP-IxG与SHAP、Integrated Gradients、InputXGradient和LIME五种方法。

Result: 在合成数据集上，AGOP-IxG在所有三个数据集的Spearman秩相关性和噪声特征质量方面领先，在交互数据集的top-k精度上也表现最佳。速度方面，AGOP-IxG比SHAP快约350到1650倍。在Adult Income和Credit Card Default数据集上使用ROAR协议评估全局忠实度时，各方法的相对AUC差异在约1.7%以内，这与AGOP-IxG针对逐样本局部归因而非全局特征排序优化的特点一致。

Conclusion: AGOP-IxG是一种高效的逐样本局部归因方法，在保持竞争力的归因质量的同时大幅提升了计算效率，特别适合AutoML实践者使用，但在全局特征排名任务中优势不明显。

Abstract: Automated machine learning pipelines increasingly produce models whose predictions must be explained to end users, auditors, and downstream decision systems. The most widely used feature attribution methods (SHAP, Integrated Gradients, LIME) are typically chosen by convention rather than measured fidelity, because rigorous evaluation is impeded by the absence of ground-truth attribution on real data. We propose AGOP-IxG, a fast per-sample attribution method for tabular classifiers that pre-multiplies the per-sample gradient by a top-$K$ rank-truncated Average Gradient Outer Product matrix, and evaluate it against four widely-used baselines on a controlled tabular benchmark designed for AutoML practitioners. In Part 1, we construct three synthetic multi-class tabular tasks (linear, sparse nonlinear, interaction-based) where ground-truth attribution per sample is analytically or numerically derivable, and compare five methods: AGOP-IxG, SHAP (DeepExplainer), Integrated Gradients, InputXGradient, and LIME. AGOP-IxG leads on Spearman rank correlation and noise feature mass on all three synthetic datasets, and on top-$k$ precision on the interaction dataset. Across all settings, AGOP-IxG is approximately $350\times$ to $1{,}650\times$ faster than SHAP. In Part 2, we evaluate global faithfulness on Adult Income and Credit Card Default using the ROAR protocol; the methods cluster within $\sim 1.7\%$ relative AUC, consistent with AGOP-IxG being optimized for per-sample local attribution rather than global feature ranking.

</details>


### [114] [Differentiable Mixture-of-Agents Incentivizes Swarm Intelligence of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15706)
*Xingjian Wu,Junkai Lu,Siyu Yan,Xiangfei Qiu,Jilin Hu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了可微分智能体混合框架（DMoA），通过可微分上下文感知路由机制动态激活智能体，在推理过程中实现自适应的多智能体协作，在9个基准测试上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统（MAS）依赖于预定义或预编译的通信拓扑结构，这种静态方式限制了系统在应对动态任务需求时的灵活性与适应性。

Method: 提出了DMoA框架，设计了一个可微分的、上下文感知的路由机制，利用循环结构整合历史和上下文信息，以逐步的方式产生稀疏的智能体激活；引入预测熵作为自监督信号来优化路由过程，实现了无外部标注的高效测试时适应。

Result: 在9个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，DMoA达到了最先进的性能，同时展现出强大的效率、鲁棒性和集成能力。

Conclusion: DMoA通过动态路由和自监督优化成功实现了弹性且自适应的多智能体协作，克服了传统静态拓扑的局限性，为复杂推理任务提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have catalyzed the development of multi-agent systems (MAS) for complex reasoning tasks. However, existing MAS typically rely on pre-defined or pre-compiled communication topologies, which limits their flexibility and adaptability to dynamic task requirements. In this work, we propose Differentiable Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA), a self-evolving multi-agent framework that enables elastic and adaptive agent collaboration during inference. Instead of statically constructing workflows, DMoA dynamically routes and activates agents at each reasoning step, allowing the system to implicitly simulate diverse communication topologies and adapt to evolving demands. To achieve this, we design a differentiable, context-aware routing mechanism that leverages recurrent structures to incorporate historical and contextual information, producing sparse agent activations in a step-wise manner. Furthermore, we introduce predictive entropy as self-supervised signals to optimize the routing process, enabling efficient test-time adaptation without external annotations. Extensive experiments across 9 benchmarks demonstrate that DMoA achieves state-of-the-art performance while exhibiting strong efficiency, robustness, and ensembling capabilities.

</details>


### [115] [Bidirectional Fusion Guided by Cardiac Patterns for Semi-Supervised ECG Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15722)
*Jeonghwa Lim,Minje Park,Sunghoon Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CardioMix框架，利用心脏模式引导的双向CutMix策略，通过增强标记集和强化未标记集监督来提升ECG分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电图(ECG)波形分割对心血管诊断至关重要，但标注数据稀缺限制了深度学习模型的训练。现有半监督语义分割方法主要关注未标记数据的一致性，未能充分利用标记与未标记数据之间的信息交换。

Method: 提出CardioMix框架，采用心脏模式引导的双向CutMix策略：(1)将未标记数据的真实变化丰富到标记集中；(2)同时对未标记集施加更强的监督信号。心脏模式引导的混合确保所有增强样本在生理学上保持意义，框架设计为即插即用模块，可与多种半监督分割算法兼容。

Result: 在公共多数据集基准SemiSegECG上的广泛实验表明，CardioMix作为即插即用模块，在不同数据集和标记比例下，持续优于现有的基于CutMix的融合策略，并与多种半监督分割算法保持高兼容性。

Conclusion: CardioMix通过双向信息交换有效缓解了ECG分割中标注数据稀缺的问题，为半监督医学图像分割提供了一个通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate delineation of electrocardiogram (ECG), the segmentation of meaningful waveform features, is crucial for cardiovascular diagnostics. However, the scarcity of annotated data poses a significant challenge for training deep learning models. Conventional semi-supervised semantic segmentation (SemiSeg) methods primarily focus on consistency from unlabeled data, underutilizing the information exchange possible between labeled and unlabeled sets. To address this, we introduce CardioMix, a framework built on a bidirectional CutMix strategy guided by cardiac patterns for ECG segmentation. This approach enriches the labeled set with realistic variations from unlabeled data while simultaneously applying stronger supervisory signals to the unlabeled set, as the cardiac pattern-guided mixing ensures all augmented samples remain physiologically meaningful. Our framework is designed as a plug-and-play module, demonstrating high compatibility with various SemiSeg algorithms. Extensive experiments on SemiSegECG, a public multi-dataset benchmark for ECG delineation, demonstrate that CardioMix consistently outperforms existing CutMix-based fusion strategies across diverse datasets and labeled ratios as a plug-and-play module compatible with various SemiSeg algorithms.

</details>


### [116] [GOMA: Toward Structure-Driven Multimodal Alignment from a Graph Signal Smoothing Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15723)
*Xu Wang,Xunkai Li,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GOMA框架，利用多模态属性图(MAG)的结构信息优化冻结的视觉-语言嵌入，在七个基准测试上达到了最优检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态对齐方法（如CLIP）通常从孤立的图像-文本对中学习，忽略了实体间的关联上下文。虽然多模态属性图提供了自然的结构化语境，但利用图结构进行优化面临三大挑战：视觉、文本和跨模态关系产生不同的邻域几何结构；无限制的图传播会导致过平滑问题；需要在语义边界崩溃前保留有效信息。

Method: GOMA将冻结的多模态嵌入视为图信号，解耦了三个关键设计选择：(1)消息流向——学习模态感知的传播算子；(2)传播方式——执行有限步耦合平滑且不使用对角跨模态捷径；(3)平滑深度保留——自适应读取节点特定的平滑轨迹，在崩溃前保留有用的平滑效果。该方法采用转导式MAG检索协议，图仅作为无标签上下文使用。

Result: 在七个MAG基准测试上，GOMA达到了最优或持平最优的检索性能，并且相比最强的图基竞争对手表现出更显著的稳定性。

Conclusion: MAG结构可以有效地作为冻结多模态嵌入的后编码器使用，通过结构驱动的后对齐框架能够有效提升多模态检索性能，证明了利用图结构优化预训练多模态嵌入的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal alignment is commonly learned from isolated image-text pairs via CLIP-style dual encoders, leaving the relational context among entities largely unused. Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs), where nodes carry multimodal attributes and edges encode corpus structure, provide a natural setting for refining frozen vision-language embeddings. This refinement is challenging: visual, textual, and cross-modal relations often induce different neighborhood geometries, while unrestricted graph propagation can quickly over-smooth retrieval representations. Effectively leveraging graph context therefore requires simultaneously breaking modality-specific topological barriers, controlling the smoothing regime, and preserving informative smoothing before semantic boundaries collapse. We propose Graph-Optimized Multimodal Alignment (GOMA), a structure-driven post-alignment framework that views frozen multimodal embeddings as graph signals and addresses these requirements through a unified retrieval-oriented design. GOMA decouples three key design choices: where messages should flow, how multimodal evidence should propagate, and which smoothing depth should be retained. Concretely, it learns modality-aware propagation operators, performs finite-step coupled smoothing without diagonal cross-modal shortcuts, and adaptively reads out node-specific smoothing trajectories to preserve useful smoothing before collapse. All experiments follow a transductive MAG retrieval protocol where the graph serves only as unlabeled context and diagonal self-pair edges are removed. On seven MAG benchmarks, GOMA achieves state-of-the-art or tied state-of-the-art retrieval and remains substantially more stable than the strongest graph competitor, demonstrating that MAG structure can serve as an effective post-encoder for frozen multimodal embeddings.

</details>


### [117] [A Unified Perturbation Framework for Analyzing Leaderboard Stability and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15761)
*Hosna Oyarhoseini,Jimmy Lin,Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一扰动框架来评估大语言模型排行榜的稳健性，发现现有排行榜非常脆弱：不到1%的针对性扰动即可改变排名结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于人类成对偏好的大语言模型排行榜（如LMArena）在评估模型性能中扮演核心角色，但其排名的稳健性尚未得到充分理解，亟需系统性的分析框架来评估这些排名的可靠性。

Method: 提出了一个基于影响函数近似的统一扰动框架，研究三种比赛级别的扰动操作（删除、添加、翻转）以及选手移除，通过top-k成员资格、Kendall's tau全局排名一致性和置信区间不确定性三个指标评估排名变化，并计算归一化的数据集级别稳健性分数。

Result: 在Chatbot Arena和六个其他成对比较数据集上的实验表明，现代排行榜在所有三个目标上均不稳健：不到1%的针对性扰动即可改变排名第一的模型、降低Kendall's tau值并改变置信区间。影响分数可高效实现针对性扰动，在提升或降低特定模型排名、减少目标模型不确定性方面，比现有操纵和主动采样基线方法需要更少的操作。

Conclusion: 该框架为审计排行榜稳定性提供了实用工具，揭示了当前评估协议的脆弱性，为设计更稳健的评估方法提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Evaluation leaderboards such as LMArena play a central role in benchmarking large language models by aggregating pairwise human preferences into model rankings, yet the robustness of these rankings remains poorly understood. We present a unified perturbation framework for analyzing Bradley-Terry leaderboards under structured data modifications using influence-based approximations. Our framework studies three match-level perturbations -- Drop, Add, and Flip -- together with player removal, and evaluates their effects on top-k membership, global ranking consistency via Kendall's tau, and confidence-interval-based uncertainty. Across Chatbot Arena and six additional pairwise-comparison datasets, we show that modern leaderboards are non-robust across all three objectives: sub-1% targeted perturbations can change the top-ranked model, degrade Kendall's tau, and alter confidence intervals. Beyond robustness auditing, we show that the same influence scores enable efficient targeted perturbations, promoting or demoting specific models and reducing target-model uncertainty with fewer actions than previous manipulation and active-sampling baselines. By summarizing these effects with normalized dataset-level robustness scores, our framework provides a practical and helpful tool for auditing leaderboard stability and motivating more robust evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [118] [Continual Learning of Domain-Invariant Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15775)
*Pascal Janetzky,Tobias Schlagenhauf,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对持续学习中存在的“捷径学习”问题，首次提出了领域不变表征持续学习方法，通过结合回放训练与序列不变性对齐，显著提升了模型在未见过领域上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续学习方法仅优化域内性能，容易学习到特定领域的虚假线索（即“捷径学习”），导致在部署后面对未见过的领域时泛化能力受限。为了解决这一局限性，作者提出学习能够捕获跨领域不变结构的表征，这种不变结构通常保留了潜在的因果机制，有助于减少对特定领域线索的过拟合。

Method: 提出了一类新的持续学习方法，旨在顺序学习捕获跨领域不变结构的表征。具体而言，该方法将基于回放的训练与定制的序列不变性对齐相结合，以在学习过程中保持不变结构。这是首次将领域不变表征学习（DIRL）方法专门开发用于持续学习场景。

Result: 在面向部署的协议下进行评估，该协议测量模型在未见过的目标领域上的性能。实验涵盖了跨越视觉、医学、制造和生态学的六个基准和真实世界数据集。结果表明，本文方法在泛化到未见过的目标领域方面始终优于现有的持续学习基线。消融实验显示，将现有DIRL方法简单扩展到序列训练只能提供有限的收益。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在持续学习中引入领域不变表征学习的有效性。通过显式地学习跨领域的不变结构（因果机制），可以有效缓解捷径学习问题，显著提升模型的分布外泛化能力，为持续学习在现实世界的部署提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially over multiple domains without forgetting previously learned knowledge. However, existing CL methods optimize for in-domain performance and are therefore prone to learning spurious, domain-specific cues (``shortcut learning''), which limits generalization to unseen domains after deployment. In this paper, we address this limitation through continual learning of domain-invariant representation. We introduce a broad class of CL methods that sequentially learn representations capturing invariant structures across domains. Our methods are motivated by the observation that such invariant structures often preserve the underlying causal mechanisms, which can reduce the risk of overfitting to domain-specific cues and thus offer better out-of-domain generalization. Our proposed CL methods combine replay-based training with a tailored sequential invariance alignment to learn -- and preserve -- invariant structures over time. We evaluate our methods under a deployment-oriented protocol that measures performance on unseen target domains. Across six benchmark and real-world datasets spanning vision, medicine, manufacturing, and ecology, our methods consistently outperform existing CL baselines in terms of generalization to unseen target domains. As an ablation, we further show that naïve extensions of sequential training with existing domain-invariant representation learning (DIRL) methods provide only limited benefits. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to develop domain-invariant representation methods for CL.

</details>


### [119] [Grokking as Structural Inference: Transformers Need Bayesian Lottery Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15787)
*Kai Hidajat,Solden Stoll,Joseph An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于贝叶斯结构推断的新理论来解释Transformer延迟泛化（grokking）现象，指出注意力机制必须满足结构条件才能泛化，并设计了KL结构干预方法来加速泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论（范数最小化、特征涌现、稀疏子网络发现）无法完整解释为何Transformer在记忆训练集后需要等待数千步才实现泛化。论文关注注意力机制的独特约束：若注意力丢弃了信息性token，下游计算无法恢复，这可能延迟泛化。

Method: 将注意力机制形式化为任务依赖图上的隐式贝叶斯后验，推导出泛化需要两个可分离条件：(1)MLP容量的Goldilocks边界（与范数理论一致）；(2)新颖的贝叶斯结构条件（注意力需对每个信息性token分配足够概率质量）。提出KL结构干预方法绕过延迟。

Result: 理论上推导出反权重衰减延迟公式（结构等待时间）和反干预强度缩放定律。实验证明该贝叶斯票据方法在算法序列任务上能够分离结构与容量因素，性能匹配或优于彩票票据迁移方法。

Conclusion: 延迟泛化本质上是延迟的结构推断：MLP通过未对齐特征记忆导致损失接近零，使注意力机制缺乏结构梯度；权重衰减需要先侵蚀记忆化才能学习缺失的依赖图。这统一并扩展了现有理论，提供了控制泛化时间的新方法。

Abstract: Why does a Transformer that has memorized its training set wait thousands of steps before it generalizes? Existing accounts locate this delay in norm minimization, feature emergence, or the late discovery of sparse subnetworks. These explanations capture important parts of the transition, but ignore a constraint unique to attention-based models: if attention discards an informative token, no bounded downstream computation can recover it. We formalize attention as an implicit Bayesian posterior over the task dependency graph and prove that generalization requires two separable conditions: a familiar Goldilocks bound on MLP capacity, coinciding with norm-based theories of grokking, and a novel Bayesian structural condition requiring attention to place sufficient mass on every informative token. This decoupling explains delayed generalization as delayed structural inference. Early in training, the MLP memorizes through unaligned features, drives the cross-entropy loss near zero, and thereby starves attention of structural gradient. Weight decay must then erode memorization before the missing graph becomes learnable, yielding the known inverse-weight-decay delay, which we derive as a structural waiting time. We then prove that this explaining-away delay can be bypassed by a KL-based structural intervention, yielding an inverse-intervention-strength scaling law for the grokking time. Experiments on algorithmic sequence tasks isolate structure from capacity and show that this Bayesian ticket matches or outperforms lottery-ticket transfer.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Context-conditioned Gaussian Overbounds for Convolution-Based Uncertainty Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15789)
*Ruirui Liu,Xuejie Hou,Yiping Jiang,Hui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的学习框架，利用神经网络生成具有上下文感知能力的高斯过包络，在保证保守性的同时显著降低了传统方法的冗余度，适用于安全关键场景下的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶、航空、金融和医疗等安全关键领域，决策需要依赖保守边界而非点估计。现有的预测器级区间（如分位数回归、共形预测、方差网络或贝叶斯模型）通常不具备组合性，无法通过线性操作传播保守性。传统的高斯过包络方法虽然能通过线性操作传播保守性，但往往是全局性的、过度保守的，且难以适应特征条件的误差。因此迫切需要一种既能保证保守性又能根据上下文动态调整的边界估计方法。

Method: 提出了一种统一学习框架，训练神经网络生成上下文感知的高斯过包络（包括均值和尺度）。设计了过包络损失函数，在选定的分位数网格上强制保守性，同时使用Wasserstein风格项惩罚分布距离。在三个明确的正则性假设下，该方法能在认证区间上实现连续尾部保守性。框架支持在强制网格上进行保守的线性组合和卷积分析，当假设成立时也可在认证区间上进行。还提供了从离散到连续保守性的范围分析以及紧致域目标正则性分析。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据集（包括多路径、电离层和对流层残余误差）上进行了验证。实验结果表明，该方法在强制网格上和实验中都能保持保守性的同时，产生比传统方法更紧的边界。框架具有模态无关性，可应用于需要在动态环境中获得保守、特征条件不确定性估计的学习系统。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了上下文感知的保守边界估计，在保证保守性的前提下显著降低了边界的冗余度。方法在离散网格上具有可证明的保守性，在满足假设条件下可在连续区间上实现认证的保守性。该方法为安全关键应用提供了一种通用的、模态无关的不确定性量化解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is essential in safety-critical settings--from autonomous driving to aviation, finance, and health--where decisions must rely on conservative bounds rather than point estimates. Predictor-level intervals (e.g., from quantile regression, conformal prediction, variance networks, or Bayesian models) generally do not compose: adding two per-variable intervals need not yield a valid interval for their sum or preserve coverage. In aviation, Gaussian overbounding replaces complex error distributions with a conservative Gaussian whose tails dominate the truth, so conservatism propagates through linear operations. Yet classical overbounds are global, often overly conservative, and hard to adapt to feature-conditioned errors. We propose a unified learning framework that trains neural networks to produce context-aware Gaussian overbounds--mean and scale--with provable conservatism on a finite quantile grid and, under three explicit regularity assumptions, continuous-tail conservatism on a certified interval. Our overbounding loss enforces conservativeness at selected quantiles while penalizing distributional distance with a Wasserstein-style term. The learned bounds support conservative linear-combination and convolution analysis on the enforced grid, and on the certified interval when assumptions hold, while being less redundant than traditional methods. We provide a scoped analysis of discrete-to-continuous conservatism and compact-domain objective regularity, and validate on synthetic data and real-world datasets, including multipath, ionospheric, and tropospheric residual errors. Across these settings, the method yields tighter bounds while maintaining conservatism on the enforced grid and in experiments. The framework is modality-agnostic and applicable to learning systems that require conservative, feature-conditioned uncertainty estimates in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [121] [Shapley Neuron Values for Continual Learning: Which Neurons Matter Most?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15877)
*Mohammad Ali Vahedifar,Abhisek Ray,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于合作博弈论的Shapley Neuron Valuation (SNV)框架，通过量化神经元重要性并选择性冻结重要神经元，解决了持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在ImageNet-1k上显著优于现有无缓冲区方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在持续学习场景下面临灾难性遗忘问题，即学习新任务会导致对先前任务性能的严重下降。现有方法往往需要额外的记忆缓冲区或扩展网络架构，限制了其实际应用。因此，亟需一种能够准确评估神经元重要性、实现无缓冲区持续学习的解决方案。

Method: 提出Shapley Neuron Valuation (SNV)框架，基于合作博弈论原理量化神经元在持续学习中的重要性。该方法选择性冻结对先前任务重要的神经元，保持其他神经元的可塑性，从而在不扩展网络架构和不使用记忆缓冲区的前提下实现持续学习。神经元重要性评估通过Shapley值实现，确保了理论上的公平性和准确性。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k数据集上的实验表明，SNV方法在无缓冲区持续学习方法中表现最优。具体而言，在类增量学习场景下准确率提升+2.88%，在任务增量学习场景下准确率提升+6.46%，均相较于第二名基线方法。

Conclusion: SNV为持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题提供了一种原理性的解决方案。通过合作博弈论指导的神经元重要性评估，实现了高效的无缓冲区持续学习，无需扩展网络架构。实验验证了该方法在标准基准上的优越性能，为持续学习研究提供了新的理论视角和实践方法。

Abstract: Continual learning enables neural networks to learn tasks sequentially without forgetting previously acquired knowledge. However, neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting, where learning new tasks degrades performance on earlier ones. We address this problem with Shapley Neuron Valuation (SNV), a principled framework that quantifies Neuron importance in continual learning, grounded in cooperative game theory. SNV selectively freezes important Neurons while keeping others plastic, enabling buffer-free continual learning without expanding architecture. Experiments on ImageNet-1k show that SNV consistently outperforms existing buffer-free methods. In particular, SNV improves accuracy by +2.88% in the class incremental learning and +6.46% in the task incremental learning scenarios compared to the second baseline.

</details>


### [122] [AOT-POT: Adaptive Operator Transformation for Large-Scale PDE Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15793)
*Qitan Lv,Hong Wang,Zhongkai Hao,Wen Wu,Xuenan Xu,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应算子变换方法(AOT-POT)，通过将复杂的PDE解算子变换为对齐性更好的统一形式，仅需增加3%的参数量，在12个PDE基准测试上相对L2误差平均降低40.9%，最高降低77.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多PDE预训练方法主要通过增加模型容量来应对解算子的复杂性和结构多样性，但未改变目标解算子本身。受经典数值分析启发，作者提出将复杂多样的解算子变换为更简单、对齐性更好的形式，使其更容易被联合建模。

Method: 提出AOT-POT（自适应算子变换预训练算子Transformer）框架：将隐藏表示扩展为多个并行流，在每个子层前后自适应聚合和重新分配这些流，并通过Sinkhorn投影的双随机矩阵混合流以确保训练稳定性，从而将多样的解算子重塑为可被单一架构有效建模的统一形式。

Result: 在12个PDE基准测试上达到最优性能，仅增加3%参数量，相对L2误差最高降低77.6%（平均40.9%）。微调后在域内PDE上L2误差进一步降低最高92%，在域外PDE（预训练时未见过的类型）上降低最高89%。

Conclusion: 自适应算子变换是推进PDE基础模型发展的有效且补充性的方向，超越了单纯扩展模型容量的传统思路，证明了变换算子形式比单纯增加模型规模更具潜力。

Abstract: Pre-training neural operators on diverse partial differential equation (PDE) datasets has emerged as a promising direction for building general-purpose surrogate models in scientific machine learning. However, the inherent complexity and structural diversity of PDE solution operators make multi-PDE pre-training fundamentally challenging. Existing methods mainly address this by increasing model capacity, while leaving the target solution operators unchanged. Inspired by classical numerical analysis, we instead propose to transform complex and diverse solution operators into simpler, better-aligned forms that are easier to model jointly. Since the optimal transformation varies across PDE types, it must be adaptive and input-dependent, allowing a single neural operator to approximate an entire family of operators. We instantiate this idea as AOT-POT (adaptive operator-transformation for pre-training operator transformer), which expands hidden representations into multiple parallel streams, adaptively aggregates and redistributes them before and after each sub-layer, and mixes streams through Sinkhorn-projected doubly stochastic matrices for stable training. These mechanisms together reshape diverse solution operators into a unified form that can be effectively modeled by a single architecture. Empirically, AOT-POT achieves state-of-the-art performance on 12 PDE benchmarks with only 3\% additional parameters, reducing relative L2 error by up to 77.6\% (40.9\% on average). Fine-tuning AOT-POT further reduces L2 error by up to 92\% on in-domain PDEs and 89\% on out-of-domain PDEs (unseen types during pre-training), demonstrating that adaptive operator transformation is an effective and complementary direction for advancing PDE foundation models beyond simply scaling model capacity.

</details>


### [123] [CHoE: Cross-Domain Heterogeneous Graph Prompt Learning via Structure-Conditioned Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15888)
*Peiyuan Li,Yongqi Huang,Jitao Zhao,Dongxiao He,Di Jin,Weixiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHoE，一种基于专家网络的跨域异构图提示学习方法，通过结构条件专家和结构感知路由机制解决了现有方法在跨域场景下性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异构图提示学习（HGPL）方法主要针对域内场景设计，而实际应用中预训练和下游任务往往来自不同分布的多个域，导致现有方法在域迁移时性能显著下降，限制了其实际应用价值。

Method: 提出了CHoE方法，基于专家网络架构，在预训练阶段引入结构条件专家进行训练，在提示调优阶段采用结构感知的专家路由和负载均衡机制为每个元路径视图选择结构兼容的专家，并设计了基于提示的语义融合模块来整合多视图表示进行下游预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，CHoE在小样本跨域应用中持续提升性能，在所有基线方法中表现最优。

Conclusion: CHoE成功解决了异构图提示学习在跨域场景中的性能下降问题，证明了专家网络架构结合结构感知机制在跨域异构图学习中的有效性，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Graph Prompt Learning (HGPL)has emerged as a promising paradigm for bridging the gap between the objectives of pre-training foundation models and their downstream applications in heterogeneous graph settings. However, existing HGPL methods are primarily designed for in-domain scenarios, whereas real-world deployments often span multiple domains, and the data used for pre-training and downstream tasks may originate from different distributions. Consequently, the applicability of current HGPL approaches is limited to in-domain settings, and their performance typically degrades when application domains shift. To address this serious limitation, we develop CHoE, a cross-domain HGPL method built upon an expert network. During pre-training, we introduce and train structure-conditioned experts, and during prompt tuning, we adopt a structure-aware expert routing and load balancing mechanism to select structurally compatible experts for each meta-path view. In addition, we design a prompt-based semantic fusion module to integrate representations across multiple views for downstream prediction. Extensive experiments show that CHoE consistently improves performance in few-shot cross-domain applications, outperforming all baseline approaches.

</details>


### [124] [Martingale Neural Operators: Learning Stochastic Marginals via Doob-Meyer Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15806)
*Kai Hidajat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了鞅神经算子（MNO），利用Doob-Meyer分解定理将半鞅分解为可预测的漂移项和不可预测的鞅项，从而高效准确地恢复随机偏微分方程解的概率分布结构，在保持算子学习单次推理效率和分辨率不变性的同时，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子作为确定性代理模型，在处理随机偏微分方程时会坍缩到条件均值，丢失对不确定性量化至关重要的方差和尾部结构；而恢复这些结构通常需要蒙特卡洛模拟或生成模型，牺牲了算子范式的核心优势——单次推理效率和分辨率不变性。

Method: 基于Doob-Meyer定理，将半鞅分解为可预测漂移项和不可预测的零均值鞅项，构建鞅神经算子（MNO）架构。MNO将初始条件直接映射到终端分布的条件均值和协方差，通过漂移式均值和低秩因子$B_φ$参数化，其中$B_φ^\top B_φ$在构造上保持半正定；实验中采用高斯残差实例化。

Result: 在1D随机偏微分方程、粗糙波动率和2D算子任务上，MNO在φ⁴场论上Wasserstein距离降低达120倍，在随机Burgers方程上降低68倍；在相同训练预算下推理速度比条件扩散基线快约3倍；在2D任务上，MNO在零样本分辨率迁移和湍流任务上与FNO相当。

Conclusion: MNO成功解决了神经算子在随机偏微分方程中丢失概率结构的问题，在保持算子学习核心优势的同时显著提升了不确定性量化的准确性，但对准确定性系统（如Gray-Scott）仍是失败模式，存在一定局限性。

Abstract: Neural operators excel as deterministic surrogates, but inevitably collapse to the conditional mean when applied to stochastic PDEs, discarding the variance and tail structure upon which uncertainty quantification depends. Recovering this structure typically requires Monte Carlo rollouts or grafted generative models, both of which surrender the one-shot efficiency and resolution invariance that define the operator paradigm. To resolve this, we draw on the Doob-Meyer theorem, which establishes that any semimartingale fundamentally decomposes into a predictable drift and an unpredictable, zero-mean martingale. Translating this theorem into an architectural prior, we introduce the Martingale Neural Operator (MNO). MNO maps an initial condition directly to the conditional mean and covariance of the terminal law, parameterized by a drift-like mean and a low-rank factor $B_φ$ with $B_φ^\top B_φ$ positive semi-definite by construction. For our experiments, we use a Gaussian residual instantiation. Across 1D SPDEs, rough volatility, and 2D operator tasks, MNO reduces Wasserstein distance by up to $120\times$ on $φ^4$ field theory and $68\times$ on stochastic Burgers, evaluating $\sim 3\times$ faster than a conditional diffusion baseline at matched wall-clock training budgets. On 2D tasks, MNO is comparable to FNO on zero-shot resolution transfer and turbulent flow, while quasi-deterministic systems such as Gray-Scott remain a failure mode.

</details>


### [125] [LoCO: Low-rank Compositional Rotation Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15916)
*An Nguyen,Jaesik Choi,Anh Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LoCO（Low-rank Compositional Orthogonal fine-tuning），一种通过低秩斜对称矩阵和组合旋转链构建正交变换的新型参数高效微调方法，在扩散Transformer、视觉Transformer和语言模型等多个领域表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法（如低秩适应）虽然实现了参数效率，但在保持预训练表示的几何结构方面存在局限性。正交变换能够更好地保持表示的几何结构，但现有正交微调方法在高维特征空间中计算复杂度高，难以实际应用。

Method: 提出LoCO方法，利用低秩斜对称矩阵构建正交变换，通过组合旋转链实现参数效率；设计近似方案使得组合旋转能够完全并行计算，从而在高维特征空间中实现实用性；在保持低计算复杂度的同时维持正交性并控制近似误差。

Result: 在扩散Transformer微调、视觉Transformer适应和语言模型适应等多个领域进行了验证；与现有的正交和非正交方法相比，LoCO展现出更优或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: LoCO成功解决了现有PEFT方法在保持预训练表示几何结构方面的不足，通过低秩斜对称矩阵和并行化的组合旋转链实现了高效的正交微调，为大规模基础模型的适应提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as an critical technique for adapting large-scale foundation models across natural language processing and computer vision. While existing methods such as low-rank adaptations achieve parameter efficiency via low-rank weight updates, they are limited in their ability to preserve the geometric structure of pretrained representations. We introduce Low-rank Compositional Orthogonal fine-tuning (LoCO), a novel PEFT method that constructs orthogonal transformations through low-rank skew-symmetric matrices and compositional rotation chains. We propose an approximation scheme that enables fully parallel computation of compositional rotations, making the approach practical for high-dimensional feature spaces. Our method maintains low computational complexity while maintaining orthogonality with controlled approximation error. We validate LoCO across diverse domains, including diffusion transformer fine-tuning, vision transformer adaptation, and language model adaptation. Our method demonstrates superior or competitive performance compared to both existing orthogonal and non-orthogonal methods.

</details>


### [126] [Intrinsic Wasserstein Rates for Score-Based Generative Models on Smooth Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15822)
*Guoji Fu,Taiji Suzuki,Wee Sun Lee,Atsushi Nitanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在紧致光滑流形上，分数生成模型(SGM)能达到内在Wasserstein-1样本指数，且网络参数具有多项式环境维度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数的生成模型在高维环境空间中训练，但许多数据分布实际上支撑在低维非线性结构上。现有理论未能充分解释SGM在流形数据上的性能，特别是如何利用数据的低维结构来克服维数灾难。

Method: 1. 将分数逼近分析分为两个机制：大噪声切向单元机制和小噪声投影中心、去高斯化拉普拉斯机制。2. 关键技术创新是通过有限内在锚点和高斯-牛顿迭代实现ReLU最近投影坐标，而非将流形投影近似为黑盒高维光滑映射。3. 对紧致d维光滑流形M⊂[0,1]^D(d>2)上严格正的β-Hölder密度进行分析。

Result: 方差保持SGM估计器达到内在Wasserstein-1样本指数$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(D^{\mathcal{O}_β(d)}n^{-(β+1)/(d+2β)})$（忽略对数因子、显式几何和密度因子）。完整的非渐近界显式分离了有限阶几何包络、Hölder半径、密度下界、环境依赖和有限阶修正项。对于具有多项式受控几何和密度下界的族，构造的分数网络参数具有多项式环境依赖性。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解SGM在流形数据上的性能提供了严格的理论保证，证明了SGM能够有效利用数据的低维流形结构，避免了环境维度的指数依赖，为高维空间中低维流形数据的生成模型设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Score-based generative models are trained in high-dimensional ambient spaces, yet many data distributions are supported on low-dimensional nonlinear structures. We prove that, for compact $d$-dimensional smooth manifolds $\mathcal{M} \subset [0,1]^D$ with $d > 2$ and $β$-Hölder densities strictly positive on $\mathcal{M}$, a variance-preserving SGM estimator attains the intrinsic Wasserstein--1 sample exponent $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(D^{\mathcal{O}_β(d)}n^{-(β+1)/(d+2β)})$, up to logarithmic factors and explicit geometry and density factors. The full nonasymptotic bound explicitly isolates the finite-order geometry envelope, Hölder radius, density lower bound, ambient dependence, and finite-order correction terms. The analysis separates score approximation into a large-noise tangent-cell regime and a small-noise projection-centered, de-Gaussianized Laplace regime. The key technical ingredient is a ReLU implementation of nearest-projection coordinates via finite intrinsic anchors and Gauss--Newton iterations, rather than approximating the manifold projection as a black-box high-dimensional smooth map. Consequently, for families with polynomially controlled geometry and density lower bounds, the constructed score-network parameters have polynomial ambient dependence.

</details>


### [127] [When and Why Adversarial Training Improves PINNs: A Neural Tangent Kernel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15959)
*Yuan-dong Cao,Chi Chiu SO,Jun-Min Wang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对对抗训练物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的新分析框架，揭示了GAN中的判别器如何影响PINNs的训练动力学，为对抗训练的有效性提供了理论依据，并提出了一种新的高效训练算法，显著提升了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在训练高频率或多尺度解时存在谱偏差、刚性和精度差等问题，对抗训练虽有效但机理不明，因此需要建立理论框架解释其有效性并改进算法。

Method: 基于GAN判别器对PINNs训练动力学的影响这一关键观察，提出新的分析框架，统一分析GAN变体在PINNs训练中的作用，并推导出新的实用高效训练算法。

Result: 新方法显著减少了PINNs训练的病态问题，模型性能优于替代方法，精度通常提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该框架为对抗训练PINNs提供了理论支撑，提出的算法在实际应用中表现出色，为高精度PINNs训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are powerful surrogates for differential equations but are notoriously difficult to train due to spectral bias, stiffness, and poor accuracy on high-frequency or multiscale solutions. Adversarial training based on generative adversarial networks (GANs) has recently gained surprisingly strong empirical results in improving training, but the underlying mechanisms remain elusive. To this end, we propose a new analysis framework for adversarially trained PINNs, based on the key observation of how the discriminator in GANs can influence the training dynamics of PINNs. The framework first provides a much needed theoretical grounding to why and when adversarial training is effective in PINNs, then presents a unified analysis of GANs variants in such training, and finally leads to a new, practical, efficient training algorithm for PINNs. Empirical results demonstrate that our method can significantly reduce the pathology of PINNs training, thereby providing better models with superior performances, often several magnitudes more accurate than alternative methods.

</details>


### [128] [From Observed Viability to Internal Predictive Approximation: A Single-Subject Latent-Space Analysis of Gait Dynamics Under Occlusal Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15862)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在单名帕金森受试者中，利用前馈神经网络成功在潜在空间内近似复现了由不同咬合状态探针引起的步态纵向转化模式，但仅限于单样本的方法学验证，不涉及临床预测或因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应生物力学系统可能表现出相似的步态表现，但在潜在组织和纵向行为上存在差异。研究旨在探索观察到的步态纵向变化能否在预测性潜在空间框架内被近似模拟，从而为未来多受试者预测模型的构建提供方法学桥梁。

Method: 采用探索性单受试者设计，对一名帕金森参与者进行间隔十一周的两次步态记录。测试六种咬合状态探针（自然咬合、张口脱离、强力咬合、两种正中关系下的垂直距离增加，以及一种下颌前伸的垂直距离增加）。利用主成分分析构建PC1-PC2潜在表征，训练一个简化监督前馈神经网络以近似观察到的纵向转化（M1-M2）。

Result: 主要分析显示，模型成功保留了三种正中关系条件的位移层级顺序（OC3 < ONL < OC2.5）。扩展的六探针分析同样保留了位移模式的整体结构。留出（Held-out）M2测试和留一条件分析显示出依赖于条件的近似变异性。

Conclusion: 研究结果不支持普适性预测、治疗优越性、咬合因果效应或临床可行性预测。结论仅限于在此单受试者数据集中，观察到的纵向潜在转化可在模型内部被近似，这为未来多受试者预测模型奠定了方法学基础。

Abstract: Adaptive biomechanical systems may show similar observable gait performance while differing in latent organization and longitudinal behavior. This study examines whether an observed longitudinal transformation of gait organization can be approximated within a predictive latent-space framework, without claiming clinical prediction or causal occlusal effects.
  Using an exploratory single-subject design in a Parkinsonian participant, gait was recorded with instrumented insoles during two sessions separated by eleven weeks. Six occlusal observational probes were tested: natural occlusion, open-mouth disengagement, strong clenching, two vertical-dimension increases in centric relation, and one vertical-dimension increase with mandibular protrusion. Principal Component Analysis was used to construct a PC1--PC2 latent representation. A simplified supervised machine-learning model, implemented as a feed-forward neural network, was trained to approximate the observed M1--M2 transformation.
  The primary analysis focused on the three centric-relation conditions and tested whether the displacement hierarchy could be reproduced. The model preserved the ordering OC3 < ONL < OC2.5. The extended six-probe analysis also preserved the global structure of the exploratory displacement pattern, with OC3 and OC3P closely grouped and the highest displacements associated with OC2.5 and open-mouth disengagement. Held-out M2 and leave-condition-out analyses showed condition-dependent approximation variability.
  These findings do not establish generalizable prediction, therapeutic superiority, causal occlusal effects, or clinical viability forecasting. They support only the restricted conclusion that observed longitudinal latent transformations can be internally approximated within this single-subject dataset, providing a methodological bridge toward future multi-subject predictive viability models.

</details>


### [129] [Constrained latent state modeling: A unifying perspective on representation learning under competing constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15995)
*Gwenolé Quellec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出约束潜在状态建模（CLSM）作为统一视角，通过识别预测充分性、最小性、时间相干性等核心属性及其内在权衡，将现有方法重新解释为在设计空间中执行不同约束子集，为解决潜在表示的可识别性问题提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在学习潜在表示时依赖于分散且隐式的假设，潜在表示通常从约束不足的目标中学习，导致多个表示满足同一目标，造成结构和解释的模糊性，缺乏统一的视角来理解不同建模方法之间的关系。

Method: 提出约束潜在状态建模（CLSM）作为统一视角，识别核心属性（预测充分性、最小性、时间相干性、观测兼容性、对干扰因素的不变性、结构约束），证明这些属性通过基本权衡内在耦合，并通过此视角重新审视主要建模家族。

Result: 展示了现有方法可被解释为执行不同约束子集，占据共同设计空间的不同区域；将可识别性缺失等挑战重新解释为约束不足公式的后果，而非孤立的技术限制。

Conclusion: CLSM提供了原则性框架，使设计选择显式化，分析权衡，指导开发更具可解释性、鲁棒性和任务对齐的潜在状态模型，为未来研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning latent representations from complex data is central to modern machine learning, spanning temporal, multimodal, and partially observed systems. In such settings, representations are better understood as latent states capturing underlying system dynamics, rather than as mere compressed summaries of observations. Yet current approaches remain fragmented, relying on distinct -- and often implicit -- assumptions about what these states should represent. We argue that this fragmentation reflects a more fundamental limitation: latent representations are typically learned from underconstrained objectives that fail to specify the properties that meaningful latent states should satisfy. As a result, multiple representations can satisfy the same objective, leading to ambiguity in their structure and interpretation. While many of the underlying principles have been explored in isolation, their interactions have not been explicitly formalized. In this work, we propose constrained latent state modeling (CLSM) as a unifying perspective. We identify a set of core properties -- predictive sufficiency, minimality, temporal coherence, observation compatibility, invariance to nuisance factors, and structural constraints -- and show that they are intrinsically coupled through fundamental trade-offs. Revisiting major modeling families through this lens, we show that existing approaches can be interpreted as enforcing different subsets of constraints, thereby occupying distinct regions of a common design space. This perspective reframes persistent challenges such as lack of identifiability as consequences of underconstrained formulations, rather than isolated technical limitations. More broadly, CLSM provides a principled framework to make design choices explicit, to analyze trade-offs, and to guide the development of more interpretable, robust, and task-aligned latent state models.

</details>


### [130] [Ti-iLSTM: A TinyDL Approach for Logic-Level Anomaly Detection in Industrial Water Treatment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15874)
*Mandar Joshi,Farzana Zahid,Judy Bowen,Matthew M. Y. Kuo,Valeriy Vyatkin,Emil Karlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ti-iLSTM框架，一种基于微型深度学习的轻量级异常检测方法，用于资源受限的PLC设备，能够有效检测工业水处理系统中的逻辑层欺骗异常，在SWaT数据集上F1-score达到0.983。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业水处理系统作为安全关键的网络物理基础设施，由于连接性增强而面临网络威胁。特别是逻辑层欺骗异常能够保持数值上的合理性，同时破坏控制过程中的因果关系，这类攻击难以通过基于阈值的监控检测，或需要重型服务器导向的异常检测模型。

Method: 提出了TinyDL-based incremental LSTM (Ti-iLSTM)框架，通过优化长短期记忆网络(LSTM)的内存和空间占用，结合逻辑感知监督，实现轻量级的设备端逻辑层异常检测，适用于资源受限的可编程逻辑控制器(PLC)。

Result: 在公开的SWaT数据集上，优化模型实现了高检测性能，F1-score为0.983，ROC-AUC为0.998；在WADI数据集上的部署式验证确认了所提轻量级框架在单一数据集之外的适用性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将逻辑感知监督与微型深度学习序列学习相结合，能够创建高效且准确的异常检测方法，适用于工业环境中资源受限的可编程逻辑控制器。

Abstract: Industrial Water Treatment Systems (IWTS) are safety critical cyber-physical infrastructures and due to increased connectivity, these systems are exposed to cyber threats that can manipulate process behaviour without creating obvious devices outliers. In particular, logic-layer deception anomalies can preserve numerically plausible measurements while breaking expected cause-and-effect relationships in the control process. These attacks are difficult to detect using threshold-based monitoring or require heavy server-oriented anomaly detection models. This paper explores the potential of Tiny Deep Learning (TinyDL) to provide lightweight on-device logic-level anomaly detection for resource constrained Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs). We propose a novel framework, TinyDL-based incremental LSTM (Ti-iLSTM) which optimises the memory and space foot print of Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), to detect logic-layer inconsistencies in Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) based Industrial Water Treatment Systems (IWTS). Experiments on the publicly available SWaT dataset show that the optimised model achieves high detection performance (F1-score=0.983 and ROC-AUC=0.998). A deployment-style validation on the WADI dataset confirms that the proposed light-weight framework remains applicable beyond a single dataset. The research demonstrates that combining logic-aware supervision with Tiny Deep Learning (TinyDL) sequence learning creates an efficient and accurate anomaly detection suitable for resource constrained Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs) in industrial environments.

</details>


### [131] [Looped SSMs: Depth-Recurrence and Input Reshaping for Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16048)
*Mónika Farsang,Ramin Hasani,Daniela Rus,Radu Grosu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在状态空间模型(SSMs)中，深度循环（参数跨层共享）和输入重塑均能独立提升性能；参数量为k的循环SSM可匹配或超越参数量为k·L的标准SSM，输入重塑可提升1-6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态空间模型(SSMs)天然具有序列维度的循环特性，但深度循环（跨层重复使用同一模块）这一在循环Transformer中已应用的技术，在SSM模型族中尚未被探索。研究者旨在探究深度循环是否能作为SSMs的有效设计策略。

Method: 在四种SSM架构(LRU, S5, LinOSS, LrcSSM)和六个时间序列分类基准上，对比参数量为k的循环SSM（迭代L次）与参数量为k·L的标准SSM的性能；同时探索输入重塑策略：对低维输入拼接时间步，对高维输入展平并重新分块特征-时间维度。

Result: 循环SSM在所有测试中持续匹配或优于标准SSM，且处于更小的假设空间中；输入重塑策略在所有模型上带来1-6%的准确率提升（5个随机种子验证）；两种技术可独立改进且效果叠加。

Conclusion: 深度循环与序列循环正交且独立有益，参数共享作为归纳偏置可简化优化过程；深度循环和输入重塑是SSM在时间序列任务中两个独立且未被充分探索的设计维度。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) are inherently recurrent along the sequence dimension, yet depth-recurrence - reusing the same block repeatedly across layers, as recently applied in looped transformers - has not been explored in this model family. We show that a looped SSM with $k$ parameters iterated $L$ times consistently closely matches or outperforms a standard SSM with $k \cdot L$ independent parameters across four architectures (LRU, S5, LinOSS, LrcSSM) and six time series classification benchmarks, despite operating within a strictly smaller hypothesis space, as we formally establish. Since the larger model contains the looped model as a special case, this dominance cannot be explained by expressivity and instead points to parameter sharing across depth as a beneficial inductive bias that simplifies optimization. These results demonstrate that depth-recurrence is orthogonal to sequence-recurrence and independently beneficial. We further show that input reshaping is an equally neglected design axis: concatenating timesteps for low-dimensional inputs, or flattening and rechunking the joint feature-time dimension for high-dimensional ones, yields accuracy gains of 1-6% across all models, confirmed over 5 random seeds. Both techniques provide standalone improvements that compound when combined, suggesting that depth and input reshaping are two independent and underexplored design axes for SSMs on time series.

</details>


### [132] [Ada-Diffuser: Latent-Aware Adaptive Diffusion for Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16054)
*Fan Feng,Selena Ge,Minghao Fu,Zijian Li,Yujia Zheng,Zeyu Tang,Yingyao Hu,Biwei Huang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ada-Diffuser，一个因果扩散模型框架，通过显式推断潜在动态来解决生成式决策中潜在变化因素被忽视的问题，支持规划和策略学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将决策制定视为序列建模问题的生成模型方法（如扩散模型）常忽视具有演化动态的潜在因素，而这些因素对环境转移、奖励结构和高层智能体行为至关重要。显式建模这些隐藏过程对精确的动态建模和有效的决策制定是必要的。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，将潜在动态推断显式地融入生成式决策中；理论上证明了在温和条件下可从小的时间观察块中识别潜在过程；引入了Ada-Diffuser，这是一个因果扩散模型，同时学习观察交互的时间结构和潜在动态，并利用其进行规划和控制；采用模块化设计支持规划和策略学习任务。

Result: 理论分析证明了潜在过程的可识别性；在模拟控制和机器人基准测试上的实验表明，该方法在准确的潜在推断和自适应策略学习方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: Ada-Diffuser通过显式建模潜在动态，能够适应动态、奖励和潜在动作中的潜在变化，在生成式决策任务中实现了更准确的潜在推断和更有效的策略学习。

Abstract: Recent work has framed decision-making as a sequence modeling problem using generative models such as diffusion models. Although promising, these approaches often overlook latent factors that exhibit evolving dynamics, elements that are fundamental to environment transitions, reward structures, and high-level agent behavior. Explicitly modeling these hidden processes is essential for both precise dynamics modeling and effective decision-making. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that explicitly incorporates latent dynamic inference into generative decision-making from minimal yet sufficient observations. We theoretically show that under mild conditions, the latent process can be identified from small temporal blocks of observations. Building on this insight, we introduce Ada-Diffuser, a causal diffusion model that learns the temporal structure of observed interactions and the underlying latent dynamics simultaneously, and furthermore, leverages them for planning and control. With a modular design, Ada-Diffuser supports both planning and policy learning tasks, enabling adaptation to latent variations in dynamics, rewards, and latent actions. Experiments on simulated control and robotic benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness in accurate latent inference and adaptive policy learning.

</details>


### [133] [Practical Validity Conditions for Byzantine-Tolerant Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15887)
*Mélanie Cambus,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijević,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEB有效性条件及其松弛版本c-MEB有效性，用于联邦学习中的鲁棒聚合，相比传统凸有效性条件具有更好的维度独立性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的凸有效性条件虽然在理论上提供强保证，但存在维度依赖的鲁棒性问题，且排除了许多实用的聚合规则，不适用于现代联邦学习系统。需要开发更适合的几何有效性条件。

Method: 引入最小包围球(MEB)有效性条件及其乘法松弛版本c-MEB有效性；提出最优MinMax-MEB聚合规则；证明标准聚合器（最小直径平均、中心点和几何中位数）的松弛MEB保证；建立MEB有效性与现有有效性条件的系统映射关系。

Result: 精确MEB有效性仍然受限于有限的鲁棒性；松弛的c-MEB有效性在诚实客户端占多数时(n>2t)可实现；MinMax-MEB规则达到c<√2的界；证明了多种标准聚合器具有松弛MEB保证；建立了MEB有效性与凸有效性、松弛凸有效性和盒有效性之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: 松弛MEB有效性连接了分布式计算中的有效性条件和拜占庭容错聚合规则，为凸有效性提供了实用的替代方案，形成了一个系统性的几何有效性条件框架。

Abstract: Robust aggregation is the core operation in Byzantine-tolerant federated learning. To ensure the quality of aggregation independently of data distribution or attacks, validity conditions are needed. They provide geometric guarantees of where the output of the aggregation must lie. The widespread convex validity requires the output to lie in the convex hull of the honest vectors. Although this guarantee is strong in theory, it is poorly suited to modern federated learning systems, as it has dimension-dependent resilience and excludes many practical aggregation rules.
  We introduce the minimum enclosing ball (MEB) validity condition for robust aggregation, as well as its multiplicative relaxation, $c$-MEB validity, where $c$ is a constant. We show that exact MEB validity still suffers from limited resilience, while relaxed $c$-MEB validity is achievable if a majority of clients is honest, i.e. $n>2t$. We give an optimal MinMax-MEB rule for the relaxed condition with the bound $c<\sqrt{2}$ and prove explicit relaxed-MEB guarantees for standard aggregators including minimum-diameter averaging, medoid and geometric median. Finally, we relate MEB validity to convex, relaxed-convex and box validity studied in prior literature, thus providing a systematic map of geometric validity conditions for Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that relaxed MEB validity connects validity conditions in distributed computing and Byzantine-tolerant aggregation rules, and offers a practical alternative to convex validity.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-level Self-supervised Pretraining on Compositional Hierarchical Graph for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16088)
*Xiayu Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Hou-biao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MolCHG，一种基于组合层次图的多层级自监督预训练框架，通过将分子结构组织为跨三个语义层级的四类节点，并引入与原子图并行的键图，实现了在九个MoleculeNet基准测试中七个数据集上的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子图自监督预训练方法大多仅在单一结构粒度上操作，并将键信息视为辅助边属性而非独立的语义层，限制了模型对分子结构的多层次理解能力。

Method: 提出了MolCHG框架，构建组合层次图将分子结构组织为跨三个语义层级的四类节点（原子、键、片段、图）；引入与原子图并行运作的键图，将键级信息提升为独立演化的节点表示；设计了三个层级特定的预训练目标：原子-键跨视图对比任务、片段级官能团预测任务和图级结构预测任务。

Result: 在九个MoleculeNet基准测试中，MolCHG在七个数据集上取得了最佳性能，涵盖分类和回归任务，在其余数据集上与最强基线保持竞争力；消融实验证实多层级监督信号具有互补性，每个组件都对整体性能有贡献。

Conclusion: 通过将键信息提升为独立的语义层并设计多层次预训练目标，MolCHG有效提升了分子性质预测的性能，证明了多粒度、多语义层级建模在分子图预训练中的重要性。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining on molecular graphs has emerged as a promising approach for molecular property prediction, yet most existing methods operate at a single structural granularity and treat bond information as auxiliary edge attributes rather than as an independent semantic layer. In this work, we propose MolCHG, a multi-level self-supervised pretraining framework built upon a novel Compositional Hierarchical Graph that organizes molecular structure into four types of nodes across three semantic levels. By introducing a bond graph that operates in parallel with the atom graph, our architecture elevates bond-level information to independently evolving node representations, enabling fragment nodes to aggregate atom-level and bond-level semantics on an equal footing. We design three level-specific pretraining objectives: an atom-bond cross-view contrastive task that aligns the atom-view and bond-view representations within each fragment, a fragment-level functional group prediction task to inject domain-relevant chemical knowledge, and graph-level structure prediction tasks to encode global molecular topology. Experiments on nine MoleculeNet benchmarks demonstrate that MolCHG achieves the best performance on seven datasets across both classification and regression tasks, remaining competitive with the strongest baselines on the rest. Ablation studies further confirm that the multi-level supervision signals are complementary and that each component contributes to the overall performance.

</details>


### [135] [Centralized vs Decentralized Federated Learning: A trade-off performance analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16089)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Guilain Leduc,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过Fedstellar模拟器、MNIST数据集和MLP分类器，对集中式(CFL)、分散式(DFL)和半分散式(SDFL)三种联邦学习架构进行了系统的实验对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管联邦学习作为一种保护数据隐私的分布式协作训练范式在物联网时代备受关注，但现有研究很少对集中式、分散式和半分散式三种架构进行实验对比。缺乏对这三种架构在性能指标上的权衡及各自优缺点的深入理解，无法为不同应用场景选择合适的FL架构提供指导。

Method: 采用Fedstellar模拟器作为实验平台，使用MNIST数据集作为基准数据，选择多层感知机(MLP)作为分类器模型，对三种联邦学习架构（CFL、DFL、SDFL）进行系统的实验对比分析。

Result: 通过实验分析揭示了三种联邦学习架构在不同性能指标上的表现差异、各自的优势与局限性，以及性能指标之间的权衡关系，为FL架构选择提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了联邦学习架构对比分析的研究空白，为根据应用需求选择合适的联邦学习架构提供了实验依据和理论指导，有助于推动联邦学习在实际场景中的部署和应用。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while preserving data privacy especially with the huge increase amount of data due to the adoption of technologies which contributes to the growing number of IoT devices. Storing this amount of data centrally is challenging due to issues like limited communication, privacy, and regulations. FL can be Centralized (CFL), Decentralized (DFL), and Semi-decentralized (SDFL). Choosing the right FL architecture depends on the application's needs. However, very few research studies have experimentally compared these three types of architectures to not only understand the respective strengths and limitations, but also trade-offs between different performance indicators. This paper overcome this lack of analysis, conducting experimental analyses using the Fedstellar simulator, MNIST dataset, and MLP classifier.

</details>


### [136] [From Layers to Networks: Comparing Neural Representations via Diffusion Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15901)
*Atharva Khandait,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将扩散几何与多视角学习引入神经表示分析，提出了多尺度的Centered Kernel Alignment和Distance Correlation变体，以及跨层融合的多层相似性度量方法，在多个基准测试上达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经表示相似性度量方法缺乏多尺度分析能力，且主要局限于层与层之间的比较。本文旨在利用扩散几何框架来刻画数据集在多尺度下的几何结构，并引入多视角学习工具，从而实现对神经网络表示的更深入分析。

Method: 核心方法是发现基于表示相似性矩阵（RSMs）的相似性度量可以等价地表示为行随机马尔可夫矩阵形式，这使得扩散几何工具得以应用。具体包括：1）利用马尔可夫转移矩阵的t次幂开发多尺度Centered Kernel Alignment和Distance Correlation变体；2）通过交替扩散（alternating diffusion）将多个层的马尔可夫矩阵融合为单一算子，实现跨层和网络间的表示相似性比较。

Result: 在包含14种架构、7个数据集和三个不同领域的表示相似性（ReSi）基准测试上进行了广泛数值实验。所提方法在语言和视觉任务中均实现了最先进的准确性和输出相关性，在分布外数据的额外基准测试上也展现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将扩散几何框架与多视角学习技术整合到神经表示分析领域，提出的多尺度相似性度量和多层融合方法不仅提供了分析神经网络表示的新视角，而且在实际应用中表现出优越的性能，为理解神经网络的内部表示机制提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Diffusion geometry is a manifold learning framework that uses random walks defined by Markov transition matrices to characterize the geometry of a dataset at multiple scales. We use diffusion geometry for neural representations, incorporating tools from multi-view learning into this field for the first time. Our key technical observation is that a broad class of similarity measures based on representational similarity matrices (RSMs) admits a closed-form equivalent formulation in terms of row-stochastic Markov matrices, opening the door to manipulations from diffusion geometry. As a first application, we develop multi-scale variants of Centered Kernel Alignment and Distance Correlation, which utilise the $t^{th}$ power of the underlying transition matrix to probe the data geometry at adjustable diffusion scales. Going further, we introduce variants of these measures which fuse the Markov matrices of several layers via alternating diffusion into a single operator that captures the network's joint sample geometry, allowing similarity to be computed across multiple layers and shifting the comparison from layer-to-layer to network-to-network. We perform extensive numerical experiments, evaluating our measures on the Representational Similarity (ReSi) benchmark comprising 14 architectures trained on 7 datasets across three different domains. Our methods achieve SoTA results in accuracy and output correlation for both language and vision tasks across different models. We furthermore show SoTA performance on an additional benchmark evaluating on out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [137] [Federated Imputation under Heterogeneous Feature Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16099)
*Imane Hocine,Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Gregoire Danoy,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedHF-Impute框架，解决了联邦学习中异构特征空间下的数据填充问题，通过全局特征图实现跨客户端知识迁移，在部分特征重叠场景下显著提升了填充精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习方法大多假设特征模式对齐，但在表格数据场景中，客户端往往只观察到部分重叠的特征子集。在这种异构特征空间中，参数平均方法（如FedAvg）在弱重叠或不相交特征组之间传输的信息很少，限制了联邦数据填充的有效性。

Method: 提出了FedHF-Impute框架，该框架将结构性特征不可用与传统缺失分离，使用共享的全局特征图通过消息传递机制在统计相关的特征之间传播信息。这使得即使特征在本地从未被联合观察到，也能实现间接的跨客户端知识迁移，同时保持标准的联邦通信方式。

Result: 在SECOM和AirQuality数据集上的模拟部分模式重叠实验中，FedHF-Impute相比联邦学习基线方法分别将填充精度（RMSE）提升了26.9%和8.4%；在PhysioNET数据集上达到与最佳基线相当的性能，差异仅为0.3%。

Conclusion: FedHF-Impute成功解决了联邦学习中异构特征空间下的数据填充挑战，通过全局特征图和消息传递机制实现了有效的跨客户端知识迁移，在保持标准联邦通信的同时显著提升了填充性能。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training across decentralized clients, but most methods assume aligned feature schemas, an assumption that rarely holds in tabular settings where clients observe only partially overlapping feature subsets. In these heterogeneous feature spaces, parameter-averaging methods (e.g., FedAvg) transfer little information across weakly overlapping or disjoint feature groups, limiting their effectiveness for federated imputation. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedHF-Impute}, a federated imputation framework that separates structural feature unavailability from conventional missingness and uses a shared global feature graph to propagate information across statistically related features through message passing. This enables indirect cross-client knowledge transfer, even when features are never jointly observed locally, while preserving standard federated communication. Under simulated partial schema overlap on the SECOM and AirQuality datasets, FedHF-Impute improves imputation accuracy (RMSE) over FL baselines by 26.9\%, and 8.4\% respectively, while achieving comparable performance on PhysioNET, with only a 0.3\% difference relative to the best baseline.

</details>


### [138] [Context-aware Entity-Relation Extraction for Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15904)
*Inoussa Mouiche,sherif Saad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTiKG框架，通过融合SecureBERT+上下文嵌入与领域本体知识，解决了网络安全知识图谱构建中的错误传播问题，在实体识别和关系抽取任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全知识图谱构建方法在从非结构化CTI报告中提取实体-关系三元组时，受限于报告结构复杂、领域专业术语和语义歧义，流水线架构存在错误传播问题，导致抽取准确率低且泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出Context-aware Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graph (CTiKG)框架，采用混合NLP模型架构，融合SecureBERT+上下文嵌入与领域本体专家知识，实现威胁实体及其关系的准确抽取与分类，有效减少误分类和缓解级联错误。

Result: 在包含21种STIX 2.1实体类型的DNRTI-AUG-STIX2数据集上，NER任务提升3-4%，关系抽取任务提升最高8%（基于精确率、召回率和F1分数）；在DNRTI和STUCCO基准数据集上进一步验证了框架的鲁棒性和实用价值。

Conclusion: CTiKG框架有效解决了网络安全知识图谱构建中的关键挑战，显著提升了威胁情报抽取性能，并通过开源数据集促进该领域的可复现性与进一步研究。

Abstract: Cybersecurity Knowledge Graphs (CKGs) unify diverse Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) sources into structured, queryable formats, offering scalable solutions for automating proactive and real-time security responses. Their increasing adoption has significantly enhanced the workflow and decision-making efficiency of security professionals. However, constructing CKGs requires extracting entity-relation triples from unstructured CTI reports, a task hindered by complex report structure, domain-specific language, and semantic ambiguity. As a result, existing pipeline-based approaches often suffer from error propagation, reducing extraction accuracy and limiting generalizability. This paper introduces the Context-aware Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graph (CTiKG) framework, a pipeline architecture designed to accurately extract and classify threat entities and their relationships from CTI reports. CTiKG incorporates hybrid NLP models that leverage SecureBERT+ contextual embeddings and expert knowledge from a domain ontology to reduce misclassifications and mitigate cascading errors. Experiments on the DNRTI-AUG-STIX2 dataset, which comprises 21 entity types aligned with STIX 2.1, demonstrate significant improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, yielding 3-4% gains in NER and up to 8% in RE performance, based on precision, recall, and F1-score. Additional validation on DNRTI and STUCCO benchmarks confirms the framework's robustness and practical applicability. All datasets, including the curated DNRTI-AUG-STIX2, are released on GitHub to foster reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [139] [Attention Dispersion in Dynamic Graph Transformers: Diagnosis and a Transferable Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16112)
*Jinhao Zhang,Kangfei Zhao,Qiuhao Zeng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断出动态图Transformer在时序分布偏移下存在“注意力分散”问题，通过引入差分注意力机制成功解决该问题，提出的DiffDyG模型在多个基准测试中达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的架构虽已成为连续时间动态图（CTDG）学习的主流范式，但在时序偏移数据集上的性能仍然受限。作者旨在探究其失败原因并寻求改进方案。

Method: 通过控制消融实验诊断出“注意力分散”是核心失效模式，即时序偏移导致注意力对比度减弱、分布过度分散，无法聚焦于携带关键预测信号的历史邻居节点。据此提出简单有效的修复方案：用差分注意力替代标准注意力，以抑制共模注意力并放大独特的令牌级信号。基于此构建了参考实现DiffDyG。

Result: 将差分注意力添加到三个代表性CTDG Transformer基线模型中，均取得持续性能提升，且收益集中在高偏移数据集上。注意力层面的测量证实了该机制降低了注意力熵并增加了关键节点上的注意力质量。在9个基准测试和三种负采样协议下，DiffDyG均达到SOTA性能，且在偏移最大的数据集上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 时序分布偏移导致的注意力分散是动态图Transformer的关键瓶颈，差分注意力机制能有效解决该问题，显著提升模型在分布偏移场景下的鲁棒性与性能，具有重要的理论与实践价值。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have become the dominant paradigm for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph (CTDG) learning, yet their performance remains limited on temporally shifted datasets. In this work, we identify attention dispersion as a shared failure mode of dynamic graph Transformers under temporal distribution shift. Through controlled ablation contrasting structurally and temporally distinguished historical neighbors against random ones, we show that prediction depends on a class of critical nodes that carry consistently more predictive signal than arbitrary neighbors. However, existing Transformers fail to focus on these nodes even when they are present in the input, as temporal shift weakens attention contrast and produces overly dispersed attention distributions. This diagnosis suggests a simple and transferable fix: replace standard attention with differential attention, which suppresses common-mode attention and amplifies distinctive token-level signals. When added to three representative CTDG Transformer baselines, differential attention consistently improves performance, with gains concentrated on high-shift datasets. Attention-level measurements further confirm the mechanism, showing reduced attention entropy and increased attention mass on critical nodes. Building on these findings, we introduce DiffDyG, a reference implementation combining differential attention with standard input encodings. Across 9 benchmarks and three negative sampling protocols, DiffDyG achieves SOTA performance, with especially large gains on the most shifted datasets.

</details>


### [140] [Entropy Across the Bridge: Conditional-Marginal Discretization for Flow and Schrödinger Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16126)
*Bruno Trentini,Dejan Stancevic,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于熵率的训练自由推理时间调度器，通过在边界附近密集采样，在低函数评估预算下显著提升了流模型和桥模型的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的推理预算下，流匹配和薛定谔桥模型的采样质量高度依赖于函数评估点的时间分布。现有方法通常采用启发式或继承自单端点扩散的离散化方案，未能充分考虑双端点桥接几何结构，导致样本质量次优。

Method: 推导了一个条件-边缘熵率目标函数，用于桥感知的离散化，该目标将端点条件的桥几何与边缘流演化分离。基于此，构建了一个无需训练的推理时间调度器。对于高斯布朗桥，该熵率具有闭式解且呈U型分布，从而指导生成边界密集的非均匀时间网格。

Result: 在二维桥/流模型上，10步ODE-Heun的MMD指标相比线性网格提升18.1%，SDE-Heun提升22.7%。在EDM/CIFAR-10上，5步FID达到最优（186.3），优于线性（200.5）和余弦（238.0）调度。在AlphaFlow蛋白质生成任务中，该方法在低NFE区间表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 熵率调度作为一种实用的低预算资源分配信号，能够有效提升高维桥接模型和流模型的采样性能，验证了边界密集型时间离散化策略的优越性。

Abstract: For a fixed flow-based generative model under a small inference budget, sample quality can depend strongly on where the sampler spends its few function evaluations. Flow matching and Schrödinger bridges define probability paths, yet their inference grids are usually heuristic or inherited from one-endpoint diffusion. We derive a conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective for bridge-aware discretization, separating endpoint-conditioned bridge geometry from marginal flow evolution, and use it to build a training-free entropic inference-time scheduler from first principles. For Gaussian Brownian bridges this rate is closed-form and U-shaped, motivating boundary-heavy nonuniform grids. On trained two-dimensional bridge/flow models, the estimated profile recovers the predicted shape and improves 10-step ODE-Heun MMD over linear by 18.1%, with a paired 22.7% SDE-Heun improvement in the same low-NFE sweep. On EDM/CIFAR-10, the entropic time-discretization gives the best tested five-step FID (186.3 \pm 4.0 versus 200.5 \pm 2.9 for linear and 238.0 \pm 5.3 for cosine). On AlphaFlow protein generation, entropic conditional-marginal (cond-marg) scheduling shows advantage in low-NFE regimes on both CAMEO22 and ATLAS benchmarks. These results support entropy-rate scheduling as a practical low-budget allocation signal for high-dimensional bridge and flow samplers.

</details>


### [141] [A Retrieval-Enhanced Transformer for Multi-Step Port-of-Call Sequence Prediction in Global Liner Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15937)
*Yanzhao Su,Fang He,Yineng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连接约束与检索增强的深度学习框架(CCRE)，用于多步港口停靠序列预测，在首港和三步预测准确率上分别达到72.3%和61.4%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的船舶多步港口停靠序列预测对战术资源编排和物流效率至关重要。然而，现有方法面临两个主要挑战：一是航行时刻表不可靠，二是AIS数据仅能提供最近下一港口的可见性，无法支持长期预测。

Method: 提出CCRE框架，核心创新包括：(1)检索增强历史编码器，从全球海事数据库检索上下文相似的历史航行案例，解决长尾航线数据稀疏和路由歧义问题；(2)基于Transformer的轨迹编码器，通过交叉注意力机制执行自适应"中间融合"，短期依赖实时运动学特征，长期依赖历史上下文；(3)采用自回归Transformer解码器，结合Scheduled Sampling和Gumbel-Softmax松弛技术，将预测建模为联合序列生成问题，减轻误差累积；(4)引入拓扑掩码强制执行海事网络可达性约束，排除不可行的运营路线。

Result: 在全球数据集上的评估表明，CCRE实现了72.3%的首港预测准确率和61.4%的平均三步预测准确率，相比CatBoost和LSTM基线模型，平均性能提升分别达到12.6%和11.3%。案例研究进一步验证了模型的可扩展性和跨国际贸易航线捕获复杂路由模式的能力。

Conclusion: CCRE框架成功解决了海事领域多步港口序列预测的核心难题，通过检索增强机制有效弥补了数据稀疏问题，融合机制实现了短期精确性与长期稳定性的平衡，拓扑约束确保了预测结果的可操作性，为海事物流资源编排提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Accurate multi-step port-of-call sequence prediction is vital for tactical resource orchestration and logistical efficiency. However, existing methods struggle with unreliable voyage schedules and the inability of AIS data to provide visibility beyond the immediate next port. To address this, this study proposes a Connectivity-Constrained and Retrieval-Enhanced (CCRE) deep learning framework. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation, CCRE introduces a retrieval-enhanced historical encoder that queries a global maritime database for contextually similar navigational precedents. Transforming these scenarios into candidate-level semantic representations compensates for data sparsity in long-tail routes and resolves routing ambiguities. Integrating this with a Transformer-based trajectory encoder, the architecture executes adaptive "middle fusion" via cross-attention. This dynamically shifts predictive reliance from real-time kinematics for short-term accuracy to historical context for long-term strategic stability. To ensure sequence-level coherence, forecasting is formulated as a joint sequence generation problem using an autoregressive Transformer decoder enriched with Scheduled Sampling and Gumbel-Softmax relaxation. This mitigates error accumulation, while topology masks strictly enforce maritime network reachability to eliminate operationally infeasible routes. Evaluated on a global dataset, CCRE achieves a 72.3% first-destination accuracy and a 61.4% average three-step accuracy, outperforming baselines like CatBoost and LSTM by average margins of 12.6% and 11.3%, respectively. Case studies further corroborate the model's scalability and ability to capture complex routing patterns across diverse international trade lanes.

</details>


### [142] [Navigating Potholes with Geometry-Aware Sharpness Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16134)
*Simon Dufort-Labbé,Mehrab Hamidi,Razvan Pascanu,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Damien Scieur,Aristide Baratin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLQR+SAM方法，通过结合SAM与基于LLQR框架的学习型预处理器，实现了在慢速学习到的损失景观几何结构上进行快速锐度校正，从而提升模型泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的锐度感知最小化（SAM）在扰动参数时对所有方向一视同仁，忽略了损失景观的底层几何结构，这可能导致对某些方向的不必要扰动或扰动不足。

Method: 提出了LLQR+SAM，将SAM与从LLQR框架（一种二阶优化方法）获得的预处理器相结合。该预处理器通过稀疏更新并以慢速指数移动平均的形式维护，捕捉平滑的、低分辨率的损失景观几何图像；SAM扰动则在此基础上以更快的速度探测曲率，形成双时间尺度结构。

Result: 理论和实验表明，预处理器能够放大SAM在“平均平坦但局部尖锐”方向上的逃逸信号，同时保持宽且平坦的盆地稳定。在标准视觉和序列建模基准测试中，LLQR+SAM相比单独使用SAM或LLQR均表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 慢速学习到的几何结构与快速锐度校正具有真正的互补性，LLQR+SAM成功利用这一双时间尺度结构改进了优化过程。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) encourages flat minima by perturbing parameters along directions of high loss curvature, but treats all parameter directions uniformly, ignoring the underlying loss geometry. We introduce LLQR+SAM, which combines SAM with a learned preconditioner obtained from the recently proposed LLQR framework, a second-order method that recasts steepest descent as a layerwise linear-quadratic regulator problem. The preconditioner is updated sparsely and maintained as a slow exponential moving average, so it captures a smoothed, low-resolution picture of the loss landscape geometry. The SAM perturbation then operates on top of this learned geometry, probing curvature at a faster timescale. We show that this two-timescale structure is not merely a computational convenience: theoretically, the preconditioner amplifies the SAM escape signal in directions that are flat under the average geometry but locally sharp (potholes). Wide, flat basins, by contrast, remain stable. Empirically, LLQR+SAM gives consistent gains over both SAM and LLQR alone across standard vision and sequence modeling benchmarks, supporting the view that slow learned geometry and fast sharpness correction are genuinely complementary.

</details>


### [143] [Surrogate Neural Architecture Codesign Package (SNAC-Pack)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16138)
*Jason Weitz,Dmitri Demler,Benjamin Hawks,Aaron Wang,Nhan Tran,Javier Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SNAC-Pack，一个面向FPGA部署的开源硬件感知神经架构协同设计AutoML框架，通过多目标全局搜索和硬件代理模型，在高能物理和量子计算应用上实现了资源高效、性能优异的模型自动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经架构搜索(NAS)方法通常仅优化精度或使用与硬件成本相关性较差的代理指标(如BOPs)，无法满足FPGA部署中对LUT、DSP、FF、BRAM和延迟等多维资源约束的实际需求，导致搜索结果与实际硬件部署存在较大差距。

Method: 提出SNAC-Pack框架，采用Optuna和NSGA-II进行多目标全局搜索，通过SQLite存储支持跨计算节点的并行工作进程；引入硬件代理模型提供资源和延迟估计，避免昂贵的综合过程；结合量化感知训练(QAT)和迭代幅度剪枝的局部搜索阶段进行模型压缩；最终通过hls4ml库将模型综合为FPGA固件；提供YAML配置和可选的代理前端支持新数据集。

Result: 在大型强子对撞机喷流分类和超导量子比特读出任务上验证了SNAC-Pack的有效性，发现的紧凑架构在任务指标上匹配或超越强基线，同时降低了FPGA资源利用率；在量子比特读出案例中，将设计空间探索过程从数月的手动调优缩短至数小时的自动搜索。

Conclusion: SNAC-Pack成功填补了NAS方法与FPGA实际部署需求之间的差距，通过硬件感知的多目标优化和端到端自动化流程，显著提升了神经架构在FPGA上的部署效率，为高能物理和量子计算等领域的硬件加速AI应用提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) is a powerful approach for automating model design, but existing methods often optimize for accuracy alone or rely on proxy metrics such as bit operations (BOPs) that correlate poorly with hardware cost. This gap is particularly large for FPGA deployment, where cost is dominated by a multi-dimensional budget of lookup tables, DSPs, flip-flops, BRAM, and latency. We present the Surrogate Neural Architecture Codesign Package (SNAC-Pack), an open-source AutoML framework for hardware-aware neural architecture codesign and end-to-end FPGA deployment. SNAC-Pack runs a multi-objective global search with Optuna and NSGA-II, loading trials to a shared SQLite store that enables parallel workers across compute nodes. A hardware surrogate model outputs per-trial resource and latency estimates, avoiding the synthesis cost that would otherwise dominate the search loop. A local search stage then applies quantization-aware training (QAT) together with iterative magnitude pruning in a combined compression loop, after which the final model is synthesized to FPGA firmware via the hls4ml Python library. A YAML configuration and an optional agentic frontend let users run the pipeline on new datasets without modifying the framework. We demonstrate SNAC-Pack on jet classification at the Large Hadron Collider and superconducting qubit readout, discovering compact architectures that match or exceed strong baselines on the task metric while reducing FPGA resource utilization and, in the qubit readout case, reducing the design space exploration process from months of manual fine-tuning to hours of automated search.

</details>


### [144] [Entropy-Based Characterisation of the Polarised Regime in Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15965)
*Peter Clapham,Lisa Bonheme,Marek Grzes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于平均表征熵的信息论分类方法识别VAE潜在变量的活跃/被动/混合维度，无需依赖高斯先验假设，在多种模型中验证有效，并发现被动维度在适当归一化后可改善下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别VAE潜在变量活跃维度的准则依赖于高斯先验假设，限制了其在变分模型和特定先验中的适用性。需要一种不依赖特定先验的方法来准确分类潜在变量的极化状态。

Method: 提出基于平均表征熵的信息论分类准则，从理论上证明熵通过熵-方差界与KL最小化耦合，并将该准则与Bonheme的活跃/被动条件相关联。同时指出仅依靠平均熵无法可靠区分活跃和混合维度，需要方差表征的额外信号。

Result: 在β-VAE、可识别VAE、最小体积自编码器和L2正则化自编码器上评估该方法，在存在极化机制时能够一致地恢复该机制。发现被动维度在潜在码适当归一化时能对下游任务产生小但一致的改进。

Conclusion: 基于熵的分类方法能有效识别VAE潜在变量的极化状态，不依赖特定先验假设。被动维度的坍缩往往是尺度问题而非绝对信息移除，为理解和利用潜在空间提供了新视角。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) often exhibit a polarised regime in which latent variables separate into active, passive, and mixed subsets. Existing criteria for identifying active dimensions depend on a Gaussian prior, limiting their applicability to variational models and specific priors. We propose a simple information-theoretic classification of the polarised regime based on the entropy of the mean representation. We show theoretically how this entropy couples to KL minimisation through entropy--variance bounds, and we relate the resulting criterion to Bonheme's active/passive conditions. We also clarify a key limitation: entropy of the mean alone cannot reliably distinguish active from mixed dimensions without additional signals from the variance representation. Empirically, we evaluate the entropy criterion on $β$-VAEs, identifiable VAEs, Least-Volume Autoencoders, and L2-regularised autoencoders, and find that it consistently recovers a polarised regime when such a regime is present across the model classes studied. Finally, we show that passive dimensions can yield small but consistent improvements on downstream tasks when latent codes are appropriately normalised, suggesting that collapse is often a matter of scale rather than absolute information removal.

</details>


### [145] [Layer Equivalence Is Not a Property of Layers Alone: How You Test Redundancy Changes What You Find](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16234)
*Gabriel Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分了Transformer层压缩中两种不同的"等价性"测试协议——替换和交换，发现它们在判断层是否可剪枝时可能产生数倍差异的结果分歧，建议在移除或合并层之前同时评估两种swap-KL指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者在判断两个Transformer层是否"等价"以进行压缩时，常混淆两种不同的测试：替换测试检查一个层的映射能否替代另一个层，交换测试检查两个层交换位置后是否近似可交换。作者发现这两种输出导向的swap-KL探测方法在预训练Transformer上可能产生显著分歧，影响了层剪枝决策的安全性判断。

Method: 作者在多个检查点和架构上测量了替换和交换两种协议：在Pythia训练轨迹（410M和1.4B参数规模）上追踪从初始化到收敛过程中两种协议的差距变化；在8B规模下使用匹配的WikiText-2约束测试Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B模型，比较两种协议指导的层移除效果。该方法仅需无标签前向传播即可完成诊断。

Result: 实验发现：（1）在Pythia训练轨迹中，替换-交换差距从初始化到收敛逐渐增大；（2）在8B规模下，Qwen3-8B进入分歧模式：在相同层预算下，交换指导的移除比替换指导的安全数倍；（3）而Llama-3.1-8B即使交换KL较低，两种协议的剪枝代价相同，表明指标差距与移除效果并非一一对应；（4）同一评估器下，协议差距可使判断哪些层可安全剪枝的结果产生数倍变化，尤其当替换距离较高时。

Conclusion: 在层移除或合并之前，应在目标检查点上同时评估两种swap-KL分数进行诊断，因为替换和交换协议可能给出不同的层剪枝安全性判断。这种诊断方法仅需无标签前向传播，计算成本低，能够更准确地识别可安全剪枝的层。

Abstract: When researchers ask whether two transformer layers are "equivalent" for compression, they often conflate distinct tests. Replacement asks whether one layer's map can substitute for another's in place; interchange asks whether two layers approximately commute when their positions are swapped. Both are output-grounded swap-KL probes, but they need not agree: on pretrained transformers the protocol gap can change which layers look safe to prune by several-fold under the same evaluator, especially when replacement distances are high.
  We measure both protocols across checkpoints and architectures. On a Pythia training trajectory (410M and 1.4B), the replacement-interchange gap grows from initialization to convergence. Under one matched WikiText-2 contract at 8B scale, Qwen3-8B enters a divergent regime: interchange-guided removal is several-fold safer than replacement-guided at the same layer budgets, while Llama-3.1-8B ties the two protocols for pruning cost even though interchange KL is lower, showing metric gaps need not map one-to-one to removal. Before layer removal or merging, score both swap-KLs on the target checkpoint; the diagnostic requires only unlabeled forward passes.

</details>


### [146] [Accelerated Gradient Descent for Faster Convergence with Minimal Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16017)
*Manuel Graca,L. Miguel Silveira,Arlindo Oliveira,Frank Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CT-AGD优化方法，通过捕捉局部曲率加速一阶方法，在保持与Adam相当的存储和计算开销下，平均减少33%的训练轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对深度学习训练中的非凸优化问题，旨在加速传统一阶优化方法的收敛速度，同时避免自适应方法（如Adam）可能带来的额外开销。

Method: CT-AGD是一种通用的加速过程，通过有限差分商显式捕捉局部曲率来加速一阶方法，并开发了启发式策略以缓解随机小批量训练引入的噪声和偏差。

Result: 实验表明CT-AGD在达到与基线一阶方法相同精度的情况下，平均减少了33%的训练轮数，且存储和计算开销与Adam相当。

Conclusion: CT-AGD是一种高效的非凸优化加速方法，能够在不增加显著开销的情况下显著提升深度学习训练效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we present CT-AGD (Curvature-Tuned Accelerated Gradient Descent), an optimization method for non-convex optimization problems in deep learning training tasks.
