CT-AGD is a general boosting procedure that accelerates first-order methods by explicitly capturing the local curvature using finite-difference quotients, and the development of heuristics aimed at mitigating noise and bias introduced by stochastic mini-batch training. CT-AGD has a comparable storage and computational overhead as adaptive gradient methods such as Adam. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that CT-AGD achieves the same level of accuracy as the baseline first-order methods, yet reduces the required training epochs by 33% on average.

</details>


### [147] [Variational Autoregressive Networks with probability priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16020)
*Piotr Białas,Piotr Korcyl,Tomasz Stebel,Dawid Zapolski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将物理先验作为训练起点的框架，解决了蒙特卡洛方法中神经网络采样器训练困难的问题，显著提升了对Ising模型和Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃模型的采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛方法在相变附近存在临界慢化问题，导致自相关时间急剧增加。虽然基于神经网络的采样器可以缓解此问题，但由于通用机器学习架构忽略了物理对称性，导致模型训练困难，需要从头学习这些对称性。

Method: 在现有整合自旋-自旋相互作用策略的基础上，提出利用先验概率分布作为训练起始点的框架，将物理先验知识直接嵌入模型，取代原本“空白板”式的通用模型。

Result: 在Ising模型和Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃模型上的实验表明，物理先验的引入显著降低了训练负担，使得更大尺寸离散自旋系统的模拟成为可能。

Conclusion: 相比通用的“空白板”模型，物理信息驱动的先验分布能更有效地解决训练难题，为大规模物理系统的蒙特卡洛模拟提供了新思路。

Abstract: Monte Carlo methods are essential across diverse scientific fields, yet their efficiency is frequently hampered by critical slowing down-a sharp increase in autocorrelation times near phase transitions. Although deep learning approaches, such as neural-network-based samplers, have been proposed to alleviate this issue, they face another serious problem: the difficulty of training the models. This difficulty partially stems from the overly general nature of original machine-learning architectures, which often ignore underlying physical symmetries and force networks to relearn them from scratch. In this paper, we demonstrate that incorporating physical priors into the model significantly enhances performance. Building upon existing strategies that integrate spin-spin interactions, we propose a framework that utilizes a prior probability distribution as a starting point for training. Our results for the Ising model, as well as for the Edwards-Anderson spin glass model, suggest that moving away from `blank slate' models in favor of physics-informed priors reduces the training burden and facilitates the simulation of larger system sizes in discrete spin models.

</details>


### [148] [Mind Dreamer: Untethering Imagination via Active Latent Intervention on Latent Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16030)
*Shaojun Xu,Xiaoling Zhou,Yihan Lin,Yapeng Meng,Xinglong Ji,Luping Shi,Rong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mind Dreamer框架，通过主动潜在干预解决MBRL中历史束缚问题，在DeepMind Control Suite上实现1.67倍平均加速，稀疏奖励任务达8.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）存在历史束缚问题：潜在想象通常从观测状态初始化，导致世界模型的流形发现速度快于策略的稀疏奖励优化，形成学习不对称性。

Method: 提出Mind Dreamer（MD）框架，实现主动潜在干预（ALI）：1）通过最小化全局中继流形期望自由能（R-EFE）重构发现过程；2）使用对抗生成器从学习到的生成器采样初始状态，合成非连续潜在跳跃至认知盲点；3）推导中继价值函数（RVF）和中继不确定性函数（RUF）解决空间断裂的信用分配悖论；4）证明不确定性传播需要二次折扣γ²，建立形式化认知视界。

Result: 理论上，MD近似方差最小化重要性采样器，扩展流形的谱间隙，减少到达关键瓶颈状态的到达时间。实验上，在DeepMind Control Suite上相比DreamerV3实现1.67倍平均加速，稀疏奖励任务中达到8.8倍加速。

Conclusion: Mind Dreamer通过突破马尔可夫连续性约束，成功解决了MBRL的历史束缚问题，实现了从观测状态到认知盲点的非连续潜在跳跃，显著提升了样本效率和稀疏奖励任务的性能。

Abstract: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) leverages latent imagination for sample efficiency, yet remains constrained by Historical Tethering: imagination is typically initialized from observed states. This creates a learning asymmetry, where the world model's manifold discovery outpaces the policy's sparse-reward optimization. We propose Mind Dreamer (MD), a framework that operationalizes Active Latent Intervention (ALI) to transcend Markovian continuity. MD reformulates discovery as the minimization of a global Relay Manifold Expected Free Energy (R-EFE); by sampling initial states from a learned generator $s_0 \sim p_{gen}(\cdot)$ rather than the historical buffer, MD utilizes an adversarial generator to synthesize non-continuous latent jumps to epistemic blind spots that are physically plausible yet cognitively challenging. To resolve the credit assignment paradox across these spatial ruptures, we derive the Relay Value Function (RVF) and Relay Uncertainty Function (RUF). These potentials treat synthesized anchors as counterfactual intermediary states, propagating pragmatic and epistemic value through a principled Bellman-style formulation. Notably, we prove that uncertainty propagation across discontinuities necessitates a quadratic discount $γ^2$, establishing a formal epistemic horizon. Theoretically, MD approximates a variance-minimizing importance sampler that expands the manifold's spectral gap, reducing the hitting time to critical bottleneck states. Empirically, MD achieves a 1.67$\times$ average speedup over DreamerV3 on DeepMind Control Suite, reaching 8.8$\times$ in sparse-reward tasks.

</details>


### [149] [SAFE Quantum Machine Learning with Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16067)
*Ying Chen,Paolo Giudici,Vasily Kolesnikov,Paolo Recchia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于振幅编码的高维变分量子分类器，通过可学习的前编码层实现稳定分类，在保持竞争力的预测性能的同时，展现出更优的鲁棒性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景中，传统的变分量子分类器在处理高维数据时存在稳定性不足的问题。现有方法难以在预测性能与模型可靠性（包括鲁棒性和可解释性）之间取得平衡，因此需要一种能够实现面向稳定性的安全机器学习的新方法。

Method: 通过振幅编码在高维深度表示上操作变分量子分类器，引入可学习的经典预编码层进行稳定化。结合归一化振幅嵌入与有界量子可观测量，构建结构化且平滑的假设类。同时，采用基于Cramer von Mises散度的SAFE-AI指标，从准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性三个维度对模型可靠性进行一致评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该量子模型在预测性能上可与强经典基线模型竞争，同时展现出更均衡的SAFE可靠性特征。具体表现为：对噪声具有更强的鲁棒性，以及在结构化特征移除下保持更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路为安全关键环境中的面向稳定性SAFE学习提供了具有原则性的机制，是构建高可靠性机器学习系统的潜在有效途径。

Abstract: We propose a variational quantum classifier operating on high dimensional deep representations via amplitude encoding, stabilized by a learnable classical pre encoding layer.By combining normalized amplitude embeddings with bounded quantum observables, the resulting model induces a structured and smooth hypothesis class with controlled sensitivity to input variations. Model reliability is assessed using SAFE-AI metrics derived from the Cramer von Mises divergence, enabling consistent evaluation across accuracy, robustness, and explainability dimensions. Empirical results show that the proposed quantum model provides competitive predictive performance compared with strong classical baselines while exhibiting a more balanced SAFE reliability profile, with improved robustness to noise and stability under structured feature removal. These findings suggest that variational quantum circuits offer a principled mechanism for stability oriented SAFE learning in safety critical settings.

</details>


### [150] [ITGPT: Generative Pretraining on Irregular Timeseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16069)
*Antoine Honoré,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ITGPT模型，这是一种基于注意力机制的架构，能够直接处理不规则采样、包含缺失值的多模态时间序列数据，通过结合自监督学习和GPT训练目标，在标签稀缺时有效利用无标签数据，在医疗和预测性维护任务上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列回归模型难以利用大量带标签的多模态数据，特别是在数据不规则采样或包含缺失值的情况下（常见于医疗和预测性维护领域）。虽然基于Transformer的大语言模型在文本等结构化数据上表现出色，但缺乏高效处理不规则采样多模态时间序列数据的灵活性。

Method: 引入了ITGPT（一种基于注意力机制的架构），该架构允许同时使用自监督学习（SSL）损失和类GPT目标进行训练。该模型专门设计用于处理多模态、不规则采样的时间序列数据，无需重采样、特征融合或显式数据填补即可直接处理原始数据。

Result: 在医疗任务（TIHM数据集）和预测性维护任务（CompX数据集）上进行了评估。结果表明，ITGPT在不需重采样、特征融合或显式数据填补的情况下达到了最先进的性能。此外，当标签稀缺时，ITGPT通过SSL和GPT训练有效利用无标签数据，性能优于纯监督方法。

Conclusion: ITGPT代表了在实际推理任务中高效利用大规模、非结构化时间序列数据的重要一步，证明了结合SSL和GPT训练目标能够有效处理不规则采样的多模态时间序列数据，尤其在标签稀缺场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Timeseries regression models often struggle to leverage large volumes of labeled multimodal data, particularly when the data are irregularly sampled or contain missing values. This is common in domains like healthcare and predictive maintenance, where data are collected from unreliable sources, and labeling requires expert knowledge or costly equipments. Transformer-based large language models have proven effective on structured data such as text through self-supervised learning (SSL) and generative pretraining (GPT) frameworks. However, such models lack the flexibility to efficiently process irregularly sampled multimodal timeseries data. In this paper, we introduce ITGPT, an attention-based architecture designed for handling multimodal, irregularly sampled timeseries by allowing training with both SSL losses and GPT-like objectives. We evaluate its performance on a healthcare task with the TIHM dataset, and a predictive maintenance task with the CompX dataset. Our results demonstrate that ITGPT achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring resampling, feature fusion or explicit data imputation. Furthermore, when labels are scarce, ITGPT effectively leverages unlabeled data through SSL and GPT training, outperforming the purely supervised approach. This represents an important step towards efficiently using large and unstructured timeseries datasets for practical inference tasks.

</details>


### [151] [MIND: Decoupling Model-Induced Label Noise via Latent Manifold Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16081)
*Dayong Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MIND框架，通过潜在流形解耦解决由预训练模型自动标注产生的系统性标签噪声问题，在大规模3D数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖预训练模型和基础模型的自动标注范式引入了模型诱导的标签噪声，这种噪声源于标注器的归纳偏置，表现为与局部特征流形紧密耦合的系统性错误。现有依赖全局转移矩阵的方法无法充分拟合这些结构化模式，而学习实例特定矩阵在数学上难以处理。

Method: 提出模型诱导噪声解耦（MIND）框架。核心是潜在流形解耦，将高维噪声流形解耦为可处理的、依赖子空间的分量。设计潜在解耦估计器（LDE），将样本动态投影到具有一致错误模式的潜在结构聚类中，从而在无需真实锚点的情况下实现噪声可识别性。

Result: 采用分层协议进行评估：从CIFAR-100上的受控噪声到大规模真实世界3D数据集（S3DIS、ScanNet）的结构压力测试。实验表明，MIND在这些复杂基准上显著优于最先进的方法，并能有效纠正视觉-语言模型（如OpenSeg）的零样本幻觉。

Conclusion: MIND作为一个理论严谨的框架，成功解决了模型诱导噪声的挑战，证明了其作为基础模型鲁棒蒸馏框架的潜力，为数据匮乏应用场景下的自动标注提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: The paradigm of learning from automatic annotations driven by pre-trained experts and Foundation Models dominates data-hungry applications. However, it introduces a critical challenge: model-induced label noise. Unlike stochastic noise in classical robust learning, this noise stems from annotator inductive biases, manifesting as systematic errors tightly coupled with local feature manifolds. Existing methods relying on global transition matrices underfit these structural patterns, while learning instance-specific matrices remains mathematically intractable. We propose Model-Induced Noise Decoupling (MIND), a theoretically grounded framework addressing this dilemma. We demonstrate that the high-dimensional noise manifold can be decoupled into tractable, subspace-dependent components via Latent Manifold Disentanglement. Specifically, our Latent Decoupling Estimator (LDE) dynamically projects samples into latent structural clusters with consistent error modes, facilitating noise identifiability without ground-truth anchor points. To rigorously evaluate robustness, we adopt a hierarchical protocol: moving from controlled noise on CIFAR-100 to a structural stress test on large-scale real-world 3D datasets (S3DIS, ScanNet), where error patterns explicitly couple with geometric manifolds. Empirically, MIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on these complex benchmarks and effectively corrects zero-shot hallucinations from Vision-Language Models (e.g., OpenSeg), highlighting its potential as a robust distillation framework for Foundation Models.

</details>


### [152] [Multi-Fidelity Flow Matching: Cascaded Refinement of PDE Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16118)
*Sipeng Chen,Junliang Liu,Hewei Tang,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多保真度流匹配(MFFM)框架，通过校准源分布和级联精炼实现参数化PDE求解，在8个基准测试上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法通常使用默认的各向同性先验作为源分布，而本文发现条件流匹配中的源分布是一个可校准的设计参数。针对参数化偏微分方程求解问题，旨在开发一种学习型多网格精炼方法，通过低保真度到高保真度的残差尺度校准源噪声，提高流匹配训练几何性能。

Method: 提出多保真度流匹配(MFFM)框架：1) 将源分布校准为经验性低保真到高保真残差尺度，并引入局部高斯模糊相关性；2) 速度网络以低保真度解为条件进行训练，使残差精炼问题比无条件场生成更容易；3) 采用多分辨率级联结构，在相邻保真度之间独立应用相同构造；4) 进行层级流匹配预训练后，通过确定性单步展开进行端到端微调，使每个查询在L次确定性网络评估内达到最优操作点。

Result: 在8个基准测试上验证了MFFM的有效性，包括两个超分辨率问题和六个时空预测任务（来自PDEBench、The Well和FNO Navier-Stokes数据集）。方法实现了学习型多网格精炼的类比，能够在L次确定性网络评估内达到最细网格精度，每个级联层级仅需一次速度评估。

Conclusion: MFFM成功将条件流匹配的源分布从默认先验转变为可校准参数，通过残差校准的源噪声和低保真度条件显著改善了流匹配训练几何。该方法作为多网格精炼的学习型模拟，在参数化PDE求解任务中展现出优越性能，为科学计算中的多保真度建模提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The source distribution in conditional flow matching is a design parameter that can be calibrated to data, not a default isotropic prior. We exploit this in Multi-Fidelity Flow Matching (MFFM), a cascade refinement framework for parametric PDE solutions: the source is calibrated to the empirical low-to-high-fidelity residual scale with local Gaussian-blur correlation, and the velocity network is conditioned on the low-fidelity solution. Conditioning makes the residual refinement problem substantially easier than unconditional field generation, while residual-calibrated source noise improves the flow-matching training geometry. A multi-resolution cascade applies the same construction independently between adjacent fidelities. After level-wise flow-matching pretraining, we fine-tune the composed cascade end-to-end with a deterministic one-step rollout, which makes one velocity evaluation per cascade level the optimized operating point at inference. The result is a learned analog of multigrid refinement that reaches the finest grid in $L$ deterministic network evaluations per query. We validate MFFM on eight benchmarks: two super-resolution problems and six spatiotemporal forecasting tasks from PDEBench, The Well, and the FNO Navier--Stokes dataset.

</details>


### [153] [Learn Where Outcomes Diverge: Efficient VLA RL via Probabilistic Chunk Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16154)
*Vaidehi Bagaria,Nikshep Grampurohit,Pulkit Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PCM方法，通过概率性地选择轨迹中有学习价值的语义阶段进行梯度计算，在保持性能的同时将GRPO训练速度提升2.38倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO强化学习在视觉-语言-动作策略后训练中存在计算效率问题：梯度计算占用了78%的墙钟时间，而轨迹收集仅占21%。GRPO将相同的优势值分配给轨迹中的每个块，导致梯度计算均匀分布在整条轨迹上，包括预训练和监督微调后策略已能很好处理的阶段，这些阶段对学习贡献很小。

Method: 提出概率块掩码（PCM）方法，作为GRPO的即插即用修改：1）使用成功-失败动作方差作为每阶段梯度方差的代理指标来评分语义阶段；2）根据在线更新的阶段级保留概率，对固定数量的块预算进行采样，概率性地选择轨迹中的小块子集进行梯度计算。该过程无需奖励模型或学习型评判器。

Result: 在三个LIBERO基准测试上，PCM在达到与标准GRPO相同最终成功率的同时，实现了：2.38倍墙钟加速、4.8倍梯度更新加速、60%的峰值激活内存降低，且仅反向传播不到20%的轨迹块。

Conclusion: 通过将梯度计算集中在真正产生学习信号的高方差阶段，PCM显著提升了GRPO的训练效率，证明了并非所有轨迹阶段都需要梯度更新，为强化学习后训练的效率优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) allows vision-language-action (VLA) policies to generalize beyond their training distribution by optimizing directly for task success, but post-training is computationally expensive. A natural response has been to speed rollout collection through faster simulators and world models. In GRPO-based VLA RL, we find that the dominant cost lies elsewhere: gradient computation accounts for approximately 78% of wall-clock time per step in our runs, while rollout collection accounts for only 21%. Gradient cost dominates because much of this computation is spent on phases that contribute little to learning. GRPO's learning signal is driven by advantage variance: only phases where successful and failed rollouts diverge produce learning signal. However, GRPO assigns the same advantage to every chunk in a rollout. As a result, actor-update compute is spent uniformly across the trajectory, including phases the policy already handles after pre-training and supervised fine-tuning. This paper presents Probabilistic Chunk Masking (PCM), a drop-in modification to GRPO that allocates gradient computation to a small, probabilistically selected subset of chunks per trajectory. PCM scores semantic phases using success-failure action variance, a rollout-derived proxy for per-phase gradient variance, and samples a fixed chunk budget with online-updated phase-level keep probabilities. We formalize per-phase gradient variance as the quantity determines where gradient computation is useful and show that success-failure action variance provides a measurable proxy for it. PCM requires no reward model or learned critic. On three LIBERO benchmarks, PCM matches the final success rate of standard GRPO while achieving 2.38 times wall-clock speedup, 4.8 times faster gradient updates, and 60% lower peak activation memory, while backpropagating through fewer than 20% of trajectory chunks.

</details>


### [154] [Entropic Auto-Encoding via Implicit Free-Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16164)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型的熵自编码器（EAE）框架，通过移除显式先验约束并利用熵隐式生成先验，有效缓解了变分自编码器（VAE）中常见的后验塌陷问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器（VAEs）普遍存在后验塌陷问题，即潜在变量被有效忽略。这一失效模式源于显式先验约束驱动优化过程趋向于对应非信息性潜在表示的损失区域，导致模型无法学习有意义的潜在表示。

Method: 提出熵自编码器（EAEs）框架，其中重构损失是唯一的显式目标函数，通过自由能最小化的编码器集合隐式地通过熵生成潜在变量的先验。该集合将学习偏向于近优解的高体积区域，而解码器更新则将搜索轨迹导向信息性潜在表示。

Result: EAE通过学习非高斯、多模态潜在分布缓解了后验塌陷，能够生成多样化且数据一致的样本。在反应扩散过程中捕获了已知低维动力学的叠加；在MNIST数据集中识别出隐式的类别区分；在CelebA数据集中展现出对面部结构的层次化理解，从"全人类"面部特征到个体特定特征。

Conclusion: EAE框架成功解决了VAE的后验塌陷问题，能够学习复杂的多模态潜在分布并保持数据中的多种底层结构，在多个数据集上展示了优越的表示学习能力，为生成模型提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Despite their ubiquity, variational autoencoders (VAEs) inherently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which latent variables are effectively ignored. This failure arises because explicit prior imposition drives optimization toward loss landscape regions corresponding to uninformative latent representations. Here, we introduce Entropic Autoencoders (EAEs), a framework in which reconstruction loss is the only explicit objective, and entropy generates the latent variables' prior implicitly through a free energy-minimizing ensemble of encoders. This ensemble biases learning toward high-volume regions of near-optimal solutions, while decoder updates direct the search trajectories toward informative latent representations. We demonstrate that EAEs mitigate posterior collapse by learning non-Gaussian, multimodal latent distributions that yield diverse, data-consistent generations and preserve different forms of underlying structure in the data. As a proof-of-concept, we show that an EAE captures a superposition of the known low-dimensional dynamics of a reaction-diffusion process. Then, we show that an EAE identifies implicit categorical distinctions in MNIST latent representations, and displays a hierarchical understanding of facial structure on the CelebA dataset, from an "all-human" face to individual-dependent features.

</details>


### [155] [BAPR: Bayesian amnesic piecewise-robust reinforcement learning for non-stationary continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16170)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BAPR算法，结合贝叶斯在线变点检测与鲁棒集成强化学习，解决分段平稳环境下的控制问题，并提供经过Lean 4形式化验证的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的控制系统经常在分段平稳条件下运行，即动力学在长时间保持稳定后发生突变。标准鲁棒强化学习方法面临两难困境：全局保守策略在稳定期间浪费性能，而局部自适应策略在未检测到环境突变时可能导致灾难性失败。

Method: 提出BAPR（Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC）算法，统一贝叶斯在线变点检测（BOCD）与鲁棒集成强化学习。核心是BAPR算子——一个由冻结信念分布加权的模式条件Bellman算子的凸组合，被证明是γ-压缩映射。设计了自适应保守机制：在检测到变化点后策略变得最大程度保守，随着置信度增长平滑放松。通过RMDM损失训练上下文条件模块，在训练时使用模拟器提供的模式ID，部署时无需模式标签。

Result: 在Lean 4中形式化验证了关键理论结果：当信念依赖于Q函数时，压缩因子变为γ+λΔ（Δ为模式奖励间隙），当γ+λΔ≥1时压缩失效，建立了清晰的边界。推导了抽象算子的逐分量形式误差界，限制切换后恢复时间。变点检测延迟为O(log(1/δ))。总共完成1,145行Lean 4代码，22个机器验证定理，无sorry占位符。

Conclusion: BAPR成功解决了分段平稳环境下的鲁棒强化学习问题，通过冻结信念分布设计实现了理论保证到实际算法的传递，形式化验证确保了理论结果的正确性，自适应保守机制有效平衡了稳定期性能与突变期安全性。

Abstract: Real-world control systems frequently operate under \emph{piecewise stationary} conditions, where dynamics remain stable for extended periods before undergoing abrupt regime changes. Standard robust RL methods face a fundamental dilemma: a globally conservative policy wastes performance during stable periods, while a locally adaptive policy risks catastrophic failure when the regime changes undetected.
  We propose \textbf{BAPR} (Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC), which unifies Bayesian Online Change Detection (BOCD) with robust ensemble RL. The BAPR operator -- a convex combination of mode-conditional Bellman operators weighted by a frozen belief distribution -- is a $γ$-contraction. A complementary counterexample, machine-verified in Lean~4, establishes a \emph{sharp boundary}: when beliefs depend on the Q-function, the contraction factor becomes $γ+ λΔ$ (where $Δ$ is the mode reward gap), and contraction fails exactly when $γ+ λΔ\geq 1$. We derive a \emph{component-wise} formal error budget for the abstract operator -- every component machine-verified -- bounding post-switch recovery; the budget applies to the abstract mode-mixture operator and inherits to the implemented shared-critic algorithm only through the frozen-parameter design intuition. All results are formally verified with no \texttt{sorry} (1,145 lines across 3 Lean~4 files, 22 machine-verified theorems). BOCD drives an adaptive conservatism mechanism: the policy becomes maximally conservative after detected change-points and smoothly relaxes as confidence grows, with detection delay $O(\log(1/δ))$. A context-conditioning module trained via RMDM loss provides mode-aware representations from simulator-provided mode IDs at training time and requires no mode labels at deployment.

</details>


### [156] [Imitation learning for clinical decision support in pediatric ECMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16175)
*Fateme Golivand,Michael Skinner,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Phillip Reeder,Kristian Kersting,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将儿科ECMO临床决策建模为模仿学习问题，发现基于Transformer的TabPFN方法在预测临床行为方面显著优于XGBoost和MLP等传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科重症监护是一个动态、高风险的过程，涉及持续监测和生命挽救治疗的调整。由于儿科ECMO（体外膜肺氧合）具有高复杂性和数据稀缺的特点，建立有效的干预模型对临床决策支持至关重要。

Method: 将临床决策制定框架化为从轨迹中学习的模仿学习问题，即从观察数据中学习动作模型，其关键特征是动作不被直接观察。研究使用基于Transformer的TabPFN方法以及传统基线方法（包括XGBoost和多层感知机MLP）在真实世界儿科ECMO数据上学习动作模型。

Result: 基于TabPFN的方法在这些经典基线方法上持续表现出更优的性能，证明了其作为儿科ECMO决策支持中强有力的临床医生行为基线的价值。

Conclusion: TabPFN可作为儿科ECMO临床决策支持的强基线模型，为临床医生行为建模提供了有效工具，支持其在高风险儿科重症监护场景中的应用。

Abstract: Pediatric critical care is a dynamic, high-stakes process involving constant monitoring and adjustments in life-saving treatments. Modeling these interventions is crucial for effective decision support. To address the challenges of high complexity and data scarcity in pediatric Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation (ECMO), we frame clinical decision-making as learning to act from trajectories, i.e., imitation learning that learns action models from observational data, with a key feature that actions are not directly observed. We consider TabPFN, a recent transformer-based approach for tabular data, and traditional baselines including XGBoost and Multi-Layer Perceptrons(MLPs) on real-world pediatric ECMO data to learn the action models. We find that the TabPFN-based approach consistently outperforms these classical baselines, supporting its use as a strong clinician-behavior baseline for pediatric ECMO decision support.

</details>


### [157] [Hypothesis-driven construction of mesoscopic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16211)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Aiqing Zhu,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数学约束假设类的介观动力学学习框架，通过广义Onsager原理统一了耗散和保守动力学，并提供了先验的理论保证，从而在复杂数据中学习出准确、鲁棒且可解释的动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的科学建模通常从固定的、实例化的有效方程出发进行分析和计算，这种方法在多尺度系统等复杂应用中面临巨大挑战。作者希望提出一种替代范式，通过学习介观动力学来克服这些困难。

Method: 构建了一个基于广义Onsager原理的统一框架，该框架包含了一个数学约束的假设类，能够同时涵盖耗散型和保守型介观动力学。在该假设类中，先验地建立了统一的理论保证（包括全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一因子分解可识别性和离散能量耗散），然后利用数据引导识别假设类中的具体成员。

Result: 在来自连续介质PDE模型的数据和微观链模型的数据上进行了验证。结果表明该方法不仅能作为有效的动力学学习器，还能提供关于底层物理的重要可解释诊断信息。学习出的模型准确、鲁棒且具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地为复杂系统提供了一个新的建模范式，通过结合严格的数学理论与数据驱动的方法，实现了兼具理论保证、物理可解释性和实际有效性的动力学学习。

Abstract: Traditional scientific modeling typically begins with fixed, instance-wise effective equations and then carries out equation-specific analysis and computation, a procedure that becomes exceptionally challenging in complex applications such as multiscale systems. We propose an alternative paradigm by learning mesoscopic dynamics within a mathematically constrained hypothesis class. Building upon a generalized Onsager principle, we introduce a unified framework encompassing both dissipative and conservative mesoscopic dynamics. We establish uniform and a priori theoretical guarantees, including global well-posedness, asymptotic stability, unique factorization identifiability, and discrete energy dissipation, applicable to all spatio-temporal evolution equations within this hypothesis class prior to all learning stages. Data from each problem instance is then used to guide the identification of members within our hypothesis class, giving rise to accurate, robust and interpretable dynamical models. We empirically validate this framework on both data from continuum PDE models as a check, and on data arising from microscopic chain models for which exact meso-scale models are unknown. The proposed approach not only acts as an effective dynamics learner, but also offers vital interpretable diagnostics of the underlying physics.

</details>


### [158] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了差分隐私下CVaR学习的核心机制：隐私相关的样本量从n变为nτ，完整的收敛速率表明，对nτ个尾部记录进行普通隐私学习是问题的核心难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究差分隐私如何影响条件风险价值（CVaR）学习的统计特性，特别是隐私约束如何改变有效样本量并影响尾部风险估计的收敛速率。

Method: 通过将私有CVaR超额风险分解为普通尾部风险统计误差与隐私代价，分别针对标量估计、有限函数类和凸Lipschitz学习场景推导上界与下界，建立完整的收敛速率。

Result: 标量估计的收敛速率为Θ(B min{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}})，有限函数类（规模M）的速率为Θ(B min{1,√(log(2M)/(nτ))+log(2M)/(εnτ)}))；凸Lipschitz学习的隐私项必然按1/(εnτ)尺度增长，维度依赖性继承自私有随机凸优化。这些完整速率在纯DP下成立，下界可推广至近似DP的小δ机制。

Conclusion: 差分隐私下CVaR学习的本质困难在于：隐私相关样本量仅为nτ而非n，使得问题等价于在Θ(nτ)个有信息量的尾部记录上进行普通隐私学习，这一发现为理解隐私约束下的尾部风险学习提供了理论基准。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [159] [Dynamics-Level Watermarking of Flow Matching Models with Random Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16239)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在生成模型的动力学层面（速度场）嵌入水印的新方法，在保持生成质量的同时实现了可靠的消息恢复和密钥保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型水印方法主要嵌入在模型权重或输出中，作者希望探索一种新的嵌入维度——将水印直接嵌入到流匹配模型学习的连续动力学（速度场）中，以实现更隐蔽且保持生成分布不变的水印方案。

Method: 将水印问题建模为连续信道上的随机编码：在训练过程中向速度场添加依赖于密钥的扰动，该扰动设计为不改变生成的分布；在检测阶段通过黑盒查询恢复嵌入的消息。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，使用不同架构进行的实验表明：该方法能够可靠恢复消息，生成质量得到保持，且在无密钥情况下解码准确率仅为随机水平。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在生成模型的动力学层面嵌入水印的可行性，为生成模型水印提供了一种新途径，具有可靠的消息恢复能力和良好的安全性。

Abstract: We introduce a dynamics-level approach to watermarking generative models. Rather than embedding signals into model weights or outputs, we embed the watermark directly into the learned continuous dynamics -- the velocity field of a flow matching model. We formulate this as random coding over a continuous channel: a key-dependent perturbation is added during training, and the message is recovered at detection time from black-box queries. The perturbation is designed to leave the generated distribution unchanged. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 across different architectures confirm reliable message recovery, preserved generation quality, and chance-level decoding accuracy without the secret key.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Estimated Dynamic Equilibrium Model: Supply and Demand as a Sample Path of a Stochastic Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15472)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Sisong Bei,Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了估计动态均衡模型（EDEM），发现了一种内生性的价格持续偏离均衡机制：对市场出清价格的序贯抽样会引发有序统计偏差，导致价格指数级上涨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分歧理论（如Miller理论）多为静态分析，且实证研究结果存在矛盾。本文旨在构建一个动态的基于主体的框架，解释在无投资者非理性假设的情况下，市场价格为何会持续偏离均衡并形成泡沫。

Method: 引入估计动态均衡模型（EDEM），将供需视为由异质性、含噪的代理人估值驱动的耦合随机过程。通过数理推导分析了i.i.d.均匀投标分布下的有序统计偏差，并结合仿真实验在模拟房地产邻域中进行了敏感性分析。

Result: 推导出了导致预期价格向上漂移的封闭形式偏差，证明了该偏差在跨期复合作用下可导致指数级价格增长。实验识别出了包括带宽稳定和泡沫失控在内的六种市场机制，验证了Walrasian均衡和Miller静态溢价是该模型的特例。

Conclusion: 该框架解释了分歧理论实证文献中的矛盾发现，并指出机器学习估值算法可能会无意中放大这种固有的统计偏差，引发市场不稳定。

Abstract: We introduce the Estimated Dynamic Equilibrium Model (EDEM), an agent-based framework that treats supply and demand as a coupled stochastic process driven by heterogeneous, noisy agent valuations. The model's primary technical contribution is the identification of a generative mechanism for persistent disequilibrium: when market-clearing prices are sequentially sampled from the upper tail of noisy bid distributions and recycled as inputs for future valuations, expected prices drift upward despite strictly zero-mean estimation errors. We derive this order-statistic bias in closed form for i.i.d. uniform bids and use simulations to show that compounding this bias across epochs yields exponential price growth without requiring assumptions of investor optimism or irrationality. This framework extends Miller's divergence-of-opinion theory to a dynamic setting, recovering Walrasian equilibrium and Miller's static premium as limiting cases. Through controlled experiments and sensitivity analysis on a simulated real-estate neighborhood, we identify six distinct regimes-ranging from band-stability to runaway bubbles-emerging from a single agent ruleset. These results offer a potential explanation for the contradictory findings in the empirical divergence-of-opinion literature and suggest that machine-learning valuation algorithms may inadvertently amplify this inherent statistical bias.

</details>


### [161] [Distributed Zeroth-Order Policy Gradient for Networked Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15697)
*Pengcheng Dai,He Wang,Dongming Wang,Jian Qin,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对网络化多智能体系统，提出了一种基于时空截断轨迹人类反馈的分布式零阶策略梯度算法，无需显式奖励信号或中心化控制即可实现协作优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于偏好反馈的方法主要针对单智能体场景且依赖中心化训练，限制了其在具有局部状态依赖的大规模网络化多智能体强化学习问题中的可扩展性与适用性。

Method: 提出了一种基于时空截断轨迹（定义为在每个智能体κ跳邻域内聚合的H视界轨迹对）的人类反馈机制。在此基础上，设计了完全分布式的零阶策略梯度算法，每个智能体利用当前策略和高斯扰动策略生成的偏好反馈来估计局部策略梯度，仅需邻域内的状态-动作信息。

Result: 理论证明了该算法能收敛至ε-平稳点，且具有多项式样本复杂度。在随机GridWorld和捕食者-猎物环境中的仿真结果验证了算法在仅依赖人类偏好反馈进行协作优化时的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式算法成功解决了无限视界网络化多智能体强化学习中的人类反馈利用难题，摆脱了对显式奖励和中心化控制的依赖，具有良好的理论保证和实际性能。

Abstract: We study a networked multi-agent reinforcement learning (NMARL) problem with human feedback in an infinite-horizon setting, where agents interact over an underlying network with localized state dependencies and aim to collaboratively maximize the average discounted return. Existing approaches with preference feedback are primarily developed for single-agent settings and rely on centralized training, which limits their scalability and applicability to large-scale networked multi-agent systems. To address this, we introduce a novel human feedback mechanism based on spatiotemporally truncated trajectories, defined as $H$-horizon trajectory pairs aggregated over each agent's $κ$-hop neighborhood. Building on this, we develop a distributed zeroth-order policy gradient algorithm, where each agent estimates its local policy gradient using human preference feedback generated from both the current joint policy and a perturbed joint policy drawn from zero-mean Gaussian distribution. Specifically, the algorithm is fully distributed, as the feedback received by each agent depends solely on the state-action information within its $κ$-hop neighborhood and does not require explicit reward signals or centralized control. We further rigorously establish that the proposed algorithm converges to an $ε$-stationary point with polynomial sample complexity. Finally, simulation results in a stochastic GridWorld environment and a predator-prey environment further demonstrate that the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed algorithm in achieving collaborative optimization based solely on human preference feedback.

</details>


### [162] [From Gridworlds to Warehouses: Adapting Lightweight One-shot Multi-Agent Pathfinding for AGVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15799)
*Hiroki Nagai,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAWPF问题，通过引入差速驱动AGV的四种实际约束填补了经典MAPF与现实仓库自动化之间的差距，并通过对代表性算法的基准测试发现基于PIBT的方法具有更好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的多智能体路径规划（MAPF）通常假设在四连接的2D网格上进行单位时间的四方向移动，这与现实中仓库自动化的实际需求存在差距。为了在保持离散组合搜索可解性的同时填补这一现实鸿沟，作者旨在为差速驱动自动导引车（AGV）提出一个更实用的规划问题。

Method: 1. 定义了MAWPF（多智能体仓库路径规划）问题，包含四项关键约束：(i) 智能体动作仅限直线运动和原地旋转；(ii) 旋转需要多步代价；(iii) 考虑加减速过程；(iv) 禁止跟随者碰撞以防追尾。\n2. 适配了四种具有代表性的次优MAPF算法（PP、LNS2、PIBT和LaCAM）以求解MAWPF问题。\n3. 进行了全面的基准测试和对比实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，PP（Prioritized Planning）和LNS2算法在处理多智能体实例时表现不佳，难以求解；而基于PIBT（Priority Inheritance with Backtracking）的方法虽然解决方案成本有所增加，但在扩展性方面表现更优，能够应对更多智能体的场景。

Conclusion: 这项工作是将经典网格世界MAPF适应于操作仓库设置的重要一步，证明了在引入现实约束后，基于PIBT的算法在处理大规模多智能体仓库路径规划问题上具有更好的潜力和实用性。

Abstract: Multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) under one-shot planning is a core component of warehouse automation, yet classical formulations typically assume four-connected 2D grids with unit-time moves in four directions. To fill reality gaps while still being trackable with discrete combinatorial search, this work proposes a more practical counterpart tailored to differential-drive AGVs. We term this multi-agent warehouse pathfinding (MAWPF), featured with four constraints: (i) agent actions are restricted to straight motion and in-place rotation; (ii) rotations require multi-step costs; (iii) acceleration and deceleration are considered, and; (iv) follower collisions are prohibited to prevent rear-end crashes. To solve MAWPF efficiently, we adapt representative suboptimal MAPF algorithms-PP, LNS2, PIBT, and LaCAM-and conduct comprehensive benchmarking. Our experiments reveal that PP and LNS2 struggle to solve instances with many agents, while PIBT-based approaches achieve preferable scalability with increased solution cost. We believe that these constitute an important step toward adapting classical gridworld MAPF to operational warehouse setups.

</details>


### [163] [Multi-Agent Cooperative Transportation: Optimal and Efficient Task Allocation and Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16097)
*Ning Zhou,Nikolai W. F. Bode,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多机器人系统无法有效协作运输大件物品的问题，提出了协作运输任务分配与路径规划（CT-TAPF）问题形式化，设计了最优求解器CT-TCBS及一系列基于全局任务视角的高效次优求解器。实验证明增量扩展策略显著优于传统方法，揭示了任务-冲突扩展困境，并建立了新的质量-效率前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体路径规划（MAPF）和任务分配与路径规划（TAPF）框架存在关键缺陷：它们缺乏真正的协作机制，无法处理需要多个智能体协同运输大件物品的任务。多机器人系统在现代物流中至关重要，但其能力往往局限于单个智能体可执行的任务。为了填补这一空白，需要建立一个新的框架来整合团队组建、任务分配和无碰撞路径规划。

Method: 1. **问题形式化**：提出协作运输任务分配与路径规划（CT-TAPF）问题，整合团队组建、任务分配和无碰撞路径规划三个核心要素。
2. **最优求解器CT-TCBS**：设计了协作运输任务冲突搜索算法，引入新颖的增量扩展策略，通过有效剪枝来应对团队组建中的组合爆炸问题。
3. **次优求解器系列**：开发了采用全局任务中心视角的次优求解器，基于全局难度度量（最佳任务或最差任务）选择下一个分配的任务，平衡计算成本与解的质量。

Result: 通过全面的实证评估，得出三个关键发现：
1. 增量扩展策略通过成功剪枝主导的任务分配搜索空间，显著优于朴素组合方法；
2. 识别出任务-冲突扩展困境，即在大智能体路径规划子问题中有效的复杂冲突解决器在集成CT-TAPF环境中可能产生负面影响；
3. 提出的次优求解器与"最近邻"智能体中心基准相比，在解质量-运行时间谱上建立了新的、更高效的前沿。

Conclusion: 本研究为协作多智能体问题提供了一个基础框架和一套有效算法。通过CT-TAPF问题形式化和CT-TCBS求解器的开发，成功解决了多机器人协作运输的实际难题。增量扩展策略的有效性和任务-冲突扩展困境的发现为未来研究指明了方向，次优求解器的提出则在保证解质量的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为实际物流应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are integral to modern logistics, but their capabilities are often limited to tasks executable by individual agents. This paper addresses a critical gap in existing frameworks like Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) and Task Allocation and Path Finding (TAPF), which lack true cooperation for transporting large items that require multiple agents. To this end, we formalise the Cooperative Transportation Task Allocation and Path Finding (CT-TAPF) problem, which integrates team formation, task assignment, and collision-free pathfinding. We present an optimal solver, Cooperative Transportation Task Conflict-Based Search (CT-TCBS), which features a novel Incremental Expansion strategy to tackle the combinatorial explosion inherent in team formation. Recognising the computational cost of optimality, we also develop a family of sub-optimal solvers that employ a global, task-centric perspective, selecting the next task to assign based on a global difficulty metric (Best Task or Worst Task). Our comprehensive empirical evaluation demonstrates three key findings: (1) the incremental expansion strategy significantly outperforms the naive combinatorial approach by successfully pruning the dominant task-allocation search space; (2) we identify a task-conflict expansion dilemma, where sophisticated conflict resolvers effective for large-agent pathfinding subproblems can be detrimental in the integrated CT-TAPF setting; and (3) our proposed sub-optimal solvers establish a new, more efficient frontier on the solution quality-runtime spectrum compared to "nn-" agent-centric baselines. This work provides a foundational framework and a set of effective algorithms for a new, practical class of cooperative multi-agent problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [DeepSlide: From Artifacts to Presentation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15202)
*Ming Yang,Zhiwei Zhang,Jiahang Li,Haoseng Liu,Yuzheng Cai,Weiguo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeepSlide，一个人在回路的多智能体演示文稿准备系统，不仅优化幻灯片本身，更注重演讲的交付过程（节奏、叙事和准备），在保持静态工件质量的同时，显著提升了动态交付表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI幻灯片生成工具主要关注生成视觉上合理的幻灯片（artifact），而忽视了演讲的交付过程（delivery process），包括节奏控制、叙事设计和演讲准备。演示文稿作为一种主要的学术交流媒介，其成功不仅取决于幻灯片本身的质量，更取决于演讲的交付效果，因此需要一种能够全面支持整个演示准备过程的系统。

Method: DeepSlide是一个人在回路的多智能体系统，集成了四个核心组件：（i）可控的逻辑链规划器，支持每个节点的时序预算分配；（ii）轻量级内容树检索器，用于证据支撑；（iii）马尔可夫风格的顺序渲染机制，支持样式继承；（iv）沙盒执行与最小修复机制，确保可渲染性。系统支持从需求引导、时序预算叙事规划，到证据支撑的幻灯片-脚本生成、注意力增强和排练支持的全流程。同时引入了双评分基准，清晰分离静态工件质量和动态交付表现。

Result: 在20个领域和多样化受众配置文件的评估中，DeepSlide在静态工件质量上与强基线方法相当，但在动态交付指标上持续取得更大提升。系统在叙事流畅性、节奏精确性、幻灯片-脚本协同效应方面表现优异，并提供更清晰的注意力引导。

Conclusion: DeepSlide成功填补了现有AI幻灯片生成工具在演讲交付过程优化方面的空白，通过人在回路的多智能体架构实现了对演示准备全流程的支持，证明了同时优化静态工件质量和动态交付表现的可行性和有效性，为学术演示文稿的智能辅助系统提供了新的发展方向。

Abstract: Presentations are a primary medium for scholarly communication, yet most AI slide generators optimize the artifact (a visually plausible deck) while under-optimizing the delivery process (pacing, narrative, and presentation preparation). We present DeepSlide, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that supports preparing the full presentation process, from requirement elicitation and time-budgeted narrative planning, to evidence-grounded slide--script generation, attention augmentation, and rehearsal support. DeepSlide integrates (i) a controllable logical-chain planner with per-node time budgets, (ii) a lightweight content-tree retriever for grounding, (iii) Markov-style sequential rendering with style inheritance, and (iv) sandboxed execution with minimal repair to ensure renderability. We further introduce a dual-scoreboard benchmark that cleanly separates static artifact quality from dynamic delivery excellence. Across 20 domains and diverse audience profiles, DeepSlide matches strong baselines on artifact quality while consistently achieving larger gains on delivery metrics, improving narrative flow, pacing precision, and slide--script synergy with clearer attention guidance.

</details>


### [165] [SDOF: Taming the Alignment Tax in Multi-Agent Orchestration with State-Constrained Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15204)
*Zhantao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SDOF框架，将多智能体执行建模为约束状态机，通过在线RLHF意图路由器和状态感知调度器实现可审计的执行控制，在招聘系统上达到86.5%任务完成率并成功阻止所有非法操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体编排框架（如LangChain、LangGraph、CrewAI）基于图管道进行任务路由，但缺乏对真实业务流程中阶段约束的强制执行能力，存在执行控制和审计方面的不足。

Method: SDOF框架包含两个防御层和三个核心组件：(1) 通过生成式奖励建模（GRPO）训练的在线RLHF专业化意图路由器；(2) 具有GoalStage有限自动机检查和前置/后置条件SkillRegistry验证的状态感知调度器，实现可审计的执行控制。

Result: 在北森iTalent平台支持的招聘系统上，使用185个专家策划场景进行测试：GSPO对齐的7B意图路由器在FSM约束对抗性路由基准上达到80.9%联合准确率（GPT-4o零样本为48.9%）；端到端执行任务完成率达86.5%；成功阻止所有22个注入和非法HR操作；消息级阻止审计达到100%精确率和88%召回率（专家一致性kappa=0.94）；在960个SGD衍生对话中发现201个阶段顺序冲突。

Conclusion: SDOF通过将多智能体执行建模为约束状态机，有效解决了现有框架在业务流程阶段约束方面的缺陷，在保持高任务完成率的同时实现了严格的执行控制和审计能力，为真实业务场景提供了更可靠的多智能体编排解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks such as LangChain, LangGraph, and CrewAI route tasks through graph-based pipelines but do not enforce the stage constraints that govern real business processes. We present SDOF, a framework that treats multi-agent execution as a constrained state machine. SDOF operates through two primary defensive layers, implemented by three components: (1) an Online-RLHF Specialized Intent Router trained via Generative Reward Modeling (GRPO) and (2) a StateAwareDispatcher with GoalStage finite-automaton checks and precondition/postcondition SkillRegistry validation for auditable execution control. On a recruitment system backed by the Beisen iTalent platform (6000+ enterprises), 185 expert-curated scenarios trigger 1671 live API calls. Our GSPO-aligned 7B Intent Router achieves higher joint accuracy than zero-shot GPT-4o on this FSM-constrained adversarial routing benchmark (80.9% versus 48.9%). In end-to-end execution, SDOF reaches 86.5% task completion (95% confidence interval 80.8 to 90.7) and blocks all 22 operations in the injection, illegal HR subset. Under a broader message-level blocking audit, SDOF attains precision 100% and recall 88%, expert agreement kappa=0.94. A separate evaluation on 960 SGD-derived dialogues spanning 8 service domains surfaces 201 stage-order conflicts under our FSM mapping, 41 of which arise in the normal split. This arXiv version reports the current validated scope; extended multi-seed training comparisons and deeper workflow evaluations will be released in a subsequent update.

</details>


### [166] [ICRL: Learning to Internalize Self-Critique with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15224)
*Jianbo Lin,Xiaomin Yu,Yi Xin,Yifu Guo,Zhuosong Jiang,Zhongqi Yue,Weishi Wang,Heqing Zou,Chengwei Qin,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ICRL框架，通过强化学习联合训练求解器和评论器，将外部批评转化为模型自身能力，在智能体和数学推理任务上平均提升6.4-7.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理在外部批评指导下能够纠正错误，但批评移除后在相同查询上仍会失败，表明模型未将批评内化；同时冻结的评论器无法随时间提升反馈质量，限制了迭代自我改进的潜力。

Method: 提出ICRL框架，从共享主干联合训练求解器和评论器。评论器根据求解器后续性能提升获得奖励，激励可执行的反馈；引入分布校准重加权比例，选择性地转移与求解器自身提示分布兼容的批评引导改进，解决批评条件行为与无批评行为间的分布偏移；采用角色分组优势估计稳定两个角色的联合优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B骨干上进行评估，涵盖智能体和数学推理任务。ICRL在智能体任务上较GRPO平均提升6.4分，在数学推理上提升7.0分。学习得到的8B评论器可媲美32B评论器，同时使用更少的token。

Conclusion: ICRL成功使求解器学会在无外部批评情况下自我改进，而非依赖批评条件行为，实现了批评引导成功向无辅助求解能力的有效转化。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents make mistakes, yet critique can often guide the same model toward correct behavior. However, when critique is removed, the model may fail again on the same query, indicating that it has not internalized the critique's guidance into its underlying capability. Meanwhile, a frozen critic cannot improve its feedback quality over time, limiting the potential for iterative self-improvement. To address this, we propose learning to internalize self-critique with reinforcement learning(ICRL), a novel framework that jointly trains a solver and a critic from a shared backbone to convert critique-induced success into unassisted solver ability. The critic is rewarded based on the solver's subsequent performance gain, incentivizing actionable feedback. To address the distribution shift between critique-conditioned and critique-free behavior, ICRL introduces a distribution-calibration re-weighting ratio that selectively transfers critique-guided improvements compatible with the solver's own prompt distribution. Additionally, a role-wise group advantage estimation stabilizes joint optimization across the two roles. Together, these mechanisms ensure that the solver learns to improve itself without external critique, rather than becoming dependent on critique-conditioned behavior. We evaluate ICRL on diverse benchmarks spanning agentic and mathematical reasoning tasks, using Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B as backbones. Results show consistent improvements, with average gains of 6.4 points over GRPO on agentic tasks, and 7.0 points on mathematical reasoning. Notably, the learned 8B critic is comparable to 32B critics while using substantially fewer tokens. The code is available at https://github.com/brick-pid/ICRL.

</details>


### [167] [Does Theory of Mind Improvement Really Benefit Human-AI Interactions? Empirical Findings from Interactive Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15205)
*Nanxu Gong,Zixin Chen,Haotian Li,Zishu Zhao,Jianxun Lian,Huamin Qu,Yanjie Fu,Xing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了交互式心智理论(ToM)评估新范式，发现静态基准测试的提升并不总能转化为动态人机交互中的更好性能，强调了基于交互的评估对开发社交感知型大语言模型的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ToM基准测试主要通过第三人称视角的故事阅读和多项选择题来评估，忽略了人机交互中第一人称、动态和开放性的本质特征，无法真实反映ToM提升技术对人机交互的实际效果。

Method: 提出交互式ToM评估新范式，包含视角和度量两个维度的转变；基于该范式，对四种代表性的ToM增强技术进行了系统研究，使用四个真实世界数据集和用户研究，涵盖目标导向任务（编程、数学）和体验导向任务（心理咨询）。

Result: 静态基准测试上的性能提升并不总能转化为动态人机交互场景中更好的表现；不同类型的任务（目标导向vs体验导向）对ToM能力的需求和评估结果存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前ToM评估方法的局限性，证明了基于交互的评估对于开发下一代具有社交感知能力的大语言模型至关重要，为人机共生环境下的社交感知型LLM发展提供了关键洞察。

Abstract: Improving the Theory of Mind (ToM) capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is crucial for effective social interactions between these AI models and humans. However, the existing benchmarks often measure ToM capability improvement through story-reading, multiple-choice questions from a third-person perspective, while ignoring the first-person, dynamic, and open-ended nature of human-AI (HAI) interactions. To directly examine how ToM improvement techniques benefit HAI interactions, we first proposed the new paradigm of interactive ToM evaluation with both perspective and metric shifts. Next, following the paradigm, we conducted a systematic study of four representative ToM enhancement techniques using both four real-world datasets and a user study, covering both goal-oriented tasks (e.g., coding, math) and experience-oriented tasks (e.g., counseling). Our findings reveal that improvements on static benchmarks do not always translate to better performance in dynamic HAI interactions. This paper offers critical insights into ToM evaluation, showing the necessity of interaction-based assessments in developing next-generation, socially aware LLMs for HAI symbiosis.

</details>


### [168] [SMCEvolve: Principled Scientific Discovery via Sequential Monte Carlo Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15308)
*Jiachen Jiang,Huminhao Zhu,Zhihui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMCEvolve框架，将LLM驱动的程序进化搜索重新表述为序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样问题，提供了有原则的组件设计指南，并在有限样本复杂度分析中给出了达到目标近似误差所需的LLM调用预算上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动程序进化框架缺乏对各组件设计的原则性指导，且无法保证搜索过程的收敛性。

Method: 将程序搜索重构为从奖励倾斜的目标分布中采样，并使用序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样器进行近似。由此视角导出三个核心机制：自适应父代重采样、变异与接受的混合、自动收敛控制。

Result: 在数学、算法效率、符号回归和端到端ML研究基准测试中，SMCEvolve在使用更少LLM调用且自主决定终止的条件下，超越了最先进的进化系统。

Conclusion: SMCEvolve通过原则性的SMC框架为LLM驱动程序进化提供了理论保障（有限样本复杂度界限）和实用组件，实现了更高效且自动收敛的自动化科学发现。

Abstract: LLM-driven program evolution has emerged as a powerful tool for automated scientific discovery, yet existing frameworks offer no principled guide for designing their individual components and provide no guarantee that the search converges. We introduce SMCEvolve, which recasts program search as sampling from a reward-tilted target distribution and approximates it with a Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) sampler. From this view, three core mechanisms emerge as principled components: adaptive parent resampling, mixture of mutation with acceptance, and automatic convergence control. We further provide a finite-sample complexity analysis that bounds the LLM-call budget required to reach a target approximation error. Across math, algorithm efficiency, symbolic regression, and end-to-end ML research benchmarks, SMCEvolve surpasses state-of-the-art evolving systems while using fewer LLM calls under self-determined termination. The code is available at https://github.com/kongwanbianjinyu/SMCEvolve.

</details>


### [169] [SkillSmith: Compiling Agent Skills into Boundary-Guided Runtime Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15215)
*Duling Xu,Zheng Chen,Zaifeng Pan,Jiawei Guan,Dong Dong,Jialin Li,Bangzheng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSmith框架，通过离线编译技能包为最小可执行接口，解决LLM智能体系统中技能注入带来的上下文冗余和重复推理问题，显著降低了计算开销并提升了任务执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体框架在技能注入时存在两大冗余问题：无关上下文注入和重复的技能特定推理与规划，导致计算资源浪费和效率低下。

Method: 提出SkillSmith，一个“边界优先”的编译-运行时框架。该框架在离线阶段将技能包编译为最小可执行接口，通过从技能中提取细粒度的操作边界，使智能体在运行时能够动态访问并执行相关组件，从而最小化不必要的上下文注入和冗余推理开销。

Result: 在SkillsBench基准测试中，与使用原始技能相比，SkillSmith将解决阶段的token使用量减少57.44%，思考迭代次数减少42.99%，解决时间缩短50.57%（提速2.02倍），token比例货币成本降低57.44%。此外，由强模型生成的编译产物可被较小的运行时模型复用，提升了原始技能解释失败情况下的任务准确性。

Conclusion: SkillSmith有效解决了技能注入中的冗余问题，大幅提升了LLM智能体系统的执行效率和经济效益，同时实现了编译产物的跨模型复用，增强了系统的鲁棒性和灵活性。

Abstract: Recently, skills have been widely adopted in large language model (LLM)-based agent systems across various domains. In existing frameworks, skills are typically injected into the agent reasoning loop as contextual guidance once matched to a runtime task, enabling specialized task-solving capabilities. We find that this execution paradigm introduces two major sources of redundancy: irrelevant context injection and repeated skill-specific reasoning and planning. To this end, we propose SkillSmith, a boundary-first compiler-runtime framework that compiles skill packages offline into minimal executable interfaces. By extracting fine-grained operational boundaries from skills, SkillSmith enables agents to dynamically access and execute only the relevant components at runtime, thereby minimizing unnecessary context injection and redundant reasoning overhead. In the evaluation on SkillsBench benchmark, SkillSmith reduces solve-stage token usage by 57.44%, thinking iterations by 42.99%, solve time by 50.57% (2.02x faster), and token-proportional monetary cost by 57.44% compared with using raw-skills. Moreover, compiled artifacts produced by a stronger model can be reused by a smaller or more efficient runtime model, improving task accuracy in cases where raw skill interpretation fails. The source code and data are available at https://github.com/AetherHeart-AI/Aeloon.

</details>


### [170] [Belief Engine: Configurable and Inspectable Stance Dynamics in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15343)
*Joshua C. Yang,Maurice Flechtner,Damian Dailisan,Michiel A. Bakker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Belief Engine (BE)，一个可审计的信念更新层，通过证据吸收和先验锚定控制来显式建模LLM智能体在协商交互中的立场变化，解决了传统LLM模拟中立场变化原因不透明的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的协商交互模拟（如谈判、冲突解决、多轮意见交换）中，生成的对话记录无法解释智能体立场变化的原因，变化可能源于证据吸收、锚定、角色漂移、回声或提示/检索上下文改变等多种因素，缺乏可解释性和可审计性。

Method: 将"信念"定义为命题上的证据状态并暴露为标量立场。BE提取论点至结构化记忆，采用受证据吸收参数u和先验锚定参数a控制的log-odds规则更新立场。通过参数扫描验证控制机制，并在人类协商数据集DEBATE上进行验证。

Result: 在多种基础LLM上，参数控制能可靠地塑造立场动态并保留证据级更新轨迹。在DEBATE数据集上，BE最佳重建了最终立场遵循提取证据的参与者；稳定和证据对立案例则指向锚定或提取证据流之外的因素。

Conclusion: BE为研究基于证据的协商提供了可配置的基础设施，使得开放性、承诺、收敛和分歧可以与显式的更新假设相关联，而非隐藏的提示效应，提升了LLM协商模拟的可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used to simulate deliberative interactions such as negotiation, conflict resolution, and multi-turn opinion exchange. Yet generated transcripts often do not reveal why an agent's stance changes: movement may reflect evidence uptake, anchoring, role drift, echoing, or changed prompt and retrieval context. We introduce the Belief Engine (BE), an auditable belief-update layer that treats "belief" as an evidential state over a proposition and exposes it as scalar stance. BE extracts arguments into structured memory and updates stance with a log-odds rule controlled by evidence uptake u and prior anchoring a. Across multiple base LLMs, parameter sweeps show that these controls reliably shape stance dynamics while preserving an evidence-level update trail. On DEBATE, a human deliberation dataset with pre/post opinions, BE best reconstructs participants whose final stance follows extracted evidence; stable and evidence-opposed cases instead point to anchoring or factors outside the extracted evidence stream. BE provides configurable infrastructure for studying evidence-grounded deliberation, where openness, commitment, convergence, and disagreement can be tied to explicit update assumptions rather than hidden prompt effects.

</details>


### [171] [Fair outputs, Biased Internals: Causal Potency and Asymmetry of Latent Bias in LLMs for High-Stakes Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15217)
*Jagdish Tripathy,Marcus Buckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现在抵押贷款审批任务中，指令调优的语言模型虽然输出结果表面公平，但内部仍保留被抑制的种族偏见信息。通过激活引导技术将这些信息重新注入关键层，可以导致决策发生近完全逆转，且这种潜在偏见具有不对称性和可利用性，揭示了仅依赖输出审计的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令调优的语言模型在高风险决策中展现出行为公平性，但其内部表征中仍保留偏见关联。目前尚不清楚这些被抑制的表征是否会影响模型输出，以及这种因果效力在不同人口群体间是否对称。研究旨在探究模型输出公平性与内部表征之间的关系，验证行为审计的充分性。

Method: 使用开放权重模型进行抵押贷款审批实验，采用仅在种族关联姓名上存在差异的配对申请。通过分析模型各层的人口统计表征变化，运用激活引导（activation steering）和新型跨层干预技术，测试被抑制信息对决策的因果影响。同时检验干预效果的不对称性，以及对抗性提示工程和参数高效微调对潜在偏见的影响。

Result: 模型在输出层面未表现出偏见，但在各层中保留并放大了人口统计表征。被抑制的信息具有决策相关性：在关键层重新注入时，可导致近完全的决策逆转。这种潜在偏见具有不对称性——引导干预仅影响一个人口群体方向的决策，反向干预效果极小。该偏见易受对抗性提示工程和参数高效微调的影响。

Conclusion: 仅关注输出的行为审计不足以评估模型公平性，公平的输出可能掩盖可被利用的内部偏见。研究建议高风险决策的AI治理应采用双层测试框架，结合输出评估与表征分析，以全面检测潜在偏见。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models exhibit behavioural fairness in high-stakes decisions while retaining biased associations in their internal representations. However, whether these suppressed representations can affect model outputs - and whether such causal potency is symmetric across demographic groups - remains unknown. We investigate the use of open-weight models for mortgage underwriting using matched applications that differ only in racially-associated names and reveal a critical disconnect: models show no output-level bias, yet retain and amplify demographic representations across model layers. Through activation steering and novel cross-layer interventions, we demonstrate that this suppressed information is decision-relevant: when reinjected at critical layers, it produces near-complete decision reversals. Critically, this latent bias is asymmetric - steering interventions affect decisions in one demographic direction, while producing minimal effects in reverse - and susceptible to adversarial prompt engineering and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate that behavioural audits focused on outputs are insufficient: fair outputs can mask exploitable internal biases. They also motivate dual-layer testing frameworks combining output evaluation with representational analysis for AI governance in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [172] [Context, Reasoning, and Hierarchy: A Cost-Performance Study of Compound LLM Agent Design in an Adversarial POMDP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16205)
*Igor Bogdanov,Chung-Horng Lung,Thomas Kunz,Jie Gao,Adrian Taylor,Marzia Zaman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在网络防御POMDP环境中，程序化状态抽象比原始观察性价比更高；层级分解优于单体架构；但在层级中叠加推理工具会产生“推理级联”效应，反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在对抗性、部分可观察的序列环境中部署复合大语言模型智能体时，缺乏关于输入表示、推理方式和任务分解等设计维度的有效指导，实践者难以判断哪些设计选择能真正提升性能，而非徒增推理成本。

Method: 在模拟网络防御的CybORG CAGE-2环境（POMDP）中进行了控制实验。研究跨越5个模型家族、6个模型和12种配置（共3,475个回合），对比了不同上下文表示（原始观察vs状态跟踪层）、推理策略（自问、自评等工具）以及任务分解方式（单体ReAct vs层级委托）的性能与token成本。

Result: (1) 程序化状态抽象的单位token回报最高，比原始观察平均回报提升高达76%；(2) 在层级结构中分发推理工具会导致所有模型家族性能下降（“推理级联”现象），回报降低达3.4倍且token消耗增加1.8-2.7倍；(3) 无推理工具的层级分解对大多数模型效果最好，上下文工程比推理更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 针对结构化对抗性POMDP场景，应优先投资于程序化基础设施和清晰的任务分解，而非追求深度的单智能体推理，因为将推理策略与层级分解结合时会产生负面干扰。

Abstract: Deploying compound LLM agents in adversarial, partially observable sequential environments requires navigating several design dimensions: (1) what the agent sees, (2) how it reasons, and (3) how tasks are decomposed across components. Yet practitioners lack guidance on which design choices improve performance versus merely increase inference costs. We present a controlled study of compound LLM agent design in CybORG CAGE-2, a cyber defense environment modeled as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). Reward is non-positive, so all configurations operate in a failure-mitigation mode. Our evaluation spans five model families, six models, and twelve configurations (3,475 episodes) with token-level cost accounting. We vary context representation (raw observations vs. a deterministic state-tracking layer with compressed history), deliberation (self-questioning, self-critique, and self-improvement tools, with optional chain-of-thought prompting), and hierarchical decomposition (monolithic ReAct vs. delegation to specialized sub-agents). We find that: (1) Programmatic state abstraction delivers the largest returns per token spent (RPTS), improving mean return by up to 76% over raw observations. (2) Distributing deliberation tools across a hierarchy degrades performance relative to hierarchy alone for all five model families, reaching up to 3.4$\times$ worse mean return while using 1.8-2.7$\times$ more tokens. We call this destructive pattern a deliberation cascade. (3) Hierarchical decomposition without deliberation achieves the best absolute performance for most models, and context engineering is generally more cost-effective than deliberation. These findings suggest a design principle for structured adversarial POMDPs: invest in programmatic infrastructure and clean task decomposition rather than deeper per-agent reasoning, as these strategies can interfere when combined.

</details>


### [173] [CAX-Agent: A Lightweight Agent Harness for Reliable APDL Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15218)
*Chenying Lin,Yichen Hai,Yi He,Ran Wang,Haiyan Qiang,Liang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAX-Agent，一个用于MAPDL有限元仿真的轻量级智能体框架。实验表明，其核心组件——模型驱动的恢复策略（model_only）在标准结构基准测试中表现最佳，任务完成率达92.67%，显著优于无恢复和仅规则恢复的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）在MAPDL有限元仿真应用中面临实际可靠性挑战。由于缺乏结构化执行控制、工具封装和故障恢复机制，模型输出可能不一致，任务失败较为常见。

Method: 提出CAX-Agent智能体线束架构，将执行分为三层：LLM服务、智能体线束和求解器后端。核心组件为恢复阶梯机制，包含确定性规则修补、模型驱动重生成、上下文丰富和人工干预等升级策略。在50个标准结构基准上评估了三种恢复策略（无恢复、仅规则、仅模型），共450次运行，由两位独立评估者在盲测条件下评分。

Result: 模型驱动恢复策略（model_only）表现最优，任务完成率为0.9267，任务得分3.59/4，总分9.16/10，零干预率0.84。该策略显著优于仅规则恢复（完成率0.7733）和无恢复策略（完成率0.6933），效应量较大（Cliff's delta = 0.81-0.87）。评分者间一致性高（二次加权Cohen's kappa = 0.84）。

Conclusion: CAX-Agent通过结构化的执行控制和恢复机制有效提升了LLM在MAPDL仿真中的可靠性。模型驱动的恢复策略在基准测试中展现出优越性能，为后续更广泛场景的验证提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models deployed for MAPDL finite-element simulation face practical reliability challenges: without structured execution control, tool encapsulation, and fault recovery, outputs may be inconsistent and task failures are common. The Agent Harness paradigm addresses this by inserting domain-specific orchestration middleware that manages tool lifecycles, workflow state, and recovery escalation. This paper presents the architecture of CAX-Agent, a lightweight agent harness purpose-built for MAPDL automation, and empirically evaluates one of its core components -- the recovery policy.CAX-Agent organizes execution into three layers -- LLM service, agent harness, and solver backend -- with a recovery ladder that escalates from deterministic rule patching through model-driven regeneration to context enrichment and human intervention. We evaluate three recovery strategies (no_recovery, rule_only, and model_only) on 50 standard structural benchmarks with three repeated runs per strategy (450 case-runs total). Two independent human raters score task completion under blind conditions; inter-rater agreement is strong (quadratic weighted Cohen's kappa = 0.84, 96 percent of score pairs within one point). Model_only achieves the best completion rate (0.9267), task score (3.59/4), total score (9.16/10), and zero-intervention rate (0.84), outperforming rule_only (0.7733, 3.17/4, 7.03/10, 0.00) and no_recovery (0.6933, 2.74/4, 5.60/10, 0.00) with large effect sizes (Cliff's delta = 0.81-0.87). The benchmark uses deliberately simple geometries to isolate recovery-policy effects; we discuss the scope of these findings and directions for broader validation.

</details>


### [174] [FORGE: Self-Evolving Agent Memory With No Weight Updates via Population Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16233)
*Igor Bogdanov,Chung-Horng Lung,Thomas Kunz,Jie Gao,Adrian Taylor,Marzia Zaman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FORGE协议，通过无梯度更新的自生成记忆机制，使分层ReAct智能体在网络防御任务中相比零样本提升1.7-7.7倍性能，将重大失败率降至约1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体是否能在不进行梯度更新的情况下，通过自生成的记忆改善决策能力，解决现有方法在复杂决策任务中表现不佳的问题。

Method: 提出FORGE协议，包含：(1)内循环：反思智能体将失败轨迹转化为可复用的文本启发式规则(Rules)、少样本示例(Examples)或混合形式；(2)外循环：基于种群的优化，传播最优实例的记忆，并通过毕业准则冻结已收敛的实例。

Result: 在CybORG CAGE-2网络防御任务中：(1)相比零样本基线提升1.7-7.7倍平均回报，相比Reflexion基线提升29-72%；(2)将重大失败率降至约1%；(3)种群广播是关键机制，Examples效果最强，Rules更具成本效益；(4)较弱模型受益更大。

Conclusion: FORGE证明了无梯度记忆演化的有效性，种群广播机制和多种记忆表示形式可显著提升LLM智能体决策能力，且对较弱模型的提升更为明显，有助于缩小能力差距。

Abstract: Can LLM agents improve decision-making through self-generated memory without gradient updates? We propose FORGE (Failure-Optimized Reflective Graduation and Evolution), a staged, population-based protocol that evolves prompt-injected natural-language memory for hierarchical ReAct agents. FORGE wraps a Reflexion-style inner loop, where a dedicated reflection agent (using the same underlying LLM, no distillation from a stronger model) converts failed trajectories into reusable knowledge artifacts: textual heuristics (Rules), few-shot demonstrations (Examples), or both (Mixed), with an outer loop that propagates the best-performing instance's memory to the population between stages and freezes converged instances via a graduation criterion. We evaluate on CybORG CAGE-2, a stochastic network-defense POMDP at a 30-step horizon against the B-line attacker, where all four tested LLM families (Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, Grok-4-Fast, Llama-4-Maverick, Qwen3-235B) exhibit strongly negative, heavy-tailed zero-shot rewards. Compared against both a zero-shot baseline and a Reflexion baseline (isolated single-stream learning), FORGE improves average evaluation return by 1.7-7.7$\times$ over zero-shot and by 29-72% over Reflexion in all 12 model-representation conditions, reducing major-failure rates (below $-100$) to as low as $\sim$1%. We find that (1) population broadcast is critical mechanism, with a no-graduation ablation confirming that broadcast carries the performance gains while graduation primarily saves compute; (2) Examples achieves the strongest returns for three of four models, Rules offers the best cost-reliability profile with $\sim$40% fewer tokens; and (3) weaker baseline models benefit disproportionately, suggesting FORGE may mitigate capability gaps rather than amplify strong models. All evidence is confined to CAGE-2 B-line; cross-family findings are directional evidence.

</details>


### [175] [NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219)
*Salman Avestimehr,Ken Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NOVA框架分析AI自改进发现新知识的机制，揭示了污染陷阱等失败模式，并证明了发现成本随规模呈超线性增长（Θ(D^α)），解释了人类专家介入的关键价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统是否能通过迭代自改进发现真正的新知识，以及这种发现过程需要付出什么代价，从而理解AI知识发现的能力边界与潜在风险。

Method: 引入NOVA框架，将"生成-验证-积累-重训练"循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程，通过理论分析识别充分条件、失败模式，并基于Zipf定律假设推导成本缩放定律。

Result: 识别出四种失败模式（污染、遗忘、探索失败、接受失败）；发现污染陷阱现象——易发现知识耗尽后，误报率会导致无效知识快速积累；证明发现D个真实发现的累计成本为Θ(c_gen D^α)，揭示了发现前沿推进时的边际收益递减规律。

Conclusion: AI知识发现存在固有的成本扩展瓶颈，Good-Turing估计不能用于预测长期未发现的有效知识质量，人类专家在自主探索障碍附近发挥最大价值，这为理解AI辅助科学发现的局限性和人机协作策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Can AI systems discover genuinely new knowledge through iterative self improvement, and if so, at what cost? We introduce the NOVA framework, which models the common ``generate, verify, accumulate, retrain'' loop as an adaptive sampling process over a knowledge space. We identify sufficient conditions under which accumulated genuine knowledge eventually covers a finite domain, and show how their violations produce distinct failure modes: contamination, forgetting, exploration failure, and acceptance failure. We then analyze imperfect verification and identify a contamination trap: as easy-to-find knowledge is exhausted, the model mass assigned to new valid artifacts shrinks, so even small false-positive rates can cause invalid artifacts to enter the knowledge base faster than genuine discoveries. We clarify that Good--Turing estimation is a local batch-diversity diagnostic, not an estimator of the historically undiscovered valid mass that governs long-term discovery. Under a separate tail-equivalence assumption relating the model's effective discovery distribution to a Zipf law with exponent $α>1$, we prove that the cumulative generation cost required to obtain $D$ distinct genuine discoveries satisfies $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$, where $c_{\mathrm{gen}}$ is the per-candidate generation cost. This scaling law quantifies asymptotic diminishing returns as the discovery frontier advances. Finally, we formalize human amplification through guidance, generation, and verification, explaining why expert input is most valuable near autonomous exploration barriers.

</details>


### [176] [NIMO Controller: a self-driving laboratory orchestrator based on the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15227)
*Naruki Yoshikawa,Ryo Tamura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的SDL软件架构，并引入了NIMO Controller编排器，通过统一的MCP接口实现了人类用户与AI代理对自驱动实验室的无缝访问与实验流程设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自驱动实验室（SDL）软件开发技术门槛高，且当前编排软件框架主要面向人类交互设计，缺乏适合AI代理访问的标准化接口，限制了SDL的可访问性和灵活性。

Method: 提出一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的SDL软件架构，将所有SDL功能通过MCP服务器暴露；开发了MCP型SDL编排器NIMO Controller，利用MCP工具发现机制自动生成可视化编程界面，同时允许AI代理通过同一MCP后端进行访问。

Result: 通过一个颜色匹配SDL案例研究进行了演示，结果验证了所提出的基于MCP的SDL架构的可用性，实现了人类用户无需编码即可设计实验工作流，同时为AI代理提供了统一接口。

Conclusion: 基于MCP的SDL架构成功实现了人类用户与AI代理的统一接口访问，降低了SDL软件开发门槛，提高了SDL的可访问性和灵活性，为加速科学发现提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) have attracted increasing attention as a means of accelerating scientific discovery; however, developing SDL software remains technically demanding. To improve accessibility, orchestration software frameworks have been proposed to coordinate SDL components. Nevertheless, existing frameworks are primarily designed for human interaction and do not provide standardized interfaces suitable for AI agents. In this work, we propose an SDL software architecture based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), in which all SDL functionalities are exposed through MCP servers. Following this design principle, we introduce an MCP-based SDL orchestrator, named NIMO Controller. It provides a visual programming interface automatically generated through MCP-based tool discovery, allowing human users to design experimental workflows without writing code. The same MCP backend can also be accessed by AI agents, providing a unified interface for both human users and AI agents. We demonstrate the proposed system through a case study on a color-matching SDL. The results validate the usability of the proposed MCP-based SDL architecture.

</details>


### [177] [Verifiable Agentic Infrastructure: Proof-Derived Authorization for Sovereign AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15228)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式信任框架（DTF），通过引入Justification Proof、共识模型、临时执行身份和仅追加证据链，将授权从静态身份转变为基于可验证证明的动态权限，解决了自主AI代理在云和企业系统中的安全风险问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云和企业系统依赖以身份为中心的授权机制，假设持有有效凭证的调用者是安全的。然而，自主AI代理的出现打破了这一假设：代理可以生成语法有效但语义不安全的操作，使得静态权限成为重大的运营风险。这一问题在主权AI系统中尤为严峻，因为自主代理可能与云基础设施、受监管数据、金融工作流和国家级数字服务进行交互。虽然受治理的突变基础设施通过拦截代理操作来降低风险，但这将信任边界转移到了如何使意图授权决策变得可验证、分布式且可重现的问题上。

Method: 本文提出了分布式信任框架（DTF），这是一个用于受治理突变系统的验证框架。DTF引入了四个核心组件：（1）Justification Proof，用于编码操作的可接受性基础；（2）共识模型，用于独立评估；（3）从批准的证明派生的临时执行身份；（4）仅追加的证据链，用于保留授权生命周期。该框架在声明的基础设施假设下，强制执行紧凑的授权不变式：没有证明对象就不能进行高风险执行，没有共识就不能获得派生权限，没有证据链就不能进行有效突变。研究者在基于OpenKedge的受治理突变基础设施上实例化了该模型，并展示了其如何映射到云原生环境。

Result: DTF框架成功将授权从静态身份转变为基于证明派生的权限，提供了一套完整的机制来确保自主AI代理执行的治理性、可审计性和边界性。在给定的基础设施假设下，该架构能够强制执行授权不变式，确保高风险操作必须有证明对象支持，派生权限必须经过共识机制，且所有有效突变都与证据链关联。通过OpenKedge平台的实例化验证了该方法的可行性，并展示了其在云原生环境中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 通过将授权机制从静态身份转移到基于证明派生的权限，DTF为自主AI执行的治理提供了基础设施基础。该框架使得在主权AI部署中，代理执行变得可控、可审计且有明确边界，为解决自主AI代理带来的安全挑战提供了创新的架构方案，为未来云和企业环境中的AI治理奠定了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Modern cloud and enterprise systems rely on identity-centric authorization, assuming that callers possessing valid credentials are safe to execute commands. The emergence of autonomous AI agents invalidates this assumption: agents can generate syntactically valid but semantically unsafe actions, making standing privileges a significant operational risk. This risk becomes especially acute in sovereign AI systems, where autonomous agents may interact with cloud infrastructure, regulated data, financial workflows, and national-scale digital services. Governed mutation substrates reduce this risk by interposing on agent actions: agents submit intents, infrastructure evaluates context and policy, and execution is mediated. However, this shifts the trust boundary: how can the decision to authorize an intent be made verifiable, distributed, and replayable?
  We introduce a Distributed Trust Framework (DTF), a verification framework for governed mutation systems that computes execution authority from structured, verifiable artifacts. DTF introduces a Justification Proof to encode the admissibility basis of an action, a consensus model for independent evaluation, an ephemeral Execution Identity derived from the approved proof, and an append-only Evidence Chain that preserves the authorization lifecycle. Under stated substrate assumptions, this architecture enforces a compact authorization invariant: no high-stakes execution without a proof object, no derived authority without consensus, and no valid mutation detached from evidence.
  We define the model, instantiate it over an OpenKedge-based governed mutation substrate, and show how it maps onto cloud-native environments. By shifting authorization from standing identity to proof-derived authority, DTF provides an infrastructure foundation for making agentic execution governable, auditable, and bounded in sovereign AI deployments.

</details>


### [178] [Solvita: Enhancing Large Language Models for Competitive Programming via Agentic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15301)
*Han Li,Jinyu Tian,Rili Feng,Yuqiao Du,Chong Zheng,Chenyu Wang,Chenchen Liu,Shihao Li,Xinping Lei,Yifan Yao,Weihao Xie,Letian Zhu,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Solvita框架，通过为多智能体系统配备可训练的图结构知识网络，实现了在不更新底层LLM权重的情况下从历史经验中持续学习，在竞技编程任务上达到新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在处理高难度竞技编程问题时仍存在可靠性不足的挑战。当前的多智能体框架本质上是无状态的，依赖静态检索，丢弃了之前任务中获得的问题解决和调试经验，无法实现经验累积和迁移。

Method: 提出Solvita智能体进化框架，将问题解决重组为包含策略选择、程序合成、认证监督和定向攻击的闭环系统，由Planner、Solver、Oracle和Hacker四个专业化智能体执行。核心创新在于每个智能体配备可训练的图结构知识网络，将结果信号（如通过/失败判决、测试认证质量、对抗性漏洞）转化为强化学习更新来调整网络权重，从而根据过去的成功和失败动态路由未来查询。

Result: 在CodeContests、APPS、AetherCode和实时Codeforces轮次上进行评估，Solvita在代码生成智能体中建立了新的SOTA，超越现有多智能体流程，准确率几乎是单次通过基线的两倍。

Conclusion: Solvita成功证明了通过为多智能体系统引入可训练的知识网络，可以在不更新底层LLM权重的前提下实现持续学习和经验积累，有效弥合了无状态系统的局限性，为竞技编程和代码生成领域提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) still struggle with the rigorous reasoning demands of hard competitive programming. While recent multi-agent frameworks attempt to bridge this reliability gap, they remain fundamentally stateless: they rely on static retrieval and discard the valuable problem-solving and debugging experience gained from previous tasks. To address this, we present Solvita, an agentic evolution framework that enables continuous learning without requiring weight updates to the underlying LLM. Solvita reorganizes problem-solving into a closed-loop system of strategy selection, program synthesis, certified supervision, and targeted hacking, executed by four specialized agents: Planner, Solver, Oracle, and Hacker. Crucially, each agent is paired with a trainable, graph-structured knowledge network. As the system operates, outcome signals, such as pass/fail verdicts, test certification quality, and adversarial vulnerabilities discovered by the Hacker, are recast as reinforcement learning updates to these network weights. This allows the agents to dynamically route future queries based on past successes and failures, effectively accumulating transferable reasoning experience over time. Evaluated across CodeContests, APPS, AetherCode, and live Codeforces rounds, Solvita establishes a new state-of-the-art among code-generation agents, outperforming existing multi-agent pipelines and nearly doubling the accuracy of single-pass baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Context Pruning for Coding Agents via Multi-Rubric Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15315)
*Jingjing Wang,Xiwen Chen,Wenhui Zhu,Huayu Li,Zhengxiao He,Feiyang Cai,Ana S. Carreon-Rascon,Xuanzhao Dong,Feng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LaMR框架，通过分解代码相关性为语义证据和依赖支持两个维度，使用专用CRF和混合专家门控网络实现结构化剪枝，在节省高达31%token的同时提升或保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理在读取仓库文件时消耗大量token预算，但检索到的代码大部分与当前任务无关。现有学习型剪枝器使用单一目标序列标注器，将所有代码相关性压缩为一个分数和一个转移矩阵，这种形式化存在建模瓶颈：单个CRF转移先验必须服务于异构的保留模式（包括连续语义跨度和稀疏结构支持行）。

Method: 提出LaMR（Latent Multi-Rubric）结构化剪枝框架：1）将代码相关性分解为语义证据和依赖支持两个可解释质量维度；2）每个维度由具有维度特定转移动力学的专用CRF建模；3）混合专家门控网络根据查询动态加权每个评分标准的发射概率；4）最终CRF层对融合的发射概率进行聚合的保留或剪枝决策；5）通过基于AST的程序分析从现有训练语料库导出多标准标签，无需额外标注成本。

Result: 在四个基准测试（SWE-Bench Verified、SWE-QA、LCC、LongCodeQA）上，LaMR在16次多轮正面比较中赢得12次。在多轮代理任务上节省高达31%的token，在单轮任务上精确匹配提升高达+3.5，通过去噪上下文经常提升性能，剩余的性能下降也很微小。LaMR经常匹配甚至超越未剪枝的完整上下文基线。

Conclusion: LaMR通过将代码相关性分解为多个可解释维度并使用专用CRF建模，有效解决了现有单一目标剪枝器的建模瓶颈。该方法在显著降低token成本的同时经常提升性能，证明了多维度结构化剪枝方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: LLM-powered coding agents spend the majority of their token budget reading repository files, yet much of the retrieved code is irrelevant to the task at hand. Existing learned pruners compress this context with a single-objective sequence labeler, collapsing all facets of code relevance into one score and one transition matrix. We show that this formulation creates a modeling bottleneck: a single CRF transition prior must serve heterogeneous retention patterns, including contiguous semantic spans and sparse structural support lines. We propose LaMR (Latent Multi-Rubric), a structured pruning framework that decomposes code relevance into two interpretable quality dimensions, semantic evidence and dependency support, each modeled by a dedicated CRF with dimension-specific transition dynamics. A mixture-of-experts gating network dynamically weights the per-rubric emissions conditioned on the query, and a final CRF layer on the fused emissions produces the aggregate keep-or-prune decision. To supervise each dimension without additional annotation cost, we derive multi-rubric labels from the existing training corpus via AST-based program analysis, simultaneously denoising the teacher's binary labels. By effectively filtering distracting noise, LaMR frequently matches or even outperforms unpruned full-context baselines. Experiments on four benchmarks (SWE-Bench Verified, SWE-QA, LCC, LongCodeQA) show that LaMR wins 12 of 16 head-to-head multi-turn comparisons. It saves up to 31% more tokens on multi-turn agent tasks and improves Exact Match by up to +3.5 on single-turn tasks, while performance is frequently enhanced by denoising the context, and any remaining drops are marginal.

</details>


### [180] [Zero-Shot Goal Recognition with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15333)
*Kin Max Piamolini Gusmão,Nathan Gavenski,Nir Oren,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对前沿大语言模型在经典PDDL基准测试中的零样本目标识别能力进行了系统评估，发现模型表现参差不齐，部分模型能有效整合证据并接近基于地标的准确率，而其他模型则始终依赖世界知识先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在规划任务上的表现接近经典规划器，但这种能力更多依赖于世界知识而非真正的符号推理。目标识别作为一种互补的溯因任务，其结构更适合LLM的优势——评估与世界知识的一致性，而非生成新的动作序列。因此，作者希望系统评估LLM在目标识别任务上的真实能力。

Method: 对前沿大语言模型进行系统性零样本评估，以经典PDDL基准测试为平台，让模型执行目标识别任务。通过定量分析模型在不同证据量下的准确率表现，并进行定性分析以研究模型推理过程中证据整合方式的差异。

Result: LLM在目标识别任务上表现不均衡：部分模型能够随证据积累而扩展，在全观测情况下接近基于地标的识别准确率；而其他模型无论积累多少证据都始终固守世界知识先验。定性分析表明，这种差异源于证据整合方式的根本不同，而非领域熟悉度。

Conclusion: 目标识别任务可作为一个有原则的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型的基础规划知识，同时揭示了不同模型在证据整合能力上的根本差异。

Abstract: Large language models have recently reached near-parity with classical planners on well-known planning domains, yet this competence relies on world-knowledge exploitation rather than genuine symbolic reasoning. Goal recognition is a complementary abductive task structurally better suited to LLM strengths: it consists of evaluating consistency with world knowledge rather than generating novel action sequences. This paper provides the first systematic zero-shot evaluation of frontier LLMs as goal recognisers on key classical PDDL benchmarks. Our results show that LLM competence on goal recognition is uneven: some models scale with evidence and approach landmark-based accuracy at full observations, while others remain anchored to world-knowledge priors regardless of how much evidence accumulates. Qualitative analysis of model reasoning traces reveals that this divergence reflects a fundamental difference in evidence integration rather than domain familiarity. These findings position goal recognition as a principled benchmark for the foundational planning knowledge of LLMs.

</details>


### [181] [Ensemble Monitoring for AI Control: Diverse Signals Outweigh More Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15377)
*Eugene Koran,Yejun Yun,Samantha Tetef,Benjamin Arnav,Pablo Bernabeu-Pérez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，将多样化的监控器组合成集成系统可显著提高对AI代理不对齐行为的检测能力。最佳3监控器集成相比同质集成提升2.4倍性能，且微调监控器在顶级集成中表现优异，证明了多样性（而非规模）是性能提升的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在大规模自主代理场景中的广泛部署，确保其行为安全且符合用户意图至关重要。然而，可靠的监控器难以构建，且系统规模使得人工监督不切实际，因此需要有效的自动化监控机制来检测不对齐行为。

Method: 构建12个基于GPT-4.1-Mini的监控器，采用提示工程和微调两种策略。在编程任务上进行评估，测试候选方案通过标准测试但在对抗性输入上失败的场景。将多样化监控器的信号组合成集成系统，并与个体监控器和同质集成进行对比。

Result: 多样化集成显著优于个体监控器和同质集成。最佳3监控器集成相比同质集成实现2.4倍检测性能提升，且在独立数据集上表现强劲。微调监控器出现在所有顶级集成中，并在分布外攻击类型上保持优势。最佳集成结合了强个体性能和监控器间低相关性。

Conclusion: 多样性（而非规模）是监控性能提升的核心驱动力。微调能够激发仅靠提示工程无法实现的检测能力。集成监控作为一种实用的AI控制策略，能够在合理的推理成本下实现安全增益，为AI安全部署提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in autonomous agentic settings at scale, it is important to ensure the actions they take are safe and aligned with user intent. Monitoring agent actions is a key safety mechanism, yet reliable monitors remain difficult to build and the scale of these systems makes human oversight impractical. We show that combining signals from diverse monitors into an ensemble improves detection of misaligned actions. We build 12 GPT-4.1-Mini monitors using both prompting and fine-tuning strategies. We evaluate them on coding tasks where candidate solutions pass standard tests but fail on adversarial inputs. In this setting, diverse ensembles outperform both individual monitors and homogeneous ensembles. Our best 3-monitor ensemble achieves 2.4x greater detection performance gain compared to an ensemble composed of three identical monitors, with the same ensemble performing strongly on an independent dataset. We contend that these results show that diversity - not scale - drives gains. The best ensembles combine strong individual performance with low correlation between monitors.
